### [1] [The Myth of Hamel's Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35857)
*Robert Singer*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文澄清所谓“Hamel悖论”：在组建Lagrange方程前将速度约束代入动能所得的方程一般不正确，但这一“不可容许替换”早在1890年代已被认识（Neumann称之为“非合法动能形式”），Hadamard、Chaplygin、Appell、Hamel等已确立其有效范围。作者从原始文献收集证据，并用几何分析指出该替换函数是质量度量在约束分布上的限制：分布可积时替换合法，否则正确嵌入的Boltzmann–Hamel方程与限制能量方程相差一个余项Res_α=c^s_{αβ}ω^βP_s，并给出其消失条件，纠正了Hamel 1904年关于必要性的论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献将“把速度约束代入动能再组建Lagrange方程会导致错误运动方程”这一现象称为“Hamel悖论”，并据称源自Hamel 1949年教科书。作者指出这并非悖论，而是早已被认识并命名的“不可容许操作”（Neumann的“非合法动能形式”）。论文动机在于：现代讨论未引用1890s—1900s的原始文献，造成历史误读；同时需要给出几何刻画以明确该替换何时合法、何时失效，并检验现代争论中的论断及Hamel 1904年的必要性命题。

Method: 文献考证与几何分析相结合。首先从一手文献（Hadamard 1895、Chaplygin 1897、Appell 1899、Neumann 1899、Hamel 1904/1949）收集关于该替换禁令及其有效范围的记载，并与现代争论的主张逐条对照。随后进行几何分析：把代入约束后的动能识别为质量度量在约束分布上的限制；由此区分约束分布可积与不可积两种情形。

Result: 若约束分布可积，则限制是某积分流形的诱导度量，替换合法；若不可积，则正确嵌入的Boltzmann–Hamel方程与限制能量所得方程仅相差单一余项Res_α=c^s_{αβ}ω^βP_s，其相对于约束分布的补空间由该“捷径”所选择。该余项当且仅当其系数的对称部分为零时消失。两种经典可容许机制——质量度量的解耦与括号分量消失——对此是充分的；对单一约束也是必要的；但对多个约束既不充分也不必要，这纠正了Hamel 1904年论文中的一个必要性论断。

Conclusion: “Hamel悖论”并不存在：该替换的不可容许性自1890年代即被认识并命名，且在专业与几何力学文献中长期在册，现代争论忽略了这一记录。几何上，问题归结为质量度量在约束分布上的限制；其合法性取决于分布是否可积，不可积时偏差由唯一余项精确刻画，其消失条件统一并修正了经典结论。

Abstract: Substituting a velocity constraint into the kinetic energy before forming Lagrange's equations yields, in general, incorrect equations of motion. Recent literature calls this failure "Hamel's paradox," a term introduced in 2010. We show that there is no paradox. The inadmissibility of the substitution was recognized in the 1890s; C. Neumann (1899) named the operation the illegitimate form of the kinetic energy, and Hadamard (1895), Chaplygin (1897), Appell (1899) and Hamel (1904) established its limits of validity. Hamel's textbook of 1949, the nominal source of the paradox, states the prohibition and uses Neumann's term; the result remained in print in the specialist literature and in geometric mechanics. The modern debate cites none of this record; we collect it from the primary sources and measure the debate's claims against it. A geometric analysis identifies the substituted function as the restriction of the mass metric to the constraint distribution. If the distribution is integrable, the restriction is the induced metric of an integral manifold, and the substitution is legitimate. If not, the correctly embedded Boltzmann--Hamel equations differ from those of the restricted energy by a single residue, $\mathrm{Res}_α=c^{s}_{αβ} ω^βP_s$, relative to the complement of the constraint distribution that the shortcut selects. The residue vanishes exactly when the symmetric part of its coefficient vanishes. The two classical admissibility mechanisms, decoupling of the mass metric and vanishing bracket components, are sufficient for this and, for a single constraint, also necessary; for several constraints they are not, which corrects a necessity claim in Hamel's paper of 1904.

</details>


### [2] [Parametric excitation of rotational soft modes of the buckled discrete elastic ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36320)
*Panagiotis Koutsogiannakis,Massimo Ruzzene*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本研究以离散屈曲环为对象，通过解析分析与数值模拟，揭示了在横向简谐激励下通过参数共振激活“旋转软模”的机制，并表明该激活依赖基础的非线性（分段线性）特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究屈曲结构中软模的激活与控制机制，理解横向振动能否以及如何激发具有旋转性质的振动模式，并为软体机器人驱动及纳米力学中的分子体系操控提供理论基础。

Method: 构建由集中质量、线性弹簧与扭转弹簧连接并置于弹性基础上的离散环模型；环经面内均匀压缩形成屈曲态。采用数学（解析）分析结合数值模拟，并引入约束运动学模型；将基础设为分段线性（压缩与拉伸刚度不同）以引入非线性。

Result: 所关注的旋转软模表现为环变形的大幅旋转而角动量为零。该软模可通过横向简谐力以参数共振方式激活，且仅在基础存在非线性时才可能实现；无非线性时横向振动无法激发旋转模。线性基础刚度会使旋转模与横向振动解耦。分段线性基础下，受迫环的动力响应存在三种可能行为：i) 无旋转；ii) 在有限旋转角范围内的极限环；iii) 在相邻平衡角之间发生诱导旋转。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了屈曲结构中软模激活与控制的机理，非线性基础是实现旋转软模参数共振激发的必要条件，结果可望应用于软体机器人驱动及纳米力学中分子体系的先进操控策略。

Abstract: In the present study, we explore the existence and characteristics of a rotating vibrational mode in a discrete buckled ring that manifests under transverse harmonic forcing. The study is conducted through mathematical analysis complemented by numerical simulations. The ring is composed of uniformly distributed concentrated masses connected by linear and torsional springs and supported by an elastic foundation. The buckled state of interest results from homogeneous in-plane compression and is characterized by vibrational modes that have a rotating nature because of the rotational symmetry of the system. The resulting vibrational mode, called a rotational soft mode, is characterized by large amplitude rotations of the ring deformation while keeping its angular momentum zero. It is shown that rotational soft modes can be activated via transverse harmonic forcing through parametric resonance, which is possible only when nonlinearities are introduced to the foundation. In the absence of nonlinear behavior, transverse vibrations of the ring cannot lead to excitation of the rotational mode. Analysis of a constrained kinematics model shows that linear stiffness of the foundation leads to decoupling of the rotational mode from transverse vibrations. When the foundation is considered piecewise linear, with different stiffness values in compression and in extension, it is shown that three different behaviors are possible for the dynamic response of the forced ring: i) no rotation, ii) a limit cycle within a limited finite rotation angle range, iii) induced rotation between subsequent equilibrium angles. The present study contributes to the understanding of the activation and control of soft modes in buckled structures and may lead to actuation applications in soft robotics, as well as advanced manipulation strategies of molecular systems in the context of nanomechanics.

</details>

### [3] [Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients: Hankel Formulas, Laguerre--Freud Dynamics, and Painlevé V Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35895)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 研究广义Charlier多项式在离散权ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2下的首一递推系数β_n、γ_n，用互补矩、Hankel行列式、离散动力系统与连续可积（Laguerre–Freud、Toda、Painlevé V）多角度刻画，并通过数值交叉验证确认大阶渐近β_n∼n、γ_n∼μ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Charlier多项式递推系数的显式与可计算描述尚缺统一框架；作者希望在同一归一化下整合矩/行列式、离散动力递推与连续可积表示，为递推系数提供互补刻画并便于数值验证。

Method: 给出Markov函数与Jacobi连分数的相容归一化，用Hankel行列式（对角系数β_n用加边行列式）表示β_n、γ_n；由原始Laguerre–Freud方程导出局部递推系统并联系Toda形变；回顾与双参数广义Charlier族相一致的Painlevé V表示；以矩/Hankel构造与局部Laguerre–Freud递推作数值交叉验证。

Result: 建立了递推系数的相容归一化与行列式、动力、可积多重建构，导出可计算的局部递推并与Toda形变关联；两种独立方法数值结果吻合，且与主导渐近β_n∼n、γ_n∼μ一致。

Conclusion: 广义Charlier族递推系数可由矩/Hankel行列式与Laguerre–Freud局部递推互补刻画，并与Toda、Painlevé V结构相容，数值交叉验证支持其渐近行为。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  The coefficients $β_n$ and $γ_n$ in the recurrence relation
  \[
  P_{n+1}(x)
  =
  (x-β_n)P_n(x)-γ_nP_{n-1}(x)
  \]
  are described through complementary moment, determinant, discrete dynamical, and continuous integrable formulations. We give a consistent normalization of the Markov function and its Jacobi continued fraction, and express the recurrence coefficients in terms of Hankel determinants, using a bordered determinant for the diagonal coefficient $β_n$.
  Starting from the original Laguerre--Freud equations, we derive the local recursive system used for computation and relate it to the Toda deformation. We also recall the known Painlevé~V representations associated with the two-parameter generalized Charlier family, in a normalization consistent with the present specialization. Moment/Hankelconstructions provide complementary moment-theoretic realizations of the recurrence coefficients, while the local Laguerre--Freud recursion provides an independent dynamical construction. Their comparison is used for numerical cross-validation. The numerical results are consistent with the leading large-degree behavior
  \[
  β_n\sim n,
  \qquad
  γ_n\simμ.
  \]

</details>


### [4] [Fredholm determinant solutions to the modified KP hierarchy and 2D Toda lattice: isospectral and non-isospectral reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37421)
*Dan Dai,Han-Han Sheng*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究mKP方程族与2DTL方程的Fredholm行列式解，通过等谱约化构造出mKdV、mCH和聚焦NLS方程的显式解，并证明非等谱情形下可约化到非等谱负KdV方程，其解由变形Bessel行列式刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fredholm行列式是可积系统中构造精确解的重要工具，但mKP方程族（尤其是正、负成员）与2DTL方程的解结构及其约化关系尚需系统刻画；同时等谱与非等谱约化的解形式差异值得深入探究。

Method: 采用Fredholm行列式方法求解mKP方程族的前两个正成员、第一个负成员及2DTL方程；通过等谱约化技术构造mKdV、mCH和聚焦NLS方程的显式解；在非等谱情形下建立从mKP与2DTL到非等谱负KdV方程的约化。

Result: 显式构造了mKdV、mCH及聚焦NLS方程的Fredholm行列式解；在非等谱情形下，约化得到的非等谱负KdV方程解由变形Bessel行列式表示。

Conclusion: Fredholm行列式框架统一刻画了mKP与2DTL方程族的解，等谱约化联系到多个经典可积方程，非等谱约化则引出变形Bessel行列式这一新的解结构。

Abstract: We study Fredholm determinant solutions to the first two positive and the first negative members of the modified Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (mKP) hierarchy, together with the bilinear two-dimensional Toda lattice (2DTL) equation. By applying isospectral reductions, we explicitly construct Fredholm determinant solutions for the modified Korteweg-de Vries (mKdV), the modified Camassa-Holm, and the focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equations. Furthermore, we demonstrate that in the non-isospectral case, the reduction from the mKP and 2DTL equations to the non-isospectral negative KdV equation yields solutions characterized by the deformed Bessel determinant.

</details>


### [5] [Asymptotic behavior analysis of solutions to the Heisenberg ferromagnet equation with the Schwartz initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37573)
*Xumeng Zhou,Xianguo Geng,Bo Xue*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文利用规范变换、谱分析与逆散射方法，将Schwartz初值下Heisenberg铁磁方程Cauchy问题的解表示为复k平面上的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert问题，并通过非线性最速下降法（Deift-Zhou）获得精确的领头阶渐近公式及一致误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Schwartz初值下Heisenberg铁磁方程Cauchy问题解的长时渐近行为。

Method: 采用规范变换、谱分析与逆散射方法，将解表示为复k平面上的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert问题；给出多种Deift-Zhou围道变形及其动机；对相关矩阵值Riemann-Hilbert问题应用非线性最速下降法。

Result: 建立了解的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert表示，并得到Cauchy问题解的精确领头阶渐近公式和一致误差估计。

Conclusion: Heisenberg铁磁方程Cauchy问题解的长时渐近行为被完整刻画，所得渐近公式与误差估计为一致有效。

Abstract: This work aims to investigate the long-time asymptotic behavior of solutions to the Cauchy problem for the Heisenberg ferromagnet equation with the Schwartz initial data. Utilizing the gauge transformations, spectral analysis and the inverse scattering method, we prove that the solutions to the Heisenberg ferromagnet equation can be expressed in terms of the solutions to a matrix Riemann-Hilbert problem formulated in the complex $k$-plane. Various Deift-Zhou contour deformations and the motivation behind them are given. By applying the nonlinear steepest descent method to the associated matrix-valued Riemann-Hilbert problem, we obtain the exact leading-order asymptotic formulas and uniform error estimates for solutions to the Cauchy problem of the Heisenberg ferromagnet equation.

</details>

### [6] [Estimation of Room Impulse Responses from Handclaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35839)
*Shih-Yu Lai,Kyung Yun Lee,Nils Meyer-Kahlen,Eloi Moliner,Bing-Yu Chen,Vesa Välimäki*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出利用消声拍手数据集训练深度神经网络，在无需已知激励信号的情况下直接从自然拍手录音中估计房间冲激响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拍手可作为无需设备的房间声学激励，但其源波形未知且多变，导致房间冲激响应（RIR）估计困难；本文旨在探究能否直接从拍手中估计RIR。

Method: 构建含17名参与者、2,540次拍手的消声拍手数据集，覆盖自然拍手及不同手型差异；先用正则化反卷积建立已知激励下的性能上界，并验证以混响录音中直达声加窗近似未知激励的不足；随后以消声拍手录音为标签，采用监督回归目标训练深度神经网络，实现无需已知激励的RIR估计。

Result: 在受控合成基准上，所提神经回归器在所有仪器化指标上显著优于基于加窗的基线；在真实声学空间实测拍手录音上，同一房间位置的不同拍手测量所推断的RIR频谱具有一致性。

Conclusion: 结果表明无需知晓激励信号即可直接从自然拍手估计RIR，验证了该思路的可行性。

Abstract: Handclaps provide an equipment-free excitation for room acoustics, but their unknown and variable source waveform makes room impulse response (RIR) estimation challenging. In this work, we investigate whether RIRs can be estimated directly from handclaps. To this end, we introduce an anechoic handclap dataset containing 2,540 claps from 17 participants, designed to capture variability across natural claps and different hand configurations. We first establish the performance attainable when the excitation clap is known using regularized deconvolution, and show that approximating the unknown excitation by windowing the direct sound from the reverberant recording is insufficient. To estimate the RIR without a known excitation, we propose using the anechoic handclap recordings to train a deep neural network with a supervised regression objective. Evaluated on a controlled synthetic benchmark, the proposed neural regressor significantly outperforms windowing-based baselines across all instrumental metrics. Furthermore, we test the proposed method on handclap recordings measured in real acoustic spaces, showing that the inferred RIR spectra are consistent across different handclap measurements taken in the same room location. These results showcase the feasibility of directly estimating RIRs from natural handclaps without requiring knowledge of the excitation signal.

</details>


### [7] [HEAR: Real Voices, Real Bias: A Large-Scale Human-Recorded, Demographically Diverse Benchmark for Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35952)
*Shen Yan,Duc Le,Irina-Elena Veliche*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAR基准，用87k真实人类语音样本评估Audio-LLM的语音偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Audio-LLM偏见评估多依赖合成语音或文本，缺乏生态效度，无法反映真实人类语音引发的偏见。

Method: 构建HEAR基准，包含来自843名人口学多样参与者的87k真实人类音频样本，采用多项选择问答与开放式长文本任务，评估实时语音到语音与语音到文本架构。

Result: 语音条件偏见是模型特异的属性；个性化指令会持续加剧人口学差异。

Conclusion: 语音偏见是可控制的模型特征，为Audio-LLM偏见缓解与评估提供了基础框架。

Abstract: We introduce HEAR (Human-recorded Evaluation of Audio-LLM bias by Real speakers), a large-scale, ecologically valid benchmark comprising 87k real human audio samples from 843 demographically diverse participants. HEAR enables comprehensive evaluation through Multiple Choice Question Answering (MCQA) and open-ended long-form tasks. To our knowledge, this is the first large-scale voice benchmark grounded entirely in authentic human speech.
  We evaluate model behavior across both real-time speech-to-speech and speech-to-text architectures. Our results reveal that voice-conditioned bias is a model-specific property. Furthermore, we demonstrate that personalization instructions consistently exacerbate demographic disparities. Our findings establish that voice bias is a controllable model characteristic, providing a foundational framework for future bias mitigation and evaluation in Audio-LLM development.

</details>


### [8] [Enabling Immersive Audio-Visual Experience from Any Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36295)
*Zitong Lan,Mutian Tong,Jiatao Gu,Mingmin Zhao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: OmniDream：一种免训练框架，将静音单目视频转化为可自由环视、声音与视觉场景空间对齐的沉浸式视听体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频视野狭窄且缺乏空间音频，沉浸感不足；近期视频生成模型虽可将透视视频扩展为全景视频，但未提供与之对应的空间声场，导致扩展后的视觉世界仍不完整。

Method: 提出以对象为中心的音频表示，将每个声源的固有音频内容与其依赖场景的声学效果解耦，从而实现独立音频生成、基于物理的传播效果模拟以及灵活的空间音频渲染；整个框架无需训练。

Result: 实验表明，在音频-视觉对齐、空间正确性与感知沉浸感方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: OmniDream 能够为扩展的全景视频补全空间一致的声场，构建完整的沉浸式视听体验，且无需训练即可应用。

Abstract: Most videos capture only a narrow field of view and provide no spatial audio, limiting the sense of immersion they can provide. Recent video generation models can expand perspective videos into panoramic ones, but do not provide the corresponding spatial soundscape. Without spatially consistent audio, these expanded visual worlds remain incomplete. This paper presents OmniDream, a training-free framework that transforms a silent monocular video into an immersive audiovisual experience, where viewers can freely look around while sounds remain spatially aligned with the visual scene. At the core of OmniDream is an object-centric audio representation that disentangles each sound source's intrinsic audio content from its scene-dependent acoustic effects, enabling independent audio generation, physics-based simulation of propagation effects, and flexible spatial audio rendering. Experiments show improved audio-visual alignment, spatial correctness, and perceptual immersiveness over baselines. Examples are available on https://huggingface.co/spaces/CuriousAlien000/spatial-audio-360-demo

</details>


### [9] [Distill Locally, Schedule Globally: Flow Maps for Few-Step Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36324)
*Yentl Collin,Evan Dufraisse,Amr Mohamed,Amine Khelif Khelif,Dani Bouch,Guokan Shang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 Local Flow-Map Distillation (LFMD)，将 Eulerian Map Distillation 适配到条件 TTS，避免目标构建时的教师轨迹积分，并给出采样调度 TD-DP 与单 NFE 下的 soft-DTW 对齐感知时间自蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 TTS 合成质量高，但推理需大量 NFE 来积分生成轨迹；现有 few-step flow-map 蒸馏从数值积分的教师轨迹构造目标，导致目标精度与训练成本之间的权衡。

Method: 提出 LFMD，将 Eulerian Map Distillation 适配到条件 TTS，目标构建时不做教师轨迹积分。推理阶段由教师动力学与所学映射的一致性推导采样调度 TD-DP，用单一 cost graph 支持多种 NFE 预算且无需外部音频指标评估。针对单 NFE 下调度无灵活性，采用基于 soft-DTW 的对齐感知时间自蒸馏进行细化。

Result: 在 Seed-TTS 与 LibriSpeech-PC 上，LFMD 相对匹配的积分蒸馏基线改善了低 NFE 合成。Seed-TTS 上，细化后的学生在 1-NFE 下达到 1.80% WER，而 32-NFE 教师为 1.76%。

Conclusion: LFMD 无需教师轨迹积分即可完成 flow-map 蒸馏，配合 TD-DP 调度与 soft-DTW 自蒸馏，可在极低 NFE 下逼近甚至接近多 NFE 教师的合成质量。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech (TTS) models achieve high synthesis quality but require many neural function evaluations (NFEs) to integrate their generative trajectories. Recent few-step flow-map distillation approaches for TTS construct targets from numerically integrated teacher trajectories, creating a trade-off between target accuracy and training cost. We propose Local Flow-Map Distillation (LFMD), which adapts Eulerian Map Distillation to conditional TTS and avoids teacher trajectory integration during target construction. For inference, we derive a sampling schedule (TD-DP) from teacher dynamics and consistency of the learned maps, with a single cost graph supporting multiple NFE budgets without external audio-metric evaluation. Because scheduling offers no flexibility at one NFE, we refine this regime with alignment-aware temporal self-distillation using soft-DTW. Across Seed-TTS and LibriSpeech-PC, LFMD improves low-NFE synthesis over a matched integral-distillation baseline. On Seed-TTS, the refined student reaches 1.80% WER with 1-NFE, compared with 1.76% for its 32-NFE teacher.

</details>


### [10] [Trigger Sound Suppression for Misophonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36351)
*Vaishnavi Vidyasagar,Jasmine Zhang,Mahima Uliyar,Seunghyun Oh,Emily Catherine Gates,Mark Zachary Rosenthal,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对恐音症的选择性触发声抑制方法：构建覆盖10类最常见触发声的数据集，采用在6 ms音频块上运行的流式双路网络，探索单热与多热条件模型以抑制1–3种触发声，并通过30名临床显著恐音症受损成人的听测验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恐音症（对特定声音耐受性降低的障碍）影响5–20%的人群，但现有干预手段有限：治疗仅对少数人有效，耳塞或降噪则会一并屏蔽所有声音，缺乏选择性抑制触发声的方案。

Method: 构建覆盖10个最常见触发类别的数据集；采用基于6 ms音频块的流式双路（dual-path）网络；探索单热（one-hot）与多热（multi-hot）条件模型，使其能从声学场景中抑制1–3种触发声。

Result: 通过30名具有临床显著恐音症受损的成人听测验证模型输出：受试者对抑制后音频报告出显著更低的痛苦与唤醒水平，以及更优的效价。

Conclusion: 流式条件声抑制网络能够选择性地去除恐音症触发声，并在临床相关人群中显著改善主观情绪体验，为恐音症提供了一种可行的选择性干预方向。

Abstract: Misophonia, a disorder of decreased tolerance to specific sounds, affects 5-20% of the population, yet sufferers have no good options: therapy helps a minority, and earplugs or noise cancellation silence everything. We present a study for neural trigger sound suppression for misophonia, selectively removing trigger sounds. We curate a dataset covering the 10 most common trigger classes. Using streaming dual-path networks operating on 6 ms audio chunks, we explore both one-hot and multi-hot-conditioned models that suppress 1-3 triggers from the acoustic scene. We validate our model outputs in a listening study with 30 adults with clinically elevated misophonia impairment. Participants reported significantly lower distress and arousal, and improved valence, for suppressed audio.

</details>


### [11] [UDSS-BWE: Uncertainty- and Decision-Science Inspired Swin BandWidth Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36379)
*Tarikul Islam Tamiti,Sajid Fardin Dipto,David Vergano,Luke Baja-Ricketts,Anomadarshi Barua*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出UDSS-BWE，将风险敏感与不确定性感知的决策科学规则引入带宽扩展（BWE），用五类判别器和Swin生成器实现更优感知质量且参数减少3.89倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽扩展的失真本质上是局部化的：最具感知影响的并非平均情形失真，而是罕见的高频瞬态，而标准风险中性目标往往会将其平滑掉。为弥补这一差距，作者从风险敏感与不确定性感知的决策科学规则中寻求解决方案。

Method: 提出UDSS-BWE，引入五种决策科学与不确定性感知判别器：CVaRD（尾部池化以放大高频伪影）、CCD（原-对偶增广拉格朗日防止高频过增强）、MCUD（对谱平坦度/质心/滚降的可学习效用）、EDD（捕捉认知不确定性）、DROD（捕捉熵式KL-DRO聚合）。框架为复数域对抗式BWE，采用基于Swin的生成器（轻量双流移位窗骨干）高效捕获局部与长程结构，并通过可学习格点耦合实现受控跨流交换。

Result: 在英语和法语两个数据集、干净与噪声条件下，UDSS-BWE取得更好的感知质量，同时参数量减少3.89倍（72M vs. 18.5M）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明多学科决策科学与不确定性理论可用于设计高效判别器以产生更细腻的音频，为BWE任务树立了新基线。

Abstract: Bandwidth extension (BWE) is fundamentally localized: the most perceptual distortions are not average-case distortions, but rare high-frequency (HF) transients that standard, risk-neutral objectives tend to smooth away. To close this gap, we seek solutions in the risk-sensitive and uncertainty-aware decision science rules and present UDSS-BWE, which introduces five decision-science and uncertainty-aware discriminators: CVaRD (does tail pooling to amplify HF artifacts), CCD (a primal-dual augmented Lagrangian to prevent HF overboost), MCUD (a learnable utility over spectral flatness/ centroid/ rolloff), EDD (captures epistemic uncertainty), and DROD (captures entropic KL-DRO aggregation). UDSS-BWE is also designed as a complex valued adversarial BWE framework that uses Swin-based generators, a lightweight dual-stream shifted-window backbone, to capture local and long-range structure efficiently, while learnable lattice coupling provides controlled cross-stream exchange. UDSS-BWE is optimized extensively and achieves better perceptual quality with 3.89x fewer parameters (72M vs.18.5M) over two English and French datasets under clean and noisy conditions. To the best of our knowledge, this work shows how multi disciplinary decision-science-inspired and uncertainty theories can be successfully used to design efficient discriminators for producing more nuanced audios, establishing a new baseline in the BWE task.

</details>


### [12] [Emergent Tonal Structure in Learned Chord Embeddings and Its Relation to Tonal Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36460)
*Maral Ebrahimzadeh,Gilberto Bernardes,Sebastian Stober*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文考察skip-gram和弦嵌入是否能反映调性结构并用于分析调性张力。使用含/不含移调增强的和弦序列，从几何、功能与张力三方面评估学习空间。结果显示，增强嵌入具有强移调等变性，恢复出清晰的五度圈结构，并支持调性区域间的可解释位移；基于嵌入的两种测度与既有调性测度及人类张力感知存在对应关系，移调增强总体提升空间稳定性、调性一致性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有调性音高空间与计算模型多基于乐理原理，用于支撑调性分析并对调性张力有重要意义；同时，skip-gram等数据驱动方法可从符号语料学习和弦嵌入，但其能否恢复调性结构及其与调性张力的关系尚缺乏系统探讨。作者因此希望检验数据驱动嵌入在调性结构与张力分析中的可用性。

Method: 以skip-gram从符号语料学习和弦嵌入，对比使用与不使用基于移调的增强；分别从几何、功能、张力相关三个视角评估所学空间。进一步由和弦—调距离和上下文和弦距离关系推导基于嵌入的测度，并与匹配的调性测度及人类张力轮廓进行对照分析。

Result: 增强嵌入表现出强移调等变性，恢复出清晰的五度圈结构，并支持调性相关区域之间可解释的位移；所推导的嵌入测度能通过与匹配调性测度的对应、以及与人类张力轮廓的中等一致性，捕捉调性张力结构的有意义方面；移调增强在各项分析中总体提升学习空间的稳定性、调性一致性与可解释性。

Conclusion: skip-gram和弦嵌入可有效反映调性结构，并为调性张力的结构性分析提供有用基础；基于移调的增强是提升嵌入稳定性、调性一致性与可解释性的有效手段。

Abstract: Several tonal pitch spaces and computational models have been proposed to analyze tonal structure in Western tonal music, many of them grounded in principles from music theory and used to support tonal analysis with important implications for tonal tension. In parallel, data-driven methods such as skip-gram have been used to learn chord embeddings from symbolic corpora, but their ability to recover tonal structure and its relation to tonal tension remains underexplored. In this work, we investigate how skip-gram chord embeddings reflect tonal structure and whether they provide a useful basis for analyzing structural aspects of tonal tension. Using chord sequences with and without transposition-based augmentation, we evaluate the learned spaces from geometric, functional, and tension-related perspectives. We show that augmented embeddings exhibit strong transposition equivariance, recover a clear circle-of-fifths structure, and support interpretable shifts between key-related regions of the learned space. We then derive embedding-based measures from chord-to-key distance and contextual chord-distance relations, and show that they capture meaningful aspects of tonal tension structure through correspondence with matched tonal measures and moderate alignment with human tension profiles. Across analyses, transposition-based augmentation generally improves the stability, tonal coherence, and interpretability of the learned space.

</details>


### [13] [InterBias-SV: Compound Conditions in Speaker Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36500)
*Kamel Kamel,Hridoy Sankar Dutta,Keshav Sood,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: InterBias-SV 基准通过四术语对比（联合误差、两个边际误差、共同参考）检验噪声、信道失真与语音变化对说话人验证系统的叠加效应，含 4,068 条评分记录、17 个实验、12 个编码器标签、6 个语料库，共 1,200 万次试验评估。描述性平均对比约为 +0.0026、+0.0088、+0.0024 EER，但尚不足以证明可加性，且试验匹配、检查点一致性与部分条件元数据仍未验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话人验证系统常同时面临噪声、信道失真与语音变化的组合影响，而逐条件单独评估无法确定这些效应是否可加。论文旨在建立可复算的基准与明确条件定义，检验复合条件下误差是否等于两个边际误差之和减去共同参考项，并厘清相关解释性难题。

Method: 构建 InterBias-SV 基准，采用四术语对比框架（联合误差、两个边际误差、共同参考），组织 17 个实验、12 个编码器标签、6 个语音语料库，共 4,068 条评分记录与 1,200 万次试验评估；其中三个实验族包含计算加性对比所需的同语料项。同时分析两类解释问题：近随机 EER 下加性预测的可解释性，以及人口统计差距比值在干净参考接近零时的不稳定性。

Result: 面向说话人训练编码器的标签，其平均对比分别为 +0.0026、+0.0088 和 +0.0024（EER），不同设置间波动更大。这些均值仅属描述性结果，不足以确立与可加性的等价性：试验匹配、检查点身份及部分条件元数据仍未验证。近随机 EER 会使加性预测难以解释，但随机表现并非 EER 的硬性上限，且与预测的相关性无法识别饱和机制。人口统计差距比值在干净参考接近零时不稳定，绝对差距是更直接的汇总方式。

Conclusion: InterBias-SV 提供条件定义、分析脚本以及可解释复合条件对比的明确要求，并将可复算的汇总结果与仍需进一步实验验证的论断相区分，为说话人验证中复合条件效应的可加性研究建立可复现基础。

Abstract: Speaker verification systems encounter combinations of noise, channel distortion, and changes in speech. Evaluating each condition separately does not establish whether their effects add. InterBias-SV organises this question around a four-term comparison: joint error, two marginal errors, and a common reference. Its results artefact contains 4,068 scored records across 17 experiments, 12 encoder labels, and six speech corpora, totalling 12 million trial evaluations. Three experiment families contain the same-corpus terms needed to compute additive contrasts. For labels assigned to speaker-trained encoders, their mean contrasts are +0.0026, +0.0088, and +0.0024 in equal error rate (EER), with larger variation across settings. These descriptive averages do not establish equivalence to additivity: trial matching, checkpoint identity, and parts of the condition metadata remain unverified. We also examine two interpretation problems. Near-chance EER can make additive predictions difficult to interpret, but chance performance is not a hard EER ceiling, and correlation with the prediction does not identify a saturation mechanism. Ratios of demographic gaps are unstable when their clean reference is near zero; absolute gaps provide a more direct summary. The benchmark provides condition definitions, analysis scripts, and explicit requirements for interpretable compound-condition comparisons, while separating recomputable summaries from claims that require further experimental validation.

</details>


### [14] [Long-Term Memory-Guided Enhancement for Target Perception in Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36577)
*Zhenhong Zhou,Xuanyue Zhao,Youji Liu,Yuanhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Ma,Lianyu Hu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出免训练的长时记忆引导音频增强方法（LTM-AE），通过类别级干净参考录音的隐状态表示重建并插值输入音频token，在不改动ALLM参数的前提下显著提升噪声与干扰环境下的选择性目标感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型（ALLMs）在真实环境中受背景噪声与竞争声源混叠影响，其复杂推理与内容理解能力往往严重退化。受人类听觉长时记忆机制启发，作者希望借助已存储的听觉经验来引导模型聚焦用户指定的目标声源。

Method: LTM-AE为每个类别从独立干净参考录音中提取隐状态表示作为长时记忆；对输入音频token在所选类别的长时记忆中进行重建，并将重建结果与原始token按插入方式融合，随后送入语言主干解码。该插值控制记忆经验的影响强度，全程冻结ALLM所有参数；针对语音内容恢复任务额外引入可学习的token级门控。

Result: 在20类声类别与3个ALLM的诊断测试中，LTM-AE在三个干扰源存在时增强了对指定目标的响应。受限与自由形式分类平均精度较原始混合输入提升29.53至46.15个百分点；在语音内容恢复任务中，加入token级门控使Qwen2-Audio的词错误率由23.07%降至14.77%。

Conclusion: 该工作初步验证了将人类长时记忆原理用于增强音频大语言模型、改善真实场景听觉感知的可行性，且方法无需训练、模型参数完全固定，具备良好的通用性。

Abstract: Audio large language models (ALLMs) can reason about the content of audio recordings to perform complex tasks. However, these capabilities usually collapse in real-world environments when background noise and competing sources mix the target sound. Inspired by long-term memory in human listening, we propose Long-Term Memory-Guided Audio Enhancement (LTM-AE) to improve selective target perception by refining the audio representations of ALLMs without training. LTM-AE extracts representations in hidden states from separate clean reference recordings as long-term memory for each category, guiding enhancement toward a user-specified listening target. We reconstruct incoming audio tokens in the selected category long-term memory and interpolate the reconstructions with the original tokens before language backbone decoding. This interpolation controls the influence of stored auditory experience while keeping all ALLM parameters fixed. Diagnostic readouts across twenty sound categories and three ALLMs show that LTM-AE strengthens responses to a specified target amid three interfering sources. Averaged over constrained and free-form classification, accuracy gains over raw mixtures range from 29.53 to 46.15 percentage points across multiple open source models. For speech content recovery, LTM-AE with an additional learned token-level gate reduces Qwen2-Audio's word error rate from 23.07% to 14.77%. This work takes an initial step toward using principles of human long-term memory to enhance ALLMs for real-world listening. Our code is available at https://github.com/aynlp/ltm-audio-code

</details>


### [15] [Reconstructing the Vocal Tract with Differentiable Acoustic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36737)
*Eric Ming Chen,Jin Woo Lee,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一个可微分、GPU加速的声道声学模拟器，基于频域流体动力学公式，可从语音反演声道形状，并结合神经几何参数化实现跨语言自编码与MRI生成模型耦合的语音到运动声道重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声道是人体滤波发声以产生语音的区域，几何与声音之间的逆映射高度非凸，传统方法难以从语音准确重建声道形状；同时需要为语言学和医学影像应用提供可与其他深度学习流程集成的工具。

Method: 构建可微分且GPU加速的声道声学模拟器，沿声学管模型传播声音合成语音，并利用梯度求解逆问题。三项关键技术贡献：(1) 采用频域流体动力学公式，GPU并行度比时域有限差分高70倍；(2) 集成可微湍流模型以合成辅音；(3) 借鉴隐式神经表示/神经场，用神经网络参数化几何以加速收敛并逃离离散表示的局部极小值。

Result: (1) 实现跨11种语言的声道形状自监督自编码；(2) 将该模拟器与MRI图像生成模型耦合，在无配对数据情况下仅从语音重建说话者运动的声道。

Conclusion: 该可微分模拟器能成功求解非凸的声道几何-语音逆问题，且易于与深度学习流程集成，为语言学和医学影像等新应用提供了可能。

Abstract: The vocal tract is the region of the human body responsible for filtering one's voice to create speech. In this paper, we present a differentiable and GPU accelerated acoustic simulator for the vocal tract. The differentiable simulator synthesizes speech by propagating sound along an acoustic tube model of the vocal tract, and via its gradients, can solve the inverse problem: reconstructing the shape of the vocal tract solely from the sound it produces. Although the inverse mapping between geometry and sound is notoriously non-convex, we discover that gradient descent succeeds with three technical contributions: (1) we design a frequency domain formulation of the vocal tract's fluid dynamics that is 70x more GPU parallelizable than finite differences in time, (2) we integrate a differentiable model for turbulence to synthesize consonants, and (3) similar to prior work in implicit neural representations (INRs) and neural fields, we find that parameterizing the geometry with a neural network accelerates convergence and escapes local minima that trap discrete representations. Because the simulator is differentiable, it is readily integrated with other deep learning pipelines to enable novel linguistics and medical imaging applications. (1) We demonstrate self-supervised autoencoding of vocal tract shapes across 11 languages, and (2) we couple our simulator with a generative model of MRI (magnetic resonance imaging) images to reconstruct one's moving vocal tract from only their speech without paired data.

</details>


### [16] [When Capabilities Fail to Compose: Diagnosing the Compositionality Gap in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36921)
*Chien-Feng Liu,Chih-Kai Yang,Bo-Han Feng,Yu-Hsuan Li Liang,Hung-yi Lee,Cheng-Fu Chou*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究对大型音频语言模型（LALMs）的能力组合进行受控诊断，发现模型虽具备单项音频能力，但在需要整合多种能力时出现系统性性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALMs在单项音频任务上表现优异，但其能力能否被可靠地组合（composition）仍未得到充分探索，这一差距促使作者开展针对能力组合的系统诊断研究。

Method: 作者设计受控诊断实验，要求模型整合三类能力：音频属性识别、线索条件下的片段选择、以及下游的ASR或问答。构造包含两个话语、带有不同声学线索的输入，评估在环境声、性别、情绪三类线索下的组合能力，下游任务包括ASR、数学问答和事实问答，共覆盖四款开源LALM。此外，通过输出格式、位置偏好和思维链（CoT）分析进一步探究失败原因。

Result: 在40个模型-任务-线索组合中，组合式问答准确率有39个出现下降，平均下降26.7个百分点；ASR同样在39个组合中劣化，WER平均上升28.5个百分点。退化幅度因模型、线索类型和线索显著性而异。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了模型"具备单项音频能力"与"可靠组合这些能力"之间存在系统性差距。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) perform strongly on individual audio tasks, but whether these capabilities can be reliably composed remains underexplored. We conduct a controlled diagnostic study of capability composition in LALMs, requiring models to integrate audio-attribute recognition, cue-conditioned segment selection, and downstream ASR or question answering. We construct two-utterance inputs with distinct acoustic cues to evaluate composition across environmental sound, gender, and emotion cues, with ASR, Math QA, and Factual QA as downstream tasks. Across four open-source LALMs, compositional QA accuracy decreases in 39 of 40 model-task-cue settings, by an average of 26.7 percentage points. ASR exhibits a similarly consistent degradation, with WER increasing in 39 of 40 settings by an average of 28.5 percentage points, while the magnitude of degradation varies across models, cue types, and cue salience. We further probe these failures through output format, positional preference, and chain-of-thought (CoT) analyses. Our study reveals a systematic gap between possessing individual audio capabilities and reliably composing them.

</details>


### [17] [Interpreting and Evaluating Dynamic-Rate Speech Codec Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36951)
*Han Wang,Jiaqi Li,Yingda Shen,Yuxiang Wang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 动态帧率神经语音编解码器用变长 token 替代固定帧网格，使边界放置成为表征的一部分。本文结合边界解释与受控重构分析，发现边界含义取决于底层语音表征：浅层偏向语音/声学过渡，深层偏向音节与子词结构；高层语言对齐与更低池化失真及更好的语义 token 重构相关，音节级边界划分优于均匀与相似度划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态帧率语音编解码器将变长 token 的边界纳入表征，但边界的语义含义及其对神经语音重构是否有用尚不明确。本文旨在回答：这些边界编码了什么，以及可解释的边界是否同时有利于重构。

Method: 将边界解释与受控重构分析相结合，把预测边界与语言学和声学参考对齐比较；在 ASR 导向的 SenseVoice 与 Whisper 编码器上分析不同深度的边界含义；对比六种动态帧率算法与固定帧率基线；并采用帧率匹配的边界测试（音节派生划分 vs 音素派生划分，对照 Uniform 与 Similarity）。

Result: 边界含义依赖于底层语音表征：浅层强调音素、清浊与声学过渡，深层转向音节与子词结构。在六种动态帧率算法与均匀基线的比较中，更高的高层语言对齐对应更低的池化失真及更好的语义 token 重构。帧率匹配边界测试显示：音节派生划分优于 Uniform 和 Similarity，而音素派生划分未优于 Uniform。

Conclusion: 对于语义丰富的语音表征，有用的编解码器边界最好被理解为围绕音节尺度的分配决策。

Abstract: Dynamic-frame-rate neural speech codecs replace a uniform frame grid with variable-duration tokens, making boundary placement part of the representation itself. Yet it is unclear what these boundaries encode and whether interpretable boundaries are also useful for neural speech reconstruction. This work combines boundary interpretation and controlled reconstruction analysis by comparing predicted boundaries with linguistic and acoustic references. We find that boundary meaning depends on the underlying speech representation. For the ASR-oriented SenseVoice and Whisper encoders, shallow layers emphasize phonetic, voicing, and acoustic transitions, whereas deeper layers shift toward syllable and subword structure. In a comparison of six dynamic-frame-rate algorithms and a uniform (fixed-frame-rate) baseline, higher-level linguistic alignment is associated with lower pooling distortion and better reconstruction from semantic tokens. Frame-rate-matched boundary tests make the distinction concrete: a syllable-derived partition improves over both Uniform and Similarity, while a phoneme-derived partition does not improve over Uniform. We infer that for semantically rich speech representations, useful codec boundaries are best understood as allocation decisions organized around the syllable scale.

</details>


### [18] [RVQ Position Aware Speculative Decoding for On Device Text to Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37007)
*Berkin Durmus,Eduardo Pacheco,Zach Nagengast,Atila Orhon*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出面向Qwen3-TTS中MultiCodeDecoder的RVQ位置感知投机解码方法，在仅增加5e-4%参数、10-20%每轮投机/验证开销的情况下，实现2.47个接受token/模型调用，将实时合成的顺序模型调用从200次/秒降至88次/秒，在iPhone与Apple Silicon Mac上获得2-2.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流推理下的自回归Transformer解码受内存带宽限制，而端侧TTS属于典型单流场景。Qwen3-TTS实时流式合成需每秒200次以上顺序模型调用，瓶颈在于内层循环MultiCodeDecoder——它为每80ms音频帧生成15个RVQ码。减少顺序调用次数是提升端侧实时性的关键。

Method: 针对MultiCodeDecoder提出RVQ位置感知的投机解码（speculative decoding）方案。利用RVQ码在位置上的结构特性进行投机与验证，仅增加5e-4%参数，每轮投机/验证开销为10-20%。在部署所用的top-k采样下该方案保持分布无损。

Result: 每次模型调用接受2.47个token；实时合成的顺序模型调用由200次/秒降至88次/秒。WER与原始系统持平，与分布无损保证一致。在Qwen3-TTS 0.6B、近期iPhone及Apple Silicon Mac设备上，RVQ token生成获得2-2.2倍加速。

Conclusion: RVQ位置感知投机解码可在几乎不增加参数与可控开销的前提下显著降低端侧TTS的顺序调用次数，实现分布无损且WER持平的实时加速，适用于近期iPhone与Apple Silicon Mac部署。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding (AR) with Transformer models is memory bandwidth bound at single stream inference, the typical deployment regime for on device text to speech (TTS). Real time streaming with Qwen3-TTS requires more than 200 sequential model calls per second, dominated by the inner loop MultiCodeDecoder that emits the 15 residual vector quantization (RVQ) codes per 80 ms audio frame. We propose RVQ position aware speculative decoding for the MultiCodeDecoder, attaining 2.47 accepted tokens per model call at $5\times10^{-4}$ percent added parameters and 10 to 20 percent per round speculation/verification overhead, reducing real time synthesis from 200 to 88 sequential model calls per second. The scheme is distributionally lossless under the deployed top-k sampling, and WER parity with the original system is consistent with this guarantee. We deliver 2 to 2.2x speedup for RVQ token generation with Qwen3-TTS 0.6B on recent iPhone and Apple Silicon Mac devices.

</details>


### [19] [ReDimNet2+: Multi-Corpus Data Scaling for Robust Speaker Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37014)
*Kirill Borodin,Vasilii Kudryavtsev,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReDimNet2+，通过在七个公开语料库上大规模训练紧凑骨干网络，并结合编解码/波形增强、大间隔微调（LMFT）与图检索重排序，显著提升说话人验证的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动说话人验证需在设备、房间和压缩流程变化下保持可靠性。作者分析 VoxBlink2 子集时发现预测频谱着色发生偏移，这一现象驱动了后续的增强与训练策略设计。

Method: 以紧凑的 ReDimNet2 为骨干，在七个公开语料库（63,934 说话人、约 8,675 小时）上进行多语料训练；引入编解码与波形增强、大间隔微调（LMFT）以及基于图的检索重排序。

Result: 在统一随机 4 秒评估窗口下，ReDimNet2+ LMFT 将 VoxCeleb1 汇总 EER 从 2.42% 降至 0.82%，26 条件鲁棒性压力测试 EER 从 7.21% 降至 1.99%；VoxCeleb1-O 达 0.35% EER，优于最佳 WeSpeaker 检查点的 0.787%；VoxBlink2 检索子集上 Pr@k 由 0.7413 提升至 0.7687。

Conclusion: 多语料训练结合增强、LMFT 与检索重排序可有效提升说话人验证的准确性与跨条件鲁棒性，且紧凑模型在统一评估协议下取得领先性能。

Abstract: Automatic speaker verification must remain reliable across devices, rooms, and compression pipelines. We present ReDimNet2+, which scales training of the compact ReDimNet2 backbone across seven public corpora (63,934 speakers, about 8,675 hours). Analysis of a VoxBlink2 subset reveals a shift in predicted spectral coloration, motivating codec and waveform augmentation alongside this multi-corpus training, large-margin fine-tuning (LMFT), and graph-based retrieval reranking. With random 4-second evaluation windows for all models, ReDimNet2+ LMFT reduces pooled VoxCeleb1 EER from 2.42% to 0.82% and a 26-condition robustness stress-test EER from 7.21% to 1.99%. Under this shared local protocol, it reaches 0.35% EER on VoxCeleb1-O versus 0.787% for the best evaluated WeSpeaker checkpoint. On a VoxBlink2 retrieval subset, reranking improves the final model's Pr@k from 0.7413 to 0.7687.

</details>


### [20] [EmoRES-TTS: Residual-Enhanced Vector Steering for Emotional Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38157)
*Kuan-Po Huang,Haohe Liu,Puyuan Peng,Haibin Wu,Zhaoheng Ni,Hung-yi Lee,Jinwon Lee,Neha Chachra*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出EmoRES，一种无需训练的情感TTS向量引导方法：将情感向量分解为共享成分与残差成分，分别控制二者以提升情感可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感条件TTS模型难以稳定表达所请求情感，而通过额外训练提升可控性在计算和情感标注语音数据上代价高昂，因此需要研究免训练的向量引导方法。现有方法CoCoEmo将每个情感向量视为不可分割的方向、仅用单一全局强度控制，限制了对目标情感的遵循程度。

Method: 首先发现情感向量可分解为两部分：使语音偏离中性表达的共享成分，以及将生成导向所请求情感的残差成分。基于此提出EmoRES，在不重训练主干模型的前提下分别控制这两个成分完成向量引导。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，EmoRES在IndexTTS-2和CosyVoice2主干上均优于CoCoEmo的四项客观情感指标：秩相关分别提升26.13和12.97个百分点（相对提升118.8%和33.1%），情感命中率提升12.95和6.92个百分点（相对提升20.1%和9.8%）。人评显示听者正确识别主要目标情感的比率相对提升最高35.0%，保真度提升最高17.3%，自然度成对比较中偏好EmoRES的比例最高达63.8%。

Conclusion: 成分消融表明，有效的情感控制有赖于保留共享成分并强化情感引导向量的残差成分，EmoRES为此提供了免训练且更可控的情感TTS方案。

Abstract: Emotion-conditioned text-to-speech (TTS) models may fail to express the requested emotion reliably, and improving controllability by additional training is costly in both computation and emotion-labeled speech training data. We therefore study vector steering, a training-free approach that modifies the internal representations of a frozen model. CoCoEmo, a conventional vector steering method for emotion TTS, treats each emotion vector as an indivisible direction controlled by a single global strength, limiting adherence to the requested emotion. In this work, we first discover that an emotion vector can be decomposed into a shared component that moves speech away from neutral expression and a residual component that directs generation toward the requested emotion. Building on this finding, we propose Emotion Residual-Enhanced Steering for TTS (EmoRES), a novel method that controls the two components without retraining the backbone. On IEMOCAP, EmoRES outperforms CoCoEmo across all four objective emotion metrics on the IndexTTS-2 and CosyVoice2 backbones. Rank correlation improves by 26.13 and 12.97 percentage points, corresponding to relative gains of 118.8% and 33.1%, while emotion hit rate improves by 12.95 and 6.92 points, corresponding to relative gains of 20.1% and 9.8%. Human evaluation further shows a relative improvement up to 35.0% in the rate at which listeners correctly identified the dominant requested emotion and up to a 17.3% improvement in fidelity, while listeners prefer EmoRES for naturalness in up to 63.8% of pairwise comparisons. Component ablations further demonstrate that effective control benefits from preserving the shared component while strengthening the residual of the emotion steering vectors.

</details>


### [21] [RAWD-TTS: Ratio-Free Reward Alignment for Discrete-Diffusion Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37028)
*Maxim Maslov,Kirill Borodin,Vasilii Kudryavtsev,Nikita Vasiliev,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 RAWD-TTS，一种面向离散扩散 TTS 的无比值优势加权去噪后训练方法，用识别与说话人奖励的组相对优势加权掩码 token 重建，在俄语零样本语音克隆上同时降低词错误率并提升说话人相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 TTS 需兼顾内容准确与说话人身份保持，但监督式声学 token 预测并未直接优化这些波形级属性；基于奖励的后训练可缓解这一错配，然而离散扩散中 token 选择与揭示位置共同决定采样轨迹，导致奖励对齐困难。方法还需避免依赖逆轨迹似然或目标音频。

Method: RAWD-TTS（Ratio-free Advantage-Weighted Denoising）：用识别奖励与说话人奖励对解码样本打分，计算组相对优势，并以其加权这些样本的掩码 token 重建损失；不计算逆轨迹似然，也不需要目标音频。通过联合对齐实现内容与音色的协同优化，并进行受控实验分析识别—身份权衡及破坏数量、组构成与加权方式的影响。

Result: 在 500 条俄语 CV3-Eval 语音克隆提示上，联合对齐将词错误率从 3.18% 降至奖励选择检查点的 2.42%（相对降低 24.0%）和最终检查点的 2.58%（相对降低 19.0%）；WavLM 说话人余弦相似度从 0.733 提升至 0.748 和 0.755。

Conclusion: 基于组相对优势的奖励加权去噪可在离散扩散 TTS 中有效实现波形级属性的后训练对齐，在不依赖逆轨迹似然和目标音频的条件下同步改善内容准确性与说话人相似度，并揭示识别与身份之间的可调控权衡。

Abstract: Zero-shot text-to-speech synthesizes new utterances in a speaker's voice from a short reference recording. Voice cloning requires accurate content and preserved speaker identity, but supervised acoustic-token prediction does not directly optimize these waveform-level properties. Reward-based post-training addresses this mismatch, but in discrete diffusion, token choices and reveal positions jointly define the sampling trajectory, complicating alignment. We introduce RAWD-TTS (Ratio-free Advantage-Weighted Denoising), which scores decoded samples with recognition and speaker rewards and uses group-relative advantages to weight masked-token reconstruction of those samples, without reverse-trajectory likelihoods or target audio. On 500 Russian CV3-Eval voice-cloning prompts, joint alignment reduces word error rate from 3.18% to 2.42% at the reward-selected checkpoint (24.0% relative) and to 2.58% at the final checkpoint (19.0%), while WavLM speaker cosine rises from 0.733 to 0.748 and 0.755. Controlled experiments characterize recognition-identity trade-offs and the effects of corruption count, group composition, and weighting.

</details>


### [22] [Prediction-Layer Branch Calibration for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37100)
*Yulin Sun,Kele Xu,Yong Dou*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出BC-MLF框架，通过分支校准任务头(BCHead)显式建模预测层分支分配，并辅以融合令牌对比学习(FTCL)正则化，在不改动融合骨干的前提下提升多模态情感分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析中基于语言模型的融合方法通常将预测层的分支分配处理为隐式的，缺乏显式建模，导致各模态预测分支的组合方式未被有效优化。

Method: 1) Branch-Calibrated Task Head (BCHead)：以轻量级样本自适应约束混合方式，融合融合分支、文本分支与音视频分支的预测；2) Fusion Token Contrastive Learning (FTCL)：按连续情感亲和度组织平均池化的融合令牌表示，实现情感感知的融合令牌正则化；两者均不修改融合骨干网络。

Result: 在CMU-MOSEI和CH-SIMS上，分类与回归指标均取得所比较方法中的最优结果；相较复现的DeepMLF基线有一致提升；受控消融显示样本自适应预测层分支分配持续优于静态分支聚合。

Conclusion: 显式建模预测层分支分配并结合融合令牌对比正则化，可在不修改融合骨干的情况下稳定提升多模态情感分析性能，样本自适应分支分配优于静态聚合策略。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis integrates textual, acoustic and visual cues, yet current language-model-based fusion methods typically leave prediction-layer branch allocation implicit. We introduce Branch-Calibrated Multimodal Language Fusion (BC-MLF), which explicitly models prediction-layer branch allocation through a Branch-Calibrated Task Head (BCHead), complemented by Fusion Token Contrastive Learning (FTCL) for sentiment-aware fusion-token regularization. FTCL organizes mean-pooled fusion-token representations according to continuous sentiment affinity, while BCHead combines fusion, text and audiovisual predictions through a lightweight sample-adaptive constrained mixture. Without modifying the fusion backbone, BC-MLF consistently improves the reproduced DeepMLF baseline and achieves the strongest results among the compared methods on CMU-MOSEI and CH-SIMS across classification and regression metrics. The controlled ablations show that sample-adaptive prediction-layer branch allocation consistently outperforms static branch aggregation. Code is available at https://github.com/sunyulin0421/BC-MLF.

</details>


### [23] [Multichannel Audio Quality Assessment: Extending Pretrained Perceptual Models to Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37116)
*Gouthaman KV,Shiv Gehlot,Vishnu Raj,Lars Villemoes,Arijit Biswas*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究如何复用预训练感知模型知识于多声道空间音频质量评估，以5.1声道为例提出四种集成层级方案，其中特征级FGAtt方法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间音频感知质量评估对优化至关重要，其质量同时依赖信号保真度与声道间空间关系。但主观评测成本高，而现有感知模型多针对有限声道配置训练，无法直接用于更高声道数的音频，因此需解决预训练感知知识向多声道迁移复用的难题。

Method: 以5.1声道音频为对象，研究信号级、预测级、潜层级和特征级四种多声道集成层级，并提出两种可学习方法：潜层级的空间组表示聚合，以及特征级的频带组注意力（FGAtt），在感知处理之前于特征层面自适应融合空间组。

Result: 在五个5.1声道测试集上，FGAtt取得最强的整体性能，验证了特征级自适应对复用预训练感知知识的有效性。

Conclusion: 特征级适配是复用预训练感知知识用于多声道空间音频质量评估的有效途径，FGAtt为跨声道配置的知识迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate perceptual quality assessment is essential for evaluating and optimizing spatial audio, where perceived quality depends on both signal fidelity and inter-channel spatial relationships. However, subjective evaluation is costly, while existing perceptual models are often trained for limited channel configurations and cannot be directly applied to higher-channel-count audio. This raises the question: how can pretrained perceptual knowledge be effectively reused for multichannel spatial audio? Using 5.1-channel audio, we study four levels of multichannel integration: signal, prediction, latent, and feature and propose two learned approaches: latent-level aggregation of spatial-group representations and the feature-level Feature-Band Group Attention (FGAtt), which adaptively fuses spatial groups at the feature level before perceptual processing. Across five 5.1-channel test sets, FGAtt achieves the strongest over- all performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of feature-level adaptation for reusing pretrained perceptual knowledge

</details>


### [24] [Bad: Taming the Bioacoustic Data Deluge with a Bat Acticity Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37518)
*Stefano Ciapponi,Santiago Martinez Balvanera,Andrea Cesaretti,Elisabetta Farella,Kate E. Jones*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出硬件感知的蝙蝠活动检测器BAD，面向EFM32PG26微控制器实现8位整数量化与全层硬件卸载，可在低发生率场景下高精度区分蝙蝠叫声与干扰噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动声学监测蝙蝠会产生超大规模超声数据（单节点>27 GB/夜），给边缘存储与电池续航带来压力；传统触发器难以应对声学混淆物，而深度模型又超出微控制器资源限制。

Method: 设计硬件感知的Bat Activity Detector（BAD），针对Silicon Labs EFM32PG26（MVP）以8位整数精度部署，支持192–384 kHz可变采样率，实现全部14层的100%硬件卸载（17.2 KB Flash，73.1 KB RAM）。

Result: 在192 kHz、100 ms片段下，端到端预处理76帧耗时74.00 ms，推理耗时30.00 ms，每片段共104.00 ms。在空间域外录音且低发生率（r_pos=0.05）条件下，AUC-ROC为0.9748，抑制99.4%非目标噪声帧，保留65.3%蝙蝠叫声，较经典Goertzel基线精度提升>33倍。

Conclusion: BAD在严格资源约束的微控制器上实现了对蝙蝠叫声与生物/环境混淆物的有效区分，在低发生率与域外场景中兼具高AUC、强噪声抑制与显著精度增益，适用于边缘端被动声学监测。

Abstract: Passive Acoustic Monitoring of bats generates massive ultrasonic datasets (>27 GB/night per node), straining edge storage and battery life. Legacy triggers fail against acoustic confusers, while deep models exceed microcontroller limits. We present a hardware-aware Bat Activity Detector (BAD) specifically designed to discriminate bat calls from hard biological and environmental confusers across variable sampling rates (192-384 kHz). Tailored for the Silicon Labs EFM32PG26 (MVP) in 8-bit integer precision, our model achieves 100 percent hardware offload across all 14 layers (17.2 KB Flash, 73.1 KB RAM). End-to-end preprocessing (74.00 ms for 76 frames) and inference (30.00 ms) of 100 ms clips at 192 kHz require 104.00 ms per clip. On spatially out-of-domain recordings under a realistic low-prevalence regime (r_pos = 0.05), BAD achieves an AUC-ROC of 0.9748 and suppresses 99.4% of non-target noise frames while retaining 65.3% of bat calls - delivering a >33x precision gain over classical Goertzel baselines.

</details>


### [25] [Rate-Agnostic Bioacoustics: Heterogeneous Multi-Taxa Classification with Continuous Filterbanks and Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37540)
*Stefano Ciapponi,Francesco Ardan Dal Rı,Nicola Conci,Elisabetta Farella*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出采样率无关（SFI）前端与具备渐进时域尺度融合的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）骨干，直接在原生采样率处理录音，避免重采样与高频信息损失，并在多类群语料上优于固定采样率基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物声学分类依赖固定采样率的频谱表示，异构采样率的录音必须先重采样才能分析，这会引入高频信息损失并增加预处理成本。作者希望直接在原生采样率上处理录音，从而规避重采样及其信息损失。

Method: 提出采样率无关（SFI）前端，直接以每条录音的原生采样率进行处理；搭配带渐进时域尺度融合的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）骨干，在不同采样率下生成固定尺寸表示；并在训练阶段引入温和的采样率（sr）增强以提升对未见采样率变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在包含84个类别、60种采样率的多类群语料上评估，SFI-FNO配置优于固定采样率基线和语料最大采样率基线，达到0.906准确率、0.921平衡准确率和0.899的Macro-F1。

Conclusion: SFI前端与FNO骨干的组合可在原生采样率下统一处理异构录音，兼顾固定尺寸表示与鲁棒性，并在多采样率生物声学分类任务上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Conventional bioacoustic classification models rely on fixed-rate spectral representations, requiring recordings acquired at heterogeneous sampling rates to be resampled before analysis. We propose a Sampling-Frequency-Independent (SFI) frontend that processes each recording directly at its native sampling rate, coupled with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone featuring progressive temporal-scale fusion. This framework avoids fixed-rate resampling and high-frequency information loss while producing fixed-size representations across sampling rates. Mild training-time sampling-rate (\textit{sr}) augmentation further improves robustness to unseen rate variations. Evaluated on a multi-taxa corpus comprising 84 classes and 60 sampling rates, the proposed SFI-FNO configuration outperforms fixed-rate and corpus-maximum-rate baselines, achieving .906 accuracy, .921 balanced accuracy, and a Macro-F1 score of .899.

</details>


### [26] [Learning as Deepfakes Evolve: RF-Prompt for Continual Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37586)
*Yuankun Xie,Xiaoxuan Guo,Xiaopeng Wang,Siqing Qin,Shaole Li,Kong Aik Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文针对持续音频深度伪造检测中"真实语音域"与"伪造机制"两个变化因素难以解耦的问题，构建了五种受控任务组织方式，提出 RAMI 协议与 RF-Prompt 非对称持续提示学习方法，在受控样本预算下取得最低的平均与合并 EER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续音频深度伪造检测的 dataset-incremental 评测同时改变了真实语音域和深度伪造机制，导致二者对性能退化的贡献无法区分；同时，新伪造机制不断出现、而真实语音往往可反复获取，需要一种更贴近实际的、受控的评测协议与相应的学习方法。

Method: 1) 在相同的训练/开发/评测样本池上构造五种任务组织方式，在受控样本预算下分离真实语音域与伪造机制两种变化因素；2) 提出 RAMI（Real-Anchored Mechanism-Incremental）协议：真实语音作为重复出现的混合域参考，新伪造机制增量到达；3) 提出 RF-Prompt 非对称持续提示学习：用共享 real prompt 保存可复用的真实语音知识，用以正交残差继承的 fake experts 扩展机制特定知识；4) 通过输入自适应的软融合将累积专家压缩为固定数量的注入 token，推理时无需任务身份。

Result: 在 RAMI 协议上，RF-Prompt 达到 10.110% 平均 EER 与 10.370% 合并 EER，优于所有对比的持续学习基线；在五种受控协议下，RAMI 均取得最低的 common-average EER 与 pooled EER；组件消融、有限数据实验与跨骨干网络评测进一步验证了设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦真实语音域漂移与伪造机制增量两个因素，RAMI 协议提供了更受控、更贴近实际的持续音频深度伪造检测评测基准；RF-Prompt 以非对称提示学习与自适性专家融合，在无需任务身份的条件下实现更优的持续检测性能，具有良好的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Continual audio deepfake detection requires learning newly emerging deepfake methods while retaining discrimination of previously encountered speech. Existing dataset-incremental evaluation changes both real-speech domains and deepfake mechanisms, making their effects difficult to distinguish. We construct five task organizations over identical training, development, and evaluation pools to study these factors under a controlled sample budget. Our proposed Real-Anchored Mechanism-Incremental (RAMI) protocol reflects the practical setting in which available real speech provides a recurring mixed-domain reference while new deepfake mechanisms arrive incrementally. We further propose RF-Prompt, an asymmetric continual prompt-learning method that preserves reusable real-speech knowledge through a shared real prompt and expands mechanism-specific knowledge through inherited fake experts with orthogonal residuals. Input-adaptive soft fusion combines the accumulated experts into a fixed number of injected tokens without requiring task identity at inference. On RAMI, RF-Prompt achieves 10.110% average EER and 10.370% pooled EER, outperforming all evaluated continual-learning baselines. Across the five controlled protocols, RAMI yields the lowest common-average and pooled EER. Component ablations, limited-data experiments, and cross-backbone evaluations further validate the proposed design.

</details>


### [27] [AS$^2$D: Accelerating On-Demand Audio Understanding on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37617)
*Yunzhe Li,Kyoungjun Park,Hongzi Zhu,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 AS²D（Audio Speculative Speculative Decoding），通过解耦草稿生成与目标模型演化前缀的依赖，实现音频语言模型中草稿与验证的并发执行，显著提升端侧推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投机解码将草稿生成耦合到目标模型不断变化的已验证前缀上，导致草稿与验证串行化。作者探究这种依赖在源条件生成（如音频任务）中是否必要，发现输入音频和用户请求本身可提供有效候选，无需跟随目标模型演化的文本前缀。

Method: 提出 AS²D：由音频条件化的草稿模型沿自身生成历史独立推进，目标模型独立验证并修正已就绪的候选；无可用的候选项时目标模型单独推进。目标反馈仅决定哪些候选被采纳，而不再决定草稿模型何时推进，从而使草稿与验证并行进行，同时保留目标侧的验证与纠错。在 Android 平台 MNN 框架中实现。

Result: 在四部手机、七个数据集、三类任务、共 12.2 小时音频上评估两个目标模型：跨四部手机，AS²D 将汇总 ASR 吞吐相较仅目标解码提升 42–76%，仅 5.7% 的评估窗口慢于仅目标解码，而投机基线为 58.1–63.0%；ASR 达到逐窗口后见之明 oracle 汇总吞吐的 97.33–98.20%；使用 7B 目标模型的原生按需执行吞吐提升最高达 78%。

Conclusion: 源条件音频生成可以放宽传统投机草稿对目标模型演化输出前缀的依赖，释放显著的并行性以提升推理效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a smaller drafter to propose tokens for batched verification by a larger target. However, conventional speculative decoding couples drafting to the target's evolving verified prefix, serializing drafting and verification. We ask whether this dependency is necessary for source-conditioned generation. Our key observation is that, for audio language models, the input audio and user request can provide useful speculative candidates without following the target's evolving text prefix. We propose AS$^2$D (Audio Speculative Speculative Decoding), which enables target-decoupled drafting: an audio-conditioned drafter follows its own generation history while the target independently verifies and corrects ready candidates. Without usable candidates, the target advances alone. Thus, target feedback determines which candidates are committed but no longer determines when the drafter can make progress, enabling drafting and verification to proceed concurrently while retaining target-side verification and correction. We implement AS$^2$D in MNN for Android and evaluate two target models across four phones, seven datasets, and three tasks covering 12.2 hours of audio. Across four phones, AS$^2$D improves pooled ASR throughput by 42-76% over target-only decoding, while only 5.7% of evaluation windows are slower than target-only, compared with 58.1-63.0% for speculative baselines. For ASR, AS$^2$D reaches 97.33-98.20% of a hindsight per-window oracle's pooled throughput over the evaluated drafter/budget catalog. Native on-demand execution with a 7B target achieves up to 78% higher throughput than target-only. These results show that source-conditioned audio generation can relax the conventional dependence of speculative drafting on the target's evolving output prefix, exposing substantial parallelism for efficient inference.

</details>


### [28] [Do Music Generative Models Understand Musical Qualities? Automatic Music Evaluation with Model-Intrinsic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37710)
*Xiaosha Li,Chun Liu,Ziyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究音乐生成模型的内在信号是否与人类主观评价一致，以MusicGen为对象，提取预测损失、预测熵和稀疏自编码器（SAE）概念三类特征，训练轻量模型预测主观评分，在五个基准上验证其与人类评分强相关，其中SAE隐变量贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐生成模型能产出高质量音乐，但其内在能力是否"理解"音乐质量、是否与人类评价对齐尚不清楚。此前用生成模型似然（如文本领域常用方法）评估音乐效果不佳，导致研究者转向独立的监督式音乐评估模型，因此需要探究模型内在信号能否直接反映人类感知。

Method: 以MusicGen为研究对象，提取三类内在特征：(1) 预测损失，(2) 预测熵，(3) 通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）从模型隐表示中抽取的概念。基于这些特征训练一个轻量级预测模型来估计主观评分，并分别及组合评估各特征。作者假设这些信号对应听音过程：损失与熵的时域与频域结构反映听者的期望与惊讶，SAE隐空间的梯度方向预测感知质量。

Result: 在涵盖连续评分与成对偏好两种范式的五个主观评价基准上，模型内在信号与人类评分呈强相关，验证了上述假设；其中SAE隐变量承载了最主要的预测信号。

Conclusion: 音乐生成模型的内在信号可作为有效的音乐质量评估代理，与人类主观评价高度对齐，且不依赖独立监督评估模型；SAE提取的概念特征最为关键。

Abstract: Current music generative models can produce high-quality music, but does this ability imply that they ``understand'' the musical qualities of their outputs, and is that understanding aligned with human evaluation? Previous attempts to use the likelihood of a generative model to evaluate music, an approach commonly used in text, have proven unsuccessful, leading researchers to rely on standalone supervised music evaluation models. In this paper, we answer this question affirmatively: we show that a model's intrinsic signals---derived from its hidden representations and predictions---are strongly correlated with human ratings. In particular, we study MusicGen and consider three types of features: (1) prediction loss, (2) prediction entropy, and (3) concepts extracted from the model using a sparse autoencoder (SAE). Using these features, we train a lightweight prediction model to estimate subjective ratings. We evaluate these features both individually and in combination. We hypothesize that these signals parallel the listening process: the temporal and frequency-domain structure of loss and entropy reflects listeners' expectation and surprise, while gradient directions in SAE latent space predict perceived quality. Experiments on five human-evaluation benchmarks spanning continuous ratings and pairwise preferences confirm this hypothesis, with SAE latents carrying most of the predictive signal.

</details>


### [29] [2-Dimensional spectral gating for denoising bioacoustics recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37910)
*Julien Boussard,Mélisande Teng,Sulagna Saha,Mario Gallego-Abenza*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种对 Noisereduce 的改进降噪方法，利用动物声音跨多个频率的结构特性，在鸟类与海洋哺乳动物录音上实现了更好的去噪效果，且不增加预处理耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物声学录音中，发声、噪声及信噪比随个体、物种、环境和录音条件差异极大，导致下游生态分析（物种识别、动物通讯研究、个体/种群变异性研究）难以建立稳健且可泛化的方法。因此需在分析前进行降噪，而主流方法 Noisereduce 基于谱门限，对每个频率通道独立估计噪声阈值，未利用声音的跨频率结构。

Method: 在 Noisereduce 的基础上进行扩展，利用大多数动物声音在多个频率上具有结构性这一特点（即信号在频率维度上的相关性/一致性），改进其逐频率通道的噪声阈值估计与谱门限策略。

Result: 在鸟类和海洋哺乳动物录音（包括水上与水下声学录音）上进行验证，改进后的方法在两类录音中均取得优于 Noisereduce 的去噪效果，且预处理速度未受影响。

Conclusion: 利用动物声音的跨频率结构可以有效提升谱门限类降噪方法的表现，该扩展为生物声学录音的稳健降噪提供了一种高效且不增加计算成本的方案。

Abstract: Isolating vocalizations from noise in bioacoustics recordings is a prerequisite to many ecological analyses, including species identification, animal communication understanding, and individual or population-level variability studies. However, when recordings are acquired in open environments, vocalizations, noise, or signal-to-noise ratio can vary widely across individuals, species, environment, and recording conditions, making it hard to develop robust and generalizable methods for ecological analyses. To account for these challenges, noise reduction techniques are used to remove noise before downstream analyses. Popular methods such as Noisereduce rely on spectral gating, which estimates a noise threshold for each frequency channel. We propose a further improvement to Noisereduce, leveraging the fact that most animal sounds have structure across multiple frequencies. We apply our method on bird and marine mammals recordings and show that our extension of Noisereduce leads to improved denoising in both above and under water acoustic recordings, without impacting speed of preprocessing.

</details>


### [30] [Pruning for Efficiency, Paying in Fairness: Demographic Disparities in Pruned Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38106)
*Ganesh Pavan Kartikeya Bharadwaj Kolluri,Michael Kampouridis,Ravi Shekhar*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察音频编码器剪枝对SLAM-ASR在不同人口统计群体上的影响，发现剪枝并非对各群体均等影响，组间WER差距随剪枝加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音LLM部署成本高，压缩（如剪枝）十分必要，但通常仅用聚合WER来选择压缩模型，这可能掩盖剪枝对不同人口统计群体的差异化影响，导致公平性问题被忽视。

Method: 以SLAM-ASR为对象，在三种不同规模编码器上进行音频编码器剪枝，使用Fair-Speech和Common Voice（英语、丹麦语、荷兰语）数据集，按人口统计群体分组统计WER，并考察LoRA微调后的群体差异变化。

Result: 剪枝并非对所有群体一视同仁：最佳与最差群体间的WER差距随剪枝程度加深而扩大；该现象在三种编码器规模上均存在，但仅最大模型在初始阶段能通过聚合WER掩盖这些差异。LoRA适配改善了所有群体的WER，但对已表现较好的群体收益更大，从而对某些群体进一步拉大差距。在Common Voice的英语、丹麦语和荷兰语中，口音差距持续存在但未明显扩大，说明剪枝的公平性效应因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 剪枝模型的部署决策应纳入分群体WER，并将最差群体的错误率作为显式评估标准。

Abstract: Speech-LLMs are expensive to run, making compression important for real-world deployment. However, compressed models are usually selected using aggregate word error rate (WER), which can hide how pruning affects different demographic groups. In this work, we systematically study the effect of audio encoder pruning on SLAM-ASR for different demographic groups. Using the Fair-Speech and Common Voice datasets, we found that the pruning does not affect all demographic groups equally; the gap between best- and worst-performing groups increases in fold. These disparities appear across all three encoder scales, but only the largest model initially hides them behind aggregate WER. LoRA adaptation improves WER for every group, but benefits groups already performing well more strongly and widens for certain groups. On Common Voice English, Danish, and Dutch, accent gaps persist but do not clearly widen, showing that the fairness effects of pruning vary across datasets and must be measured directly. Our findings suggest that for pruned models, deployment decisions should include per-group WER, with the worst-performing group's error rate as an explicit criterion.

</details>

### [31] [Wave-Particle Decomposition for Kinetic Equations II: Full Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35894)
*Chang Liu,Kun Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将动理学松弛方程的波粒分解推广到完整Boltzmann方程，构造以"视界-松弛比"为参数的波粒多尺度封闭系统，并提出耦合隐式波粒迭代求解，实现从Boltzmann到Navier-Stokes的连续谱覆盖，在保持离散Boltzmann解为不动点的同时大幅加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代格式（含Shakhov版本）在近连续区收敛极慢甚至不收敛，Boltzmann求解代价高昂；需要一种既严格保持Boltzmann精度、又能跨越稀薄至连续区高效收敛的统一多尺度求解框架。

Method: 通过恒等变换将碰撞项写成向局部Maxwell分布的松弛加守恒余项；沿特征线用局部动理学视界上的平衡态积分定义"波"，其精确补量"粒子"承载余项；对波、粒子方程取矩得到扩展Navier-Stokes方程，与总守恒律及粒子方程组成波粒多尺度方程；采用耦合隐式波粒迭代：固定粒子更新守恒变量，再用预测波与宏观预测给出的端点牵引更新粒子。

Result: 在离散相容性假设下，迭代以离散Boltzmann解为不动点，近连续收敛因子被随视界-松弛比指数衰减的粒子输运份额所界定。正激波、Couette流、方腔流及高超声速圆柱与Apollo算例表明：稀薄与过渡区结果与传统迭代格式一致，近连续区与Navier-Stokes解一致；该迭代比传统格式少约3至4个以上数量级迭代次数，Boltzmann解耗时仅为同网格Navier-Stokes求解器的1至4倍。

Conclusion: 波粒多尺度分解为Boltzmann方程提供了严格且从连续到稀薄的统一描述，配套的耦合隐式迭代显著提升近连续区收敛效率并保持Boltzmann精度，使全动理学求解的计算成本接近宏观Navier-Stokes量级。

Abstract: The wave-particle decomposition of kinetic relaxation equations is extended to the full Boltzmann equation. An identity transformation writes the collision term as a relaxation toward the local Maxwellian plus a conservative remainder. Along characteristics, the equilibrium integral over a local kinetic horizon defines the wave, and its exact complement, the particle, carries the remainder. The moments of the wave and particle equations are extended Navier-Stokes equations, and the total conservation law and the particle equation form the wave-particle multiscale equations, a closed system, exact for every horizon, that spans a continuous spectrum from the Boltzmann equation to Navier-Stokes hydrodynamics, parametrized by the horizon-to-relaxation ratio. A coupled implicit wave-particle iteration solves this system, updating the conservative variables with the particle fixed and the particle with the predicted wave and an endpoint traction from the macroscopic prediction. Under discrete compatibility assumptions the iteration preserves the discrete Boltzmann solution as its fixed point, and its near-continuum convergence factor is bounded by a particle transport share decaying exponentially with this ratio. Tests on a normal shock, Couette and cavity flows, and hypersonic cylinder and Apollo flows agree with the conventional iterative scheme in rarefied and transition regimes and with Navier-Stokes solutions near the continuum limit. There the wave-particle iteration needs about three to more than four orders of magnitude fewer iterations than this scheme or, where it did not converge, its Shakhov version, and the Boltzmann solution costs one to four times the wall time of a Navier-Stokes solver on the same mesh.

</details>


### [32] [A Tensorial Extension of Fractional Dissipation at Inertial Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35905)
*José I. H. López*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个尺度相关的张量型分数阶耗散算符，将其从拉普拉斯阶数连续过渡到Kolmogorov阶数，并扩展AFNS自由能模型（引入浓度序参量c），形成正则、惯性、浓缩/反常三区制结构，通过Weyl量子化用指数张量因子修饰各向同性分数符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观Navier-Stokes对应关系暗示，随局部雷诺数增加，耗散阶数应从拉普拉斯值连续下降至Kolmogorov值。作者希望在不引入无关闭合项或额外各向同性耗散尺度的前提下，将同一宏观描述延续到速度场具有Onsager临界或亚临界正则性的区域。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个最小本构变形方案，将标量临界分数耗散算符推广为尺度相关张量算符，同时保持AFNS自由能在浓度分支关闭时不变，避免额外闭合与各向同性耗散尺度。

Abstract: We formulate a scale dependent tensorial extension of a fractional dissipation operator motivated by a macroscopic Navier Stokes correspondence. The starting point is a fractional model in which the order of dissipation decreases continuously from the Laplacian value toward the Kolmogorov value as a local Reynolds number increases. At the critical value of the local Reynolds number, the model reduces to an isotropic operator. We then ask whether the same macroscopic description can be continued into a regime in which velocity fields have Onsager, critical or subcritical regularity without introducing an unrelated closure or an additional isotropic dissipation scale. In addition, we extend the original AFNS free, energy, construction by one nonnegative concentration order parameter c. The new term is quadratic in c and linearly coupled to the defect beta function, so that minimization leaves the original AFNS free energy exactly unchanged when the concentration branch is inactive. The resulting three regime mosaic is regular, inertial, and concentrated/anomalous. The construction uses the coarse-grained stress and its critical normalization. The normalized anisotropy tensor provides the lowest order objective tensorial information available at the critical scale, while the scale derivative of the Duchon Robert energy defect, supplies a scalar measure of departure from the Onsager threshold. We propose the minimal constitutive deformation of the critical operator: in Weyl quantization, the isotropic fractional symbol is dressed by this exponential tensorial factor, giving a pseudodifferential operator that reduces to the scalar critical operator. The exponential parametrization guarantees positive eigenvalues and unit determinant for symmetric traceless, so the deformation redistributes dissipation among directions without introducing a new isotropic amplitude.

Abstract: Transporting droplets along cylindrical surfaces is an emerging area of research with a wide range of practical applications such as fog collection and filtration. In this paper, we study both the early-stage and late-stage dynamics of droplet coalescence on a sloped cylindrical fibre. Our theory predicts the bridge growth rate and demonstrates that asymmetry in the adjoining contact angles contributes to the migration of the merged droplet, showing good agreement with experimental observations. In the late stage, as droplet merging progresses, inertia effects become significant and lead to rapid mass transfer. To capture this regime, we develop a weighted residual model for liquid films flowing on a sloped pre-wetted cylinder, incorporating key physical effects such as gravity, low-to-moderate inertia, surface tension, and intermolecular forces. This model explains the experimentally observed directional self-propelled transport during droplet coalescence, where the larger droplet migrates toward the smaller one. Numerical simulations based on this model further support these findings, showing good agreement between the predicted coalescence-induced droplet migration and experimental observations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 无需声学数据标定即可由几何与材料参数预测多孔/穿孔前缘的湍流干涉噪声，穿孔板与体多孔插件在干涉噪声主导时均可获得dB级精度；但等间距窄峰所暗示的0.62倍对流声源机制尚未被现有导纳模型捕捉，需进一步建模。穿孔几何的影响集中于单一低频导纳群，且层流边界层内孔径半径在该群中相互抵消。

Abstract: Porous and perforated leading edges reduce turbulence-interaction noise, but predictions of the reduction have relied on material impedances adjusted to the acoustic data. This paper removes that adjustment. The finite-chord scattering problem is solved in compressible subsonic flow for an arbitrary chordwise admittance, with the shed wake, the Kutta condition and permeability-dependent edge singularities, and closed to the far field with the finite span and the array aperture represented. The admittance of a perforated insert is derived from the Rayleigh conductivity of one aperture with three computed corrections: plate thickness, interaction between neighbouring apertures, and Howe's grazing-flow blockage evaluated at the aperture-averaged velocity of the boundary layer. A bulk porous insert is represented by its measured permeability and pore-fluid inertia. For three perforates at four flow conditions the prediction agrees with measurements to 2.5 dB rms over 84 comparisons, against 4.4 dB with the unscaled impedance used previously. Although the Mach number is 0.05, the chord is acoustically non-compact, and an incompressible solution rises where the measurement falls. Two of three bulk porous inserts are predicted to within 1.7 dB rms where interaction noise dominates. Narrow, equally spaced peaks measured on perforated plates are reproduced by none of the admittances tested; their spacing implies a source convecting at 0.62 of the free-stream speed, absent from a frozen-gust model. A parameter study shows that the perforate geometry acts almost entirely through one low-frequency admittance group, in which the hole radius cancels for holes buried in the laminar boundary layer.

Result: 压降随De单调增大，明确了其物理机制，并界定了超稀近似的适用范围。低De渐近分析即便在低Deborah数下也可能无法准确匹配有限元结果，原因在于其无法满足入口弹性应力条件；而基于超稀极限的理论预测与有限元模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 超稀极限框架可有效突破弱黏弹性限制，准确描述O(1) Deborah数下黏弹性径向流的压降行为，为相关工业流动的理论预测提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Pressure-driven radial flows of viscoelastic fluids are common in various industrial applications, such as injection molding and extrusion. It is well known that viscoelastic rheology can significantly impact the hydrodynamic features of non-Newtonian flows. However, these hydrodynamic features remain not fully understood compared to those observed in Newtonian flows. We analyze the pressure-driven radial flow of an Oldroyd-B fluid between parallel plates and present a theoretical framework together with finite-element numerical simulations for determining the flow rate-pressure drop relation. Unlike previous theoretical studies restricted to the weakly viscoelastic limit of low Deborah ($De$) numbers, we apply lubrication theory and consider the ultra-dilute limit, which allows us to study viscoelastic radial flows at order-one Deborah numbers. Using the one-way coupling between the Newtonian velocity profile and elastic stresses, we derive closed-form expressions for the conformation tensor and pressure drop in the ultra-dilute limit. We show that the pressure drop monotonically increases with $De$, identify the physical mechanisms governing this increase, and delineate the range of validity of the ultra-dilute approximation. We further reveal that the low-$De$ asymptotic analysis of the pressure drop may fail to accurately capture finite-element simulation results, even at low Deborah numbers, owing to its inability to satisfy the inlet elastic stresses. In contrast, our theoretical predictions based on the ultra-dilute limit are in excellent agreement with the finite-element simulation results, enabling us to elucidate the pressure drop behavior for order-one Deborah numbers.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the motion of a spherical microswimmer in a planar Poiseuille flow using analytical calculations and numerical simulations. Our results show that the microswimmer's orientation undergoes periodic oscillations governed by pendulum-like dynamics. The oscillation period is proportional to the channel width and to the complete elliptic integral of the first kind evaluated at the maximum orientation angle, and inversely proportional to the square root of the product of the maximum flow speed and the self-propulsion speed. Based on the net displacement along the flow direction within one oscillation period and the signs of the maximum and minimum velocities, we identify five motion states: upstream, oscillatory upstream, zero-drift oscillatory, oscillatory downstream, and downstream motion. The net displacement is jointly determined by the ratio of the self-propulsion speed to the maximum flow speed and the complete elliptic integrals of the first and second kinds. The signs of the maximum and minimum velocities are determined by the speed ratio and the cosine of the orientation angle. Flow nonuniformity promotes upstream migration, whereas inertial lift produces negligible radial displacement during one oscillation period. These findings identify the key parameters governing the motion of spherical microswimmers and provide significant implications for understanding microorganism motility and designing microrobots with prescribed motion states.

Conclusion: 符号蒸馏可同时获得机器学习级的概率刻画能力与解析模型所需的显式函数形式，模糊边界优于传统零宽度边界判据，所提框架适用于Eulerian-Lagrangian喷雾数值模拟中的液滴碰撞子模型。

Abstract: Droplet collision governs droplet population dynamics in many chemical engineering processes, such as spray drying, spray cooling, agricultural spraying, and combustion. Existing analytical models impose deterministic, pairwise boundaries between collision outcomes, whereas machine-learning classifiers lack the explicit functional form required of analytical collision submodels. In this study, we develop a probabilistic symbolic-distillation model using nearly forty thousand experimental events spanning eight regimes and five dimensionless parameters, including over five thousand data for ambient pressure up to 50 atm. A machine-learning teacher learns the joint outcome-probability landscape from these data, and symbolic regression subsequently distils it into eight class-specific expressions that jointly define a coupled analytical model. The resulting analytical field replaces abrupt regime switching with finite-width fuzzy boundaries. It outperforms the evaluated conventional analytical boundary models and reveals that their main limitation is the inability of zero-width boundaries to represent gradual probability transitions. The "biased-dice" sampling scheme provides a statistically consistent and practically convenient model implementation for Eulerian-Lagrangian spray simulation.

Abstract: The usage of chemical dispersant to promote the breakup of oil droplets remains one of the crucial response strategy towards controlling the rapidly rising droplets during an underwater oil spillage disaster like the Deepwater horizon. However, the detrimental effects of dispersant on the marine life are now well recognized. Here, we demonstrate that vortex ring presents an environmental friendly alternative to the debatable usage of chemical dispersant that primarily aids droplet break-up. We propose two mitigating strategies through the application of vortex rings: (i) atomization of large oil droplets into smaller droplets, which enhances natural dispersion and biodegradation (ii) targeted relocation of spilled oil droplets from ecologically sensitive regions to safer remote locations during minor spill events. This proof-of-concept study demonstrates that vortex rings can efficiently atomize underwater oil droplets while simultaneously transporting them over long distances. The efficiency strongly depends on the droplet size distribution and the strength of the vortex ring. Using time scale analysis and force balances arguments, we propose droplet response and atomization criteria by a traversing vortex ring. Beyond atomization and droplet transport which are center to many engineering processes, the work finds high relevance in the area of bubble turbulence, agricultural industry, multiphase reactors, combustion process, atmospheric fluid dynamics and oil extraction from rocks, to name a few.

Abstract: Molecular mixing models for the conditional scalar diffusion (CSD) term in the filtered mass density function (FMDF) transport equation used in Large Eddy Simulation (LES-FMDF) approach are evaluated against direct numerical simulation (DNS) of spatially evolving jets. The formulation includes the compressible form of the governing equations, a generalized multicomponent diffusion model, a cubic real gas equation of state and realistic property models. Simulations are conducted over a wide range of initial pressures ($1 atm < P_0 < 100 atm$) and jet width based Reynolds numbers of 850 and 1300. FMDF of mixture fraction at various filter widths are obtained from the simulation at several spatial locations within the flow. The CSD term in the exact transport equation of FMDF is calculated from the DNS. An \textit{a priori} analysis of Interaction by Exchange of Mean (IEM), Modified Curl (MC) and Mapping Closure (MAPPING) mixing models for CSD is conducted. At high pressures, significant deviations are observed between the CSD measured from the DNS and those predicted by the models. The source of the deviations is determined to be the mixing frequency used in the models. The significance of the mixing frequency and its variation with change in pressure and diffusion models is studied. The primary objective of this work is to assess the applicability of aforementioned mixing models to flows at large pressures and determine the causes for deviation in prediction.

Although the drift is small in this case, the corrected equations conserve the reduced total inventory for every smooth periodic solution. We also distinguish this exact conservation property from higher-order compatibility with the full curved-interface boundary conditions.
