At zero environmental dephasing, repeated projective measurements remove transverse coherence regenerated during relaxation, thereby enhancing the local longitudinal response. We derive the exact finite-interval Markov kernel for successive measurement records and obtain the ordering threshold in the Curie--Weiss limit. For couplings below the equilibrium Curie--Weiss threshold, shortening the readout interval drives a continuous transition to an ordered stationary state even though the programmed Boltzmann distribution remains disordered. The stationary response and equation of state map exactly onto those of a continuously interacting model with dephasing, while the linear relaxation rates remain distinct at matched static response. Exact finite-size stationary calculations reveal the transition through bimodal magnetization distributions and mean-field critical scaling.

</details>


### [101] [Exact Trotterization in the Degenerate Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36861)
*I. Lizuain,A. Rodriguez-Prieto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可精确求解的简并极限下解析刻画了量子Rabi模型深度强耦合区的一阶Trotter化误差，指出该误差是相空间中刚性旋转与缩放的几何重正化，并给出精确可修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字量子模拟深度强耦合区量子Rabi模型需要误差刻画，但以往Trotter化误差只有定性上界，缺乏精确、定量的解析描述。

Method: 针对Rabi模型可精确求解的简并极限，对任意耦合强度推导一阶Trotter算符的闭式表达式，并用相空间几何方法分析其与精确演化轨迹的关系。

Result: Trotter演化生成的相空间轨迹恰好落在相对精确轨迹刚性旋转并缩放的圆上；导出的态保真度闭式与深度强耦合区描述revival的存活概率在结构上一致，二者都归结为位移Fock态可区分性这一几何问题；将Trotter步耦合提升为复振幅可精确修正该几何误差，且不增加线路开销。

Conclusion: 该精确结果给出了一般Rabi模型中Trotter化误差的基准，为数字量子模拟的误差表征与校正提供了可解析的参照。

Abstract: Digital quantum simulation of the quantum Rabi model in the deep strong coupling regime motivates the need for an exact characterization of Trotterization errors, so far known only through qualitative bounds. Here we derive such a characterization in the exactly solvable degenerate limit, valid for arbitrary coupling strength. The first-order Trotter operator admits a closed expression, showing that the generated phase-space trajectory lies exactly on a circle that is rescaled and rigidly rotated with respect to the exact one, rather than merely approximating it. This geometric renormalization yields a closed form for the state fidelity, structurally identical to the physical survival probability governing revivals in the deep strong coupling regime: both are the same geometric question -- the distinguishability of a displaced Fock state -- applied to two different pairs of points in phase space. We further show that this geometric error is exactly correctable by promoting the Trotter-step coupling to a complex amplitude, at no additional circuit cost. These results, obtained in the exactly solvable degenerate limit, provide a benchmark for Trotterization errors in the general Rabi model.

</details>


### [102] [Closing a single-copy gap to the Holevo bound with two-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36874)
*Changhao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文否定了"有限拷贝间隙"在三参数以上普遍存在的猜想：构造出三参数秩二模型，其Holevo界单拷贝不可达但两拷贝可精确达到；同时证明满秩模型任意参数数目的有限拷贝间隙持续性，并在超导量子比特上实验实现两拷贝集体测量，观测到低于单拷贝及分离测量界的估计误差，且在有限噪声范围内保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多参数估计中，Holevo Cramér-Rao界给出渐近可达精度，但其在有限量子态拷贝数下的可达性一般未解决。已有结果表明两参数模型中单拷贝最优与Holevo界之间在任何有限拷贝数下均存在间隙，而更多参数时是否同样普遍存在间隙尚属开放问题。

Method: 构造一个三参数秩二估计模型，分析其Holevo界在一拷贝与两拷贝下的可达性；对任意参数数目的满秩模型证明有限拷贝间隙的持续性；进一步用超导量子比特实验实现该模型的两拷贝集体测量，并通过引入可控退极化研究噪声影响。

Result: 三参数秩二模型的Holevo界单拷贝不可达、两拷贝可精确达到，否定了三参数以上有限拷贝间隙普遍持续的猜想；满秩模型则对任意参数数目保持有限拷贝间隙。实验上观测到两拷贝估计误差低于由态层析确定的单拷贝界与分离测量界，且该精度优势在有限噪声范围内持续存在。

Conclusion: Holevo界的有限拷贝可达性并非仅由参数数目决定，而取决于多参数估计模型的结构以及可用于集体测量的资源；模型秩与参数数目共同决定了有限拷贝间隙是否持续。

Abstract: The Holevo Cramér-Rao bound sets the asymptotically attainable precision of quantum multiparameter estimation, but its attainability with finite quantum state copies remains unresolved in general. While a gap between the single-copy optimum and the Holevo bound persists at any finite copy number for two-parameter estimation models, the higher-parameter case has remained open. Here we disprove universal finite-copy gap persistence beyond two parameters by constructing a three-parameter rank-two model whose Holevo bound is unattainable with one copy but attained exactly with two. We further prove finite-copy persistence for full-rank models with any number of parameters. We then experimentally implement the two-copy collective measurement for the constructed model using superconducting qubits. We observe a two-copy estimation error below the single-copy and separate-measurement bounds determined from state tomography. By introducing controlled depolarization, we show that this precision advantage persists over a finite noise range. Our results connect the attainability of ultimate quantum precision to the structure of the multiparameter estimation model and the resources available for collective measurements.

</details>


### [103] [Digital Twin Modeling of Quantum Dynamical Systems: Dissipative Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36901)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Shital Chauhan,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出耗散量子储备池计算（DQRC）框架，用单量子比特开放系统作为固定储备池、仅训练经典读出层，从数据直接学习强驱动多体量子系统的输入-输出映射，成功构建HHG的紧凑数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强驱动多体量子系统的响应建模困难：动力学非线性、具历史依赖性，且随系统规模增大仿真成本高昂。高次谐波产生（HHG）是典型例子，其辐射对驱动场高度敏感并编码长程时间关联，传统方法难以高效刻画。

Method: 引入耗散量子储备池计算（DQRC）框架，将小型开放量子系统作为储备池并保持固定，仅训练经典读出层，直接从输入-输出数据学习映射，从而构建目标系统的数字孪生。

Result: 最小单量子比特储备池即可复现规模远大于自身的Ising自旋链的HHG响应；在代表性基准上性能匹配，并在若干指标上超越此前报道的时间卷积网络与Kolmogorov-Arnold网络模型，且所用系统更简单、物理上可实现。单一固定储备池还能跨广泛驱动范围泛化。

Conclusion: 结果表明耗散量子储备池可作为紧凑、具物理基础的数字孪生，用于建模非线性、具记忆依赖的量子动力学。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modeling the response of driven many-body quantum systems from input--output data is difficult: the dynamics are nonlinear, history dependent, and expensive to simulate as system size grows. A paradigmatic case is High-Harmonic Generation~(HHG), where a strong field drives a medium to emit radiation that is highly sensitive to the drive and encodes long-range temporal correlations. We introduce a dissipative quantum reservoir computing~(DQRC) framework that builds a digital twin of such a system, learning its input--output map directly from data while the reservoir---itself a small open quantum system---stays fixed and only a classical readout is trained. We show that a minimal single-qubit reservoir reproduces the HHG response of a substantially larger Ising spin chain, and on a representative benchmark matches and on several metrics surpasses previously reported temporal convolutional and Kolmogorov--Arnold-network models, while using a simpler, physically realizable system. A single fixed reservoir further generalizes across a broad range of drives, indicating that it learns a shared physical response structure rather than memorizing trajectories. These results establish dissipative quantum reservoirs as compact, physically grounded digital twins for nonlinear, memory-dependent quantum dynamics.
  Code is available at \href{https://github.com/AI-and-Quantum-Computing/DQuRC}{https://github.com/AI-and-Quantum-Computing/DQuRC}.

</details>


### [104] [Device-independent quantification of steerability in tripartite scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36907)
*Xin-Hong Wang,Miao-Tzu Lin,Shin-Liang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将组装矩矩阵（AMM）框架从两方扩展至三方量子导引，覆盖2-steer-1和1-steer-2两种场景，构造半定规划从观测关联或Bell不等式违背中给出导引鲁棒性的下界，并设备无关地证明测量不相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关（DI）的量子导引量化在两方情形已有AMM框架，但在两方以上情形基本未被探索，因此需要将其推广到三方导引场景。

Method: 将组装矩矩阵（AMM）框架扩展至三方导引，分别针对两方联合导引第三方（2-steer-1）和单方导引其余两方（1-steer-2）两种方案，各自构造半定规划，仅依赖观测关联或Bell不等式违背来计算导引鲁棒性的下界。

Result: 所构造的半定规划能够从观测关联或仅从Bell不等式违背中给出底层组装导引鲁棒性的下界；作为副产物，这些界还能以设备无关方式证明导引方所做测量的不相容性。

Conclusion: 该工作把设备无关的导引量化从两方推广到三方，提供了2-steer-1与1-steer-2两种方案的半定规划工具，并可同时认证测量不相容性。

Abstract: Quantum steering captures the ability of one party, through local measurements on a shared entangled state, to affect the conditional states held by distant parties in a way that admits no local explanation. Its device-independent (DI) quantification -- requiring no characterization of any state or measurement -- has been developed in the bipartite setting, notably through the framework of assemblage moment matrices (AMMs) [Phys. Rev. Lett. 116, 240401 (2016)], but has remained largely unexplored beyond it. Here, we extend the AMM framework to tripartite steering, covering both the scenario in which two parties jointly steer a third one (2-steer-1) and that in which a single party steers the remaining two (1-steer-2). For each scheme, we construct a semidefinite program that, from the observed correlations -- or merely from the violation of a Bell inequality -- yields lower bounds on the steering robustness of the underlying assemblage. As a by-product, our bounds also certify, in a DI manner, the incompatibility of the measurements performed by the steering parties.

</details>


### [105] [Exact quantum algorithm for searching of multiple sets intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36908)
*Sheng-Ao Mao,Lin Zhang,Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Long的相位匹配方法，提出一种可精确搜索多个约束集合交集的量子算法，推导并证明了精确搜索所需的相位匹配条件，给出了实现该搜索的量子电路，并通过数值仿真验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法对非结构化搜索具有二次加速，但成功概率通常低于100%；Long引入相位匹配条件实现了零理论失败率的搜索。然而许多应用中目标集由多个约束集合的交集确定，而现有基于Grover的方法多限于两个集合的交集且不总能成功。因此需要一种能够精确搜索多个集合交集的算法。

Method: 在Long方法的基础上，针对多个约束集合的交集搜索，推导并证明了精确搜索的相位匹配条件，并设计了实现精确交集搜索的量子电路，最后通过数值仿真进行验证。

Result: 得到了用于精确搜索多个集合交集的相位匹配条件，构建了相应的量子电路，数值仿真结果支持理论推导的正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作将精确量子搜索从单一或两个集合的情形推广到多个约束集合的交集，实现了零理论失败率的交集搜索，为多约束条件下的量子搜索提供了理论与电路实现方案。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm achieves a quadratic speedup for unstructured search when a global oracle for the target set is available. However, its success probability is generally below $100\%$. To overcome this issue, Long presented a modified version of Grover's search algorithm by introducing a phase-matching condition, which can search for the target set with zero theoretical failure rate. Nevertheless, in many applications, the target set is determined by the intersection of multiple constrained sets. Most existing Grover-based methods are limited to the intersection of only two sets and are not always successful in searching for the target set. In this work, based on Long's method, we propose an algorithm for exactly searching the intersection of multiple sets. We derive and prove a phase-matching condition for searching the intersection exactly. Furthermore, we present a quantum circuit that implements exact intersection search. Finally, we support our theoretical results with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [106] [Universal Inductive Inference of Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36912)
*Taiga Hiroka,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Yuki Shirakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Solomonoff 通用归纳推理推广到量子系统，提出"通用量子归纳推理"框架，在迹距离意义下以概率 1-δ 输出与目标 ε-接近的预测态，给出轮复杂度 O(sε⁻²δ⁻¹) 的算法及匹配下界，并构造非 i.i.d. 量子态层析算法及计算困难性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomonoff 通用归纳推理为任意可计算随机源提供预测保证，但缺乏面向量子系统的类比理论。量子源可能具有跨时间的任意关联与纠缠，因此需要建立可学习、可预测量子源的通用归纳框架。

Method: 将量子源建模为多体量子态，其描述由未知的 s 比特程序在给定时间界内生成；学习者在每轮获得过去测量结果及对应的测量后量子系统，需输出一个联合态来预测下一次测量结果与测量后系统，同时保持与过去系统的关联。基于此模型设计信息论层析算法，并借助量子密码学假设分析计算困难性。

Result: 提出轮复杂度 O(sε⁻²δ⁻¹) 的信息论推理算法，输出态以概率至少 1-δ 在迹距离下 ε-接近目标；证明在相关参数区间内该界是紧的，即使对经典源亦成立。构造非 i.i.d. 态层析算法，输出下一系统在给定历史测量结果下条件态的经典描述，轮复杂度 O(s·min{2^s,2^n}·ε⁻²δ⁻¹)，n 为每轮接收的量子比特数。进一步在量子密码学假设下刻画了通用量子归纳推理的计算困难性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子版本的通用归纳推理理论，给出具有匹配下界的样本/轮复杂度保证，并扩展至非独立同分布量子态层析，同时揭示其计算困难性，为量子学习与预测的通用理论奠定基础。

Abstract: Solomonoff's universal inductive inference [Inf. Control. 1964] is one of the most general frameworks for learning from sequential data and predicting future observations. It provides prediction guarantees for any computable stochastic source. We ask whether an analogous universal theory of induction can be developed for quantum systems. To this end, we introduce universal quantum inductive inference, a framework for learning and predicting quantum sources that may exhibit arbitrary correlations and entanglement across time. Given the outcomes of past measurements and the corresponding post-measurement quantum systems, the learner produces a joint state that predicts the next measurement outcome and post-measurement system while preserving correlations with the past systems. We model a quantum source as a multipartite quantum state whose description is generated by an unknown $s$-bit program within a given time bound. The learner is required to produce a state that is $ε$-close in trace distance to the target with probability at least $1-δ$. We establish an information-theoretic inference algorithm with round complexity $O(sε^{-2}δ^{-1})$. We further prove a matching lower bound in the relevant parameter regime, even for classical sources. As a second result, we construct a non-i.i.d. state tomography algorithm that outputs a classical description of the conditional state of the next system given past measurement outcomes. Our information-theoretic tomography algorithm achieves round complexity $O(s\min\{2^s,2^n\}ε^{-2}δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of qubits received in each round. Finally, we investigate the computational hardness of universal quantum inductive inference under quantum cryptographic assumptions.

</details>


### [107] [Towards Scalable Quantum Unit Commitment in Power Systems: Encoding and Complexity Reduction Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36999)
*Marc Carrillo-MuÑoz,Sergio MartÍnez-Hermida,Sara Barja-MartÍnez,Antonio E. SaldaÑa GonzÁlez,MÒnica AragÜÉs-PeÑalba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子机组组合（UC）框架，结合Pauli关联编码（PCE）作为量子比特缩减预处理与基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）作为混合后处理精化方法，在Qibo框架内将UC建模为QUBO并映射到伊辛表示，通过多机组规模案例验证二者组合在可扩展性、可行性与最优解恢复概率上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机组组合问题的二进制变量随发电机数量增长导致量子比特需求急剧膨胀，现有量子求解方案在可扩展性、可行解概率与计算成本方面面临挑战，需引入量子比特缩减与后处理精化技术以提升实用性与可靠性。

Method: 将UC问题二进制化建模，转化为QUBO并映射为适合绝热量子演化的伊辛表示，在Qibo框架内实现；采用PCE作为量子比特缩减预处理，采用SQD作为混合后处理精化，并在发电机数量递增的可扩展案例中分析二者对量子比特规模、计算开销、可行性与最优解恢复概率的影响。

Result: PCE可降低编码问题的有效规模；SQD能提高概率在可行解与最优解上的集中度；二者结合在多个可扩展案例中表现最佳，相比未缩减方案可获得更可靠的解且有效计算成本更低。

Conclusion: PCE与SQD的组合为量子UC问题提供了兼顾可扩展性与求解可靠性的有效途径，验证了量子比特缩减预处理与混合后处理精化协同的潜力。

Abstract: This paper develops a Quantum optimisation Unit Commitment (UC) framework that benefits from Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) as a qubit reduction preprocessing technique and Sample-based Quantum Diagonalisation (SQD) as a hybrid post-processing refinement method. The UC problem is formulated in binary form, then transformed into a QUBO problem and mapped onto an Ising representation suitable for adiabatic quantum evolution within the Qibo framework. This study analyses how PCE and SQD techniques affect qubit scalability, computational effort, feasibility, and probability of recovering the optimal solution as the number of generators increases. The results show that PCE reduces the effective size of the encoded problem, SQD improves the concentration of probability on feasible and optimal solutions, and their combined application provides the best overall behaviour in several scalable case studies, achieving more reliable solutions with lower effective computational cost than the unreduced formulation. The repository is available open source in Github (link is not yet available until work is published. However, if requested by the reviewers, we can make it open for a scific time window, if requested).

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Query Lower Bounds for Triangle-Listing and Spanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37091)
*Yu Chen,Ananta Mukherjee,Mingyang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在一般图查询模型下给出三角形列举与显式乘性生成树构造两个关系型图问题的量子查询下界，通过归约到中间多块搜索问题，扩展了量子查询记录框架以处理双向oracle，并给出块级可靠性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形列举的下界此前未有非平凡的量子结果（Jiang与Peng提出的开放问题），且乘性生成树的量子查询下界也缺乏强刻画；作者希望借助更强的记录技术工具，在一般图查询模型中获得这两个基础关系型图问题的量子查询下界。

Method: 扩展Zhandry（CRYPTO 2019）以及Hamoudi与Magniez（ToCT 2023）的量子查询记录框架，使其支持双向oracle的记录架构；对于任意局部接受投影算子族，给出其与有界权重记录子空间最大重叠的精确算子范数刻画（块级可靠性框架）；两个下界均通过归约到中间多块搜索问题得到。

Result: 构造出一族含Θ(n)个三角形的n顶点图，列举任意常数比例的三角形需要Ω(n^{3/2-o(1)})量子查询，这是三角形列举的首个非平凡量子下界；对每个固定k≥7，构造乘性k-生成树需要Ω(n^{1+1/(2μ_k)})量子查询，其中μ_k=k/3+O(1)。当k∈{7,8}时下界为Ω(n^{5/4})，与未证明的Erdős围长猜想实例所蕴含的结果一致；对大k，指数1+3/(2k)超过Lazebnik、Ustimenko与Woldar（1995）可证明的高围长稠密图所蕴含的1+4/(3k)。

Conclusion: 通过扩展量子查询记录框架与块级可靠性框架，本文首次建立了三角形列举的非平凡量子查询下界，并为乘性k-生成树构造给出了强量子下界，部分匹配甚至超越已知经典可证结果所蕴含的界限，为一般图查询模型下关系型图问题的量子复杂度研究提供了新工具与基准。

Abstract: This paper gives quantum query lower bounds for two relational graph problems, triangle listing and explicit multiplicative spanner construction, in the general graph query model, where quantum adjacency, degree and neighborhood queries are all available in arbitrary superposition. Both results are obtained by reductions via intermediate multi-block search problems. We extend the quantum query recording framework by Zhandry (CRYPTO 2019) and Hamoudi and Magniez (ToCT 2023) to handle a recording architecture for bidirectional oracles and give a generic blockwise soundness framework, which for arbitrary families of local accepting projectors, gives an exact operator-norm characterization of their maximum overlap with the subspace of bounded weight records. Using the intermediate search problems, we exhibit a family of $n$-vertex graphs with $Θ(n)$ triangles on which listing any constant fraction of the triangles requires $Ω(n^{3/2-o(1)})$ quantum queries. This is the first nontrivial quantum lower bound for triangle listing, a question raised by Jiang and Peng (ICML 2026). Further, we show that, for every fixed $k\ge 7$, constructing a multiplicative $k$-spanner requires $Ω(n^{1+\frac{1}{2μ_k}})$ quantum queries, where $μ_k=k/3+O(1)$. For $k\in\{7,8\}$ the bound is $Ω(n^{5/4})$, which matches what would be implied by an unproven instance of the Erdős girth conjecture, and for large $k$ the exponent $1+\frac{3}{2k}$ exceeds the $1+\frac{4}{3k}$ implied by the provable high girth dense graphs due to Lazebnik, Ustimenko and Woldar, 1995.

</details>


### [109] [A quantum double-bracket algorithm for imaginary-time evolution with exponentially shorter depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37095)
*Ioannis Kolotouros,Raul Garcia-Patron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出概率性双括号量子虚时演化（PDBQITE），在单辅助比特、每轮一次受控实时演化加一次中途测量的电路下，将DB-QITE电路深度指数级降低，代价是总成功率随迭代数指数衰减，但成功率的解析下界与系统规模无关，数值显示单步成功率接近1，可执行大量迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时演化（ITE）是制备哈密顿量基态的常用技术，但现有实现（含Double-Bracket量子虚时演化DB-QITE）电路深度过大，限制其在近期量子设备上的可行性，因此需要一种能大幅降低电路深度的方案。

Method: 在DB-QITE框架上构建概率化变体PDBQITE：每轮迭代只用一个辅助量子比特，电路由一次受控实时演化与一次中途测量构成；推导与系统规模无关的总成功率解析下界；并用数值模拟评估单步成功率，同时在分子哈密顿量基态制备任务上与PITE对比、在MaxCut问题上将PDBQITE与QAOA构成混合方案进行测试。

Result: PDBQITE相对此前构造实现电路深度的指数级缩减；总成功率随迭代数指数衰减，但其解析下界不依赖系统规模；数值模拟显示单步成功概率保持接近1，足以支撑多次迭代；在分子哈密顿量基态制备上优于近期概率算法PITE；在随机正则图的MaxCut问题上，相同电路深度下混合QAOA-PDBQITE的近似比高于单独QAOA。

Conclusion: PDBQITE以可控的成功率代价换取电路深度的指数级降低，单步高成功率保证其可实际运行多轮迭代，并在基态制备与组合优化任务上分别超越PITE与同等深度的QAOA，是一种适合近期量子硬件的实用方案。

Abstract: Imaginary-time evolution (ITE) is a widely used technique for preparing the ground state of a target Hamiltonian. Building on the recently proposed framework of Double-Bracket quantum ITE (DB-QITE), we introduce a probabilistic variant (PDBQITE) that achieves an exponential reduction in circuit depth. Our algorithm requires only a single ancilla qubit and a quantum circuit that consists of one controlled real-time evolution and one mid-circuit measurement per iteration. The trade-off is an exponential decay of the total success probability with the number of iterations, for which we derive an analytical lower bound that is independent of system size. Our numerical simulations show that the per-step success probability remains close to unity, making it realistic to execute many iterations, thus providing a substantial improvement over prior constructions. We show that PDBQITE outperforms the near-term probabilistic algorithm PITE for ground-state preparation of molecular Hamiltonians, and that a hybrid QAOA-PDBQITE scheme achieves higher approximation ratios than QAOA at the same circuit depth on random regular graphs for the MaxCut problem.

</details>


### [110] [Minimal building blocks for molecular quantum circuits with exact spin symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37101)
*Mengwei Liu,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在固定粒子数N、总自旋S和自旋投影M_S的子空间内生成全部实态空间旋转所需的额外操作，并给出了最小空间轨道数及构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅保持粒子数和自旋的量子线路无法遍历所有具有指定量子数的态，需明确哪些额外操作可补足这一缺失。

Method: 在实轨道、无空间对称限制下，考察保持粒子数、全自旋对称性与实振幅的连续可调操作；结合自旋无关的轨道旋转，分析所需生成元的轨道数下界，并构造单、双电子项实现。

Result: 在最大自旋极限以下，任意两固定空间轨道间的重复单重态对转移即足够；在非平凡最大自旋边界上对转移消失，需附加一个生成元，条件是其作用非轨道旋转生成元的线性组合。所需最小轨道数内部为2、边界为3，且均可由单、双电子项达到；三轨道最优解按第三轨道占据旋转两轨道，可分解为8个对易Pauli旋转。基准显示该操作消除边界能量误差平台，稀疏内部构造在部分固定轨道比较中以更少CNOT达到目标精度。

Conclusion: 自旋与粒子数守恒不足以保证子空间内完备可达，需辅以特定对转移或额外生成元；边界情形的最小构造为三轨道单/双电子操作，可显式编译并有效提升分子基准精度与编译效率。

Abstract: Preserving particle number and spin helps quantum circuits target molecular electronic states, but does not guarantee access to every state with the required quantum numbers. We determine which additional operations, combined with spin-independent orbital rotations connecting all spatial orbitals, generate every real state-space rotation within each complete subspace of fixed particle number $N$, total spin $S$, and spin projection $M_S$. We consider spin-free molecular calculations in real orbitals, without an additional spatial-symmetry restriction, and continuously tunable operations that preserve particle number, full spin symmetry, and real amplitudes. Below the maximal-spin limits set by the electron and hole numbers, repeated singlet-pair transfer between any two fixed spatial orbitals is sufficient. On nontrivial maximal-spin boundaries, pair transfer vanishes, and an additional generator built from one- and two-electron terms is sufficient exactly when its action in the target subspace is not a linear combination of orbital-rotation generators. The minimum number of spatial orbitals needed by one additional generator is two in the interior and three on nontrivial boundaries, even when terms involving more than two electrons are allowed. Both minima are attained using only one- and two-electron terms. The three-orbital optimum rotates two orbitals according to the occupation of a third, and its unitary factors exactly into eight commuting Pauli rotations. Molecular benchmarks show that this operation removes the observed boundary energy-error plateaus, while sparse interior constructions attain the prescribed energy accuracy with fewer compiled CNOT gates in selected fixed-orbital comparisons.

</details>


### [111] [SQUARE: Structured Quantum Representation Adapters as Compact Quadratic Feature Maps for Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37134)
*Emily Jimin Roh,Hyojun Ahn,Hoyeong Lee,Soohyun Park,Sung Whan Yoon,Vaneet Aggarwal,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 SQUARE 量子表示适配器，在固定低维瓶颈上实现结构化二阶交互特征，仿真下在 GLUE 派生交互任务上优于经典对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型常被用作固定特征提取器，用于下游重排序、打分与偏好建模。核心问题是如何让紧凑适配模块在固定低维瓶颈中表示特征间的交互：常见线性/低秩适配器本身仍是线性的，而显式二阶方法虽然引入成对交互，但依赖直接参数化或预设分解，缺乏结构化且参数高效的方案。

Method: 提出 SQUARE（Structured QUAntum REpresentation）适配器：对瓶颈向量做振幅编码，施加含参量子电路，再对所得量子态进行测量。理论上证明每个基概率特征恰为瓶颈坐标的归一化二次型，额外的 Pauli-Z 读出为这些概率的带符号线性组合；因此该测量映射可用少量共享电路参数覆盖 O(d^2) 个坐标对的交互，d 为瓶颈维度。该参数化属于经典归一化二次型特征类之内，而非超越它。所有电路实验均基于仿真，学习到的特征映射可在 PyTorch 中批量精确计算，无需量子硬件。

Result: 在八个 GLUE 派生的受控交互任务、五个共享随机种子上进行同一流程的不相交评测：SQUARE 平均测试准确率为 0.7565，对比仿射归一化二次型预测器 0.7355、参数匹配的 Givens 混合模型 0.7271、MLP 0.6817、冻结电路对照 0.6155。在减少监督条件下，相对最强经典比较器仍有一致增益，且该定性模式在多个冻结语言模型骨干上成立。

Conclusion: SQUARE 提供了一种在固定低维瓶颈中结构化表示成对交互的参数高效适配方式，其表达能力位于经典归一化二次型特征类之内，却在受控交互任务与少监督设定下稳定优于所评测的经典基线，且仅依赖仿真、可高效精确计算。

Motivation: 冻结语言模型作为固定特征提取器时，需要紧凑适配模块在低维瓶颈中表达特征交互；线性/低秩适配器缺少二阶交互，显式二阶方法参数化不够结构化，因此需要参数高效的共享结构化二阶交互表示。

Abstract: Frozen language models (LMs) are increasingly used as fixed feature extractors for downstream reranking, scoring, and preference modeling, raising a practical question: how should a compact module represent interactions among features in a fixed low-dimensional bottleneck? Common linear and low-rank adapters remain linear at the adaptation module itself, whereas explicit second-order alternatives introduce pairwise interactions through direct parameterization or predefined factorizations. We propose SQUARE, a Structured QUAntum REpresentation adapter that amplitude-encodes the bottleneck vector, applies a parameterized quantum circuit, and measures the resulting state. We show that each basis-probability feature is exactly a normalized quadratic form in the bottleneck coordinates, while the additional Pauli-$Z$ readouts are signed linear combinations of these probabilities. The measured map can therefore parameterize interactions over $O(d^2)$ coordinate pairs through a small set of shared circuit parameters, where $d$ is the bottleneck dimension. It provides a structured parameterization within, rather than beyond, the classical normalized-quadratic feature class. In a disjoint same-pipeline evaluation over eight GLUE-derived controlled interaction tasks and five shared seeds, SQUARE achieves an average test accuracy of $0.7565$, compared with $0.7355$ for an affine normalized-quadratic predictor, $0.7271$ for the evaluated parameter-matched Givens mixing model, $0.6817$ for an MLP, and $0.6155$ for a frozen-circuit control. Under reduced supervision, it also shows consistent gains over the strongest evaluated classical comparator, with the same qualitative pattern across multiple frozen LM backbones. All circuit experiments use simulation, while the learned feature map can be evaluated exactly in batched PyTorch without quantum hardware.

</details>


### [112] [Arbitrary high-dimensional entanglement purification with the superposition of paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37136)
*Zi-Han Zheng,Wen-Qiang Liu,Chen-Ming Bai,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用路径叠加实现任意高维纠缠纯化协议(HDEPP)的可扩展方案，通过选取合适的单量子比特门可分别纯化qudit翻转与相位翻转错误；维度越高协议越鲁棒，纯化保真度随维度增加而提升，所需操作资源更少，并可通过多轮迭代趋近单位保真度，同时给出利用超纠缠纯化光子频率自由度的可行光学方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维量子纠缠在量子信息处理任务中相比二维情形具有显著优势，但实际传输中易受噪声影响导致纠缠退化，因此需要高效的高维纠缠纯化手段；而现有高维纠缠纯化方案在资源开销、可扩展性与保真度方面存在不足。

Method: 基于路径叠加构建可扩展的高维纠缠纯化协议框架，通过选择适当的单qudit门分别针对qudit-flip错误与phase-flip错误进行纯化；并进一步设计利用超纠缠来纯化受qudit-flip与phase-flip错误影响的光子频率自由度的可行光学方案。

Result: HDEPP在高维系统中表现出更强的鲁棒性，纯化保真度随维度增加而提高；所需操作资源更少，且原则上可通过多轮迭代实现单位保真度；同时给出了针对光子频率自由度的可行光学实现方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维量子通信与量子网络的发展提供了可行途径。

Abstract: High-dimensional quantum entanglement offers significant advantages over its two-dimensional counterparts in various quantum information processing tasks. We propose a scalable method for implementing an arbitrary high-dimensional entanglement purification protocol (HDEPP) using path superposition. By selecting appropriate single-qudit gates, qudit-flip errors and phase-flip errors can be purified. Counterintuitively, the HDEPP becomes more robust for higher-dimensional systems and exhibits enhanced purification fidelity as the dimension increases. Our HDEPP not only requires fewer operational resources but also in principle can achieve unity fidelity through multiple rounds of iteration. Furthermore, we develop feasible optical schemes to purify the frequency degree of freedom of photons suffering from qudit-flip and phase-flip errors using hyperentanglement. This work paves the way toward high-dimensional quantum communication and quantum networks.

</details>


### [113] [Margenau-Hill distribution as a Necessary and Sufficient Signature of Measurement Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37144)
*Partha Patra,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了测量不相容性与Margenau-Hill(MH)准概率分布负性之间的操作联系：对任意有限维二值可观测量，证明MH分布的正性当且仅当测量可联合测量，并给出反交换情形下的可联合性充分条件及干涉测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容性与准概率分布负性都体现非经典性，但二者关系此前多为定性描述，缺乏明确的操作性联系。本文旨在建立二者的严格操作对应关系。

Method: 首先推导任意有限维d中任意一对二值可观测量的联合可测性条件；进而严格证明unsharp二值测量下MH分布正性与联合可测性的等价；再将MH类准概率分布推广到n个二值unsharp可观测量，在互反交换条件下给出可联合测性的充分条件；最后提出受量子开关架构启发的干涉测量装置直接重构MH准概率。

Result: 证明了对于任意维度d中任意一对unsharp二值测量，MH分布正性当且仅当测量可联合测量；给出n个互反交换二值可观测量可联合测性的充分条件；提出可实验直接重构MH准概率并据此检验不相容性的干涉方案。

Conclusion: MH准概率分布的正性可作为测量可联合性的等价判据，从而将测量不相容性这一非经典特征与准概率负性建立起显式的操作联系，并提供可行的实验检验途径。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility and the negativity of quasi-probability distributions both demonstrate the signature of nonclassicality. However, their relations largely remained qualitative, and no explicit operational connection has been established. We provide an operational link between measurement incompatibility and the Margenau-Hill(MH) quasi-probability distribution associated with two dichotomic observables. { We first derive the joint measurability (measurement compatibility) condition for any pair of dichotomic observables in arbitrary finite dimension $d$.} We then rigorously prove that for any pair of unsharp dichotomic measurements in dimension $d$, the positivity of the MH distribution is equivalent to joint measurability \emph{i.e.}, the MH distribution is positive \emph{if and only if} the measurements are jointly measurable. We further introduce the MH-like quasi-probability distribution for $n$ dichotomic unsharp observables in arbitrary finite dimension and derive a sufficient condition of joint-measurability when the observables are mutually anticommuting. Finally, we propose an interferometric setup, inspired by quantum-switch architectures, that directly reconstructs MH quasi-probability which in turn provides a test of incompatibility.

</details>


### [114] [A quantum thermal machine surpassing the classical thermodynamic limit on precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37149)
*Simon Sundelin,Ludvig Nordqvist,Khalak Mahadeviya,Vyom Kulkarni,Mohammed Ali Aamir,Mark T. Mitchison,Simone Gasparinetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在超导量子热机中实验观测到稳态量子输运违反经典热力学不确定性关系（TUR），测得 TUR 比值 Q = 1.71 ± 0.17，低于经典下界 Q ≥ 2，揭示了量子与经典热力学在精度—耗散权衡上的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机动力学中，抑制电流涨落必然伴随熵产生增加，TUR 定量刻画了这一权衡，并成为非平衡热力学的基石。然而，经典 TUR 能否在可控量子器件中被违反，仍是悬而未决的实验问题，其难点在于需要分辨极小的稳态电流及其涨落。

Method: 构建超导量子热机，一端耦合微波波导作为冷浴，另一端耦合经典噪声源以提供等效无限温浴；在稳态下测量量子输运电流及其涨落，提取 TUR 比值并判定是否突破经典下界。

Result: 实验测得 TUR 比值 Q = 1.71 ± 0.17，显著低于经典 TUR 所要求的 Q ≥ 2，即观测到对经典 TUR 的违反。

Conclusion: 该结果证明稳态量子输运可以突破经典 TUR 所允许的精度极限，确立了经典与量子热力学之间的根本性差异，为在更低能量代价下实现更高精度的量子热器件开辟了道路。

Abstract: Precision in nonequilibrium processes comes at a thermodynamic cost: suppressing fluctuations generally requires increased dissipation. Thermodynamic uncertainty relations (TURs) make this trade-off quantitative by linking current fluctuations to entropy production in classical stochastic dynamics. In the decade since its discovery, the canonical steady-state TUR and its finite-time generalizations have become a cornerstone of non-equilibrium thermodynamics, constraining the performance of molecular machines and allowing heat dissipation to be inferred from observable fluctuations. Whether the canonical TUR can be violated in a controlled quantum device remains an outstanding experimental question, in part because doing so requires resolving extremely small steady-state currents as well as their fluctuations. Here we experimentally show that steady-state quantum transport can surpass the precision permitted by the canonical TUR. We observe this violation in a superconducting quantum thermal machine coupled to a microwave waveguide acting as a cold bath and to a classical noise source providing an effective infinite-temperature bath. We observe a TUR ratio $Q = 1.71 \pm 0.17$, in violation of the classical bound $Q \geq 2$. Our results demonstrate a fundamental distinction between classical and quantum thermodynamics, paving the way for quantum thermal devices that achieve enhanced precision at reduced energy cost.

</details>


### [115] [Non-Canonical Quantum Otto Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37194)
*Wei Wu,Jun-Hong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了非正则热化对有限时间量子Otto循环效率与功率统计的影响，发现通过调控复合系统能谱结构可同时提升平均效率与功率并降低其涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间量子热机的效率与功率因热涨落和量子涨落存在权衡关系，难以同时实现高功率与高效率；而传统研究假设正则热化，仅在弱耦合条件下成立。

Method: 在有限时间量子Otto循环框架下，考虑工作物质与热浴组成的复合系统，研究非正则热化效应对效率和功率统计（平均值与方差）的影响，并通过工程化复合系统能谱结构进行调控。

Result: 效率与功率的平均值和方差高度可调控；在由束缚态形成所诱导的量子临界点附近，平均效率与功率可被同时提升，且两者的方差均减小。

Conclusion: 揭示了量子储库工程在热力学中的实用性，为实现高效且稳定的量子能量器件奠定了基础。

Abstract: The fast-developing quantum technology is propelling thermal machine, which lies at the heart of thermodynamics, into a new golden era. Being stochastic because of the non-negligible thermal and quantum fluctuations, the efficiency and power of a finite-time quantum heat engine exhibit a trade-off, preventing the simultaneous achievement of high power and high efficiency. Conventional studies treat the statistics of a heat engine by assuming canonical thermalization, which is valid only under weak-coupling conditions. We here investigate the noncanonical effect on the statistics of the efficiency and power for a finite-time quantum Otto cycle. It is revealed that their average values and variances are highly controllable by engineering the energy spectrum structure of the composite system consisting of the working substance and the bath. Near the quantum critical point induced by the formation of a bound state in the energy spectrum, the average efficiency and power can be boosted simultaneously, meanwhile, both of their variances are decreased. Deepening our understanding of the usefulness of quantum reservoir engineering in thermodynamics, our result lays the foundation for the realization of efficient and stable quantum energy devices.

</details>


### [116] [Encoding universal quantum computation into quantized Berry phases: Hardness results and classical algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37199)
*Kazuki Sakamoto,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了量子化Berry相估计的计算复杂性：证明在逆多项式谱隙下判定Berry相为0或π是BQP完全的，并将其hardness推广到2D方格晶格上的Heisenberg与XY模型，同时给出固定维度晶格上常数谱隙几何局域Hamiltonian的经典多项式算法，表明谱隙（而非精度）是经典可解性的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已确立在逆多项式精度下Berry相估计存在超多项式量子优势，但该hardness是否对由对称性量子化、可在常数精度下区分的Berry相依然成立尚不清楚，本文旨在刻画量子化Berry相估计的计算复杂性，探索量子物理中实际量子优势的新路径。

Method: 首先引入一种编码，将任意量子计算的输出映射为恰好为0或π的Berry相，据此证明在谱隙为逆多项式小量时判定Berry相为0或π是BQP完全的；其次将该hardness推广到2D方格晶格上物理动机明确的Hamiltonian（含Heisenberg和XY相互作用）；最后针对固定维度晶格上具有常数谱隙的几何局域Hamiltonian构造经典多项式时间算法。

Result: 证明在逆多项式谱隙下判定Berry相是精确0还是π为BQP完全，意味着（假设BPP≠BQP）区分量子化Berry相仍具超多项式量子优势；该hardness可扩展至2D方格晶格上的Heisenberg和XY相互作用模型；并给出固定维度晶格上常数谱隙几何局域Hamiltonian的经典多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 本文确立了计算在对称性与谱隙保持形变下不变的量子化拓扑不变量所具有的超多项式量子优势，指明谱隙而非精度是经典可解性的关键资源，为量子物理中实现实际量子优势提供了新进展。

Abstract: The Berry phase is a fundamental geometric quantity for characterizing the geometry and topology of quantum many-body systems. Previous work established a super-polynomial quantum advantage in Berry phase estimation at inverse-polynomial precision, given an ansatz state approximating the initial ground state. However, whether this hardness persists for Berry phases quantized by symmetry, which can be distinguished at constant precision, remained open. In this work, we characterize the computational complexity of quantized Berry phase estimation. First, we prove that deciding whether the Berry phase is exactly $0$ or $π$ is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete when the spectral gap is inverse-polynomially small. This result implies that distinguishing the quantized Berry phase still has a super-polynomial quantum advantage, assuming $\mathsf{BPP}\neq \mathsf{BQP}$. To prove this result, we introduce an encoding that maps the output of any quantum computation to a Berry phase that is exactly $0$ or $π$. Second, we extend this hardness to physically motivated Hamiltonians on a 2D square lattice, including Heisenberg and XY interactions. Third, we provide a classical polynomial-time algorithm for geometrically local Hamiltonians on fixed-dimensional lattices with constant spectral gaps. Therefore we identify that the spectral gap is a key resource for the classical tractability, rather than precision. These results establish a super-polynomial quantum advantage for computing a quantized topological invariant that is unchanged under symmetry- and gap-preserving deformations, and provide new steps towards practical quantum advantage in quantum physics.

</details>


### [117] [Optimal Entanglement Routing in Quantum Repeater Chains: Beyond Fixed Operation Order and Purification Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37206)
*Aikaterini Mandilara,Antonia Tsili,Dimitris Syvridis,Konstantinos Christodoulopoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究有限链路容量下线性中继链的纠缠路由，联合放松操作顺序与纯化调度两个限制，建立容量感知优化框架并给出可解性与吞吐界的理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠路由方法将决策空间限制在两个维度：操作顺序通常固定为先纯化后交换（PtS），每条链路的纯化调度常限于pumping。作者希望放松这两项限制，研究在保真度阈值约束下最大化端到端期望吞吐量。

Method: 针对两种量子噪声模型，构建统一的容量感知优化框架：PtS下用精确混合整数线性规划（MILP），可实例化为pumping或一般树状纯化调度；对任意操作顺序用精确动态规划（DP）。并在对称Pauli噪声下进行理论分析。

Result: 对称Pauli噪声下，证明pumping收敛到严格小于1的保真度，导致与容量无关的可行性界及每条基于pumping的PtS方法可支持的最大链长，而一般树状调度给出依赖容量的界；进一步证明交换后纯化不会提升吞吐量，故自由操作顺序只能扩大可行性。数值评估显示：中等保真度阈值下树状调度可服务pumping无法服务的请求的90%以上；pumping类内自由操作顺序可挽回大部分优势；但链路采用树状调度后，树状MILP与顺序精确DP服务的请求集合完全一致。

Conclusion: 操作顺序与纯化调度是可相互替代的因素，但在对称Pauli噪声下纯化调度是主导可行性的关键因素。

Abstract: Entanglement routing establishes entangled pairs between distant nodes of a quantum network by purifying and swapping pairs generated on elementary links. Existing methods typically restrict the decision space along two axes: the operation order, often fixed to purify-then-swap (PtS), and the purification schedule of each link, often restricted to pumping. Focusing on linear repeater chains with finite link capacities, we relax both restrictions and study the maximization of the expected end-to-end throughput subject to a fidelity threshold under two quantum-noise models. We develop a unified capacity-aware optimization framework comprising an exact mixed-integer linear program (MILP) under PtS, instantiated with either pumping or general tree purification schedules, and an exact dynamic program (DP) over arbitrary operation orders. Under symmetric Pauli noise, we prove that pumping converges to a fidelity strictly below unity, imposing a capacity-independent feasibility bound and a maximum chain length on every pumping-based PtS method, whereas general tree schedules yield a capacity-dependent bound. We further prove that post-swap purification never increases the throughput, so a free operation order can only increase feasibility. Numerical evaluations confirm the bounds: tree schedules serve over 90\% of the requests that pumping cannot at moderate fidelity thresholds; within the pumping class, a free operation order recovers much of this advantage; but once links use tree schedules, the tree-based MILP and the order-exact DP serve identical request sets throughout. Operation order and purification schedule are thus substitutes, with the purification schedule the dominant factor governing feasibility under symmetric Pauli noise.

</details>


### [118] [Near-Optimal Quantum Algorithm and Complexity Analysis for Riccati Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37207)
*Jingyao Wang,Yanqiao Wang,Bowen Li,Jin-Peng Liu,Zhengfeng Ji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展量子算法，为连续/离散代数Riccati方程、微分Riccati方程及有限Riccati递归构造解矩阵的块编码，并给出查询复杂度上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riccati方程在控制与量子化学等领域很重要，但求解解矩阵的计算成本高，亟需量子加速以及复杂度极限的理论刻画。

Method: 提出Quantum Weighted Riesz Method，用加权Riesz分支算子构造解图的投影算子并恢复解矩阵，统一处理四类Riccati问题；同时证明查询下界及相关BQP完全性。

Result: CARE/DARE查询复杂度近最优Θ(Rα)（忽略对数因子）；DRE与有限时域问题额外含初始化条件因子κ_init；给出乘积式查询下界，并证明单控制离散代数与零终端微分Riccati方程的选取值promise问题为BQP完全。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了谱分离、输出归一化与初始化对量子Riccati求解必要影响，应用于LQ控制反馈评估、量子化学sRPA及其数值验证。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms that construct block-encodings of solution matrices for continuous- and discrete-time algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs and DAREs), differential Riccati equations (DREs), and finite Riccati recursions. The Quantum Weighted Riesz Method, unifying four kinds of Riccati problems, constructs projectors onto the solution graphs using weighted Riesz branching operators and recovers the solution matrices. Under the stated access and normalization assumptions, our algorithms for CAREs and DAREs have near-optimal query complexity $Θ(\mathcal Rα)$ up to logarithmic factors, with the generalized singularity factor $\mathcal R$ that reflects spectral separation, and the output normalization $α$. For DREs and finite-horizon problems, the query complexity has an additional initialization conditioning factor $κ_{\rm init}$. We establish product query lower bounds for specified input-oracle families, demonstrating necessary joint dependence on spectral separation and initialization or output scale. We also prove that selected-value promise problems are BQP-complete on explicit circuit-generated families of single-control discrete-time algebraic and zero-terminal differential Riccati equations. Applications include classical feedback evaluation for linear-quadratic control, a query-complexity comparison for stable random-phase approximation Riccati equations in quantum chemistry, and heated boundary control with numerical validation.

</details>


### [119] [Encoding and Node Choices in Transversal Fault-Tolerant Distributed Quantum Computations: An Initial Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37210)
*Seng W. Loke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 比较不同Bivariate-Bicycle(BB)编码与节点选择在分布式量子操作(如非局域CNOT、GCZ)中的表现，指出仅以最小化ebit消耗为编码选择标准不一定最优，还应考虑码距等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分布式量子计算选择合适的逻辑量子比特编码。直觉上倾向于使用物理量子比特少、ebit消耗低的编码，但作者希望验证这一直觉是否真正有利于降低分布式计算的逻辑错误率。

Method: 对比研究多种BB编码及节点选择方案，以分布式(非局域)CNOT和跨分布式逻辑量子比特的全局门(GCZ)为示例操作，并分析编码选择对并发操作能力(如自对偶[[120,8,12]] BB码上的横向物理GCZ)的影响。

Result: 若目标是降低分布式计算的逻辑错误率，仅减少ebit消耗未必是选择编码的最佳准则，还应考虑采用更多物理量子比特但码距更大的编码。编码选择可支持特定的并发操作：在自对偶[[120,8,12]] BB码上，横向物理GCZ在考虑横向Hadamard引起的逻辑置换后可实现8个并发的逻辑GCZ操作。

Conclusion: BB编码的选择需在ebit消耗与码距(错误率)之间权衡，不应仅以最小化ebit为目标；合适的编码还能解锁并发分布式逻辑操作。

Abstract: We compare and study different Bivariate-Bicycle (BB) encodings and node choices for distributed quantum operations such as transversal non-local CNOTs. We observe that while some encodings have more physical qubits requiring more ebits for a distributed computation, reducing ebit consumption alone might not be the best criterion for selecting an encoding for the logical qubits, if the goal is to reduce the logical error rate of the distributed computation; for example, one should also consider encodings with a larger distance, which using more physical qubits can provide. We consider distributed, or non-local, CNOTs and computation of the global gate (GCZ) over distributed logical qubits as examples. The choice of encoding enables particular concurrent operations; e.g., we show that a transversal physical GCZ on a self-dual $[[120,8,12]] BB$ code can realize eight concurrent logical GCZ operations after accounting for the logical permutation induced by transversal Hadamard.

</details>


### [120] [Ultra-high-distance quantum memories from amplified qLDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37231)
*Zijian Liang,Boren Gu,Yu-An Chen,Jens Eisert,Zongyuan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"距离放大器"模块化构造,通过在量子LDPC码上张量积基础码与放大器,并结合故障响应证书证明电路距离随放大步骤乘积式增长,从而由小码块构建并认证高距离量子存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大的码距本身并不保证量子存储受到良好保护,因为伴随式提取中的故障会传播为关联数据错误。随着电路规模增大,对电路距离的认证变得困难。因此需要一种可扩展、可认证的方法,在提升码距的同时提升电路级保护。

Method: 引入"距离放大器"这一模块化方法,将基础码与放大器做张量积,并对其伴随式提取电路进行适配。提出"故障响应证书",给出电路距离的严格下界。通过合适的门排序与旗比特(flag qubits)控制关联错误,建立明确条件使放大电路的电路距离等于各组成部分电路距离之积。证书可递归扩展。论文对三类放大器(flagged [[4,2,2]]、超图积、旋转曲面码)证明了该乘积关系,并展示了逻辑量子比特在放大过程中的显式追踪。

Result: 证明了电路距离在每一步放大中精确相乘的乘积关系,适用于三种放大器。示例:对[[18,4,4]]种子码连续四次应用flagged [[4,2,2]]放大器,得到[[13320,64,64]]码,电路距离 d_circ ≥ 48,校验权重 w ≤ 14。

Conclusion: 该框架使高距离量子存储可由较小构建块搭建并认证,为可扩展容错量子计算提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: A large code distance does not by itself guarantee a well-protected quantum memory, as faults during syndrome extraction can propagate into correlated data errors. Certifying circuit distance becomes demanding as circuits grow. In this work, we introduce distance amplifiers, a modular approach to increasing both code distance and certified circuit-level protection in quantum low-density parity-check codes. The construction tensors a base code with an amplifier, adapting their syndrome-extraction circuits to the amplified code. To certify the resulting memory, we develop a fault-response certificate that yields rigorous lower bounds on circuit distance. Suitable gate ordering and flag qubits control correlated errors, allowing us to establish explicit conditions under which the amplified circuit distance equals the product of the constituent circuit distances. We prove this multiplicative relation for three types of amplifiers: the flagged $[[4,2,2]]$, hypergraph-product, and rotated surface codes. The certificate extends recursively, so the circuit distance multiplies exactly at every amplification step. For example, four successive applications of the flagged $[[4,2,2]]$ amplifier to a $[[18,4,4]]$ seed yield a $[[13320,64,64]]$ code with the circuit distance $d_{\mathrm{circ}} \geq 48$ and check weights $w \leq 14$. We further show how individual logical qubits can be tracked explicitly through amplification. By enabling high-distance quantum memories to be built and certified from smaller building blocks, our framework offers a systematic route toward scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [121] [Approximate majorization and high-order capacity of quantum depolarizing channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37240)
*Zhiwei Song,Xin Wang,Marco Tomamichel,Kun Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究退极化信道在多次信道使用允许相干操作时纠缠是否带来优势的问题，提出并近似证明退极化信道输出的majorization关系，进而得到经典容量的紧致三阶展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息论的核心问题之一是：允许跨多次信道使用的相干操作时，纠缠能否为信息处理带来优势。对于退极化信道，King关于最小输出熵可加性的证明排除了经典通信在主导渐近阶上的优势，但更高阶是否可能存在优势仍未解决。

Method: 作者对退极化信道输出猜想了一个majorization关系，并证明其近似版本。证明结合了移位log-行列式的精确排序，以及针对乘积态输出谱的反集中界，从而细化King基于Schatten范数的分析。

Result: 作为直接应用，得到了量子退极化信道经典容量的紧致三阶展开。由此表明，纠缠码字与集体译码相对于乘积编码策略最多只能将消息规模提升O(log log n)比特。

Conclusion: 该工作加深了对有限块长情形下纠缠作用的理解，说明纠缠在退极化信道经典通信中带来的增益在高阶上受到严格限制，仅至多O(log log n)比特。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum information theory is to determine whether entanglement provides an advantage for information processing when coherent operations across multiple channel uses are allowed. For depolarizing channels, King's proof of the additivity of the minimum output entropy rules out such an advantage for classical communication at leading asymptotic order, while leaving open the possibility of an advantage for higher orders. In this work, we conjecture a majorization relation for the outputs of depolarizing channels and prove an approximate version of this conjecture. Our proof combines an exact ordering of shifted log-determinants with an anti-concentration bound for the spectrum of the product-state output, refining King's analysis based on Schatten norms. As a direct application, we obtain a tight third-order expansion for the classical capacity of quantum depolarizing channels. Consequently, entangled codewords and collective decoding can improve the message size over the product-coding strategy by at most $O(\log \log n)$ bits, sharpening our understanding of the role of entanglement in the finite-blocklength regime.

</details>


### [122] [L-band single-photon generation from a cavity-coupled silicon C-center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37244)
*Carolina Crosta,Kyu-Young Kim,Purbita Purkayastha,Abhijit Biswas,Jasvith Raj Basani,Amirehsan Alizadehherfati,Amirehsan Boreiri,Chang-Min Lee,Fabio Pezzoli,Edo Waks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在硅中隔离出单个C色心，并将其耦合到纳米光子晶体微腔，实现L波段电信波长的单光子发射与Purcell增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单光子源单片集成到硅光子学中，是实现可扩展量子通信架构的关键。硅色心中，C色心在电信L波段具有零声子发射，对应光纤最低传输损耗窗口，但此前在固态量子光学中一直未能实现单个C色心的隔离。

Method: 隔离单个硅C色心，并将其与纳米光子晶体微腔耦合以增强零声子线的自发辐射速率；通过时间分辨测量激发态寿命，并用二阶自相关测量验证单光子发射特性。

Result: 耦合后激发态寿命相比体材料条件缩短五倍，对应Purcell因子下界为33；二阶自相关测量得到未经背景校正的光学纯度 g²(0) = 0.23，证实单光子发射。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了硅中天然电信L波段量子光源，为超低损耗、长距离量子应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Monolithic integration of single-photon sources into silicon photonics offers a compelling solution for realizing scalable quantum telecommunication architectures. Among the candidate silicon color centers, the C-center features a zero-phonon emission in the telecom L-band, corresponding to the spectral window of minimum transmission loss in optical fibers. Despite this advantage, isolating single C-centers has remained an unrealized challenge in solid-state quantum optics. In this work, we report the isolation of a single silicon C-center. We enhance the spontaneous emission rate of the zero-phonon line by coupling the single emitter to a nanophotonic crystal cavity, observing a fivefold reduction in the excited-state lifetime compared to bulk conditions, which corresponds to a Purcell factor lower bound of 33. Second-order autocorrelation measurements confirm single-photon emission from the investigated defect, obtaining an optical purity of $g^{(2)}(0) = 0.23$ without background correction. Our results establish a native telecom L-band quantum light source in silicon, laying the foundation for ultra-low-loss and long-distance quantum applications.

</details>


### [123] [Directional transfer from coherence to longitudinal polarization and algebraic storage in a transmon with Markovian dephasing and a glassy environment: A non-Markovian fractional renewal model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37270)
*Ruha Uğraş Erdoğan,Ferit Acar Savacı*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个完全正定且保迹的非马尔可夫transmon模型：纵向退相干为马尔可夫型，横向为受玻璃态二能级系统环境启发的更新事件。通过微观哈密顿量识别耦合子空间，并用归一化Prabhakar等待时间律粗粒化慢缺陷重排。前向时间序更新历史给出精确映射级Volterra方程与非对易核-预解主方程，以及等价的漂移共轭Caputo型表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画玻璃态二能级系统环境下transmon的非马尔可夫退相干行为，尤其是慢缺陷重排导致的等待时间重型尾部如何按状态与方向选择性重塑退相干，并区分更新记忆效应与泊松型参考模型的差异。

Method: 建立CPTP非马尔可夫transmon模型（纵向马尔可夫退相干+横向更新事件）；由微观哈密顿量识别耦合子空间；用归一化Prabhakar等待时间律粗粒化慢缺陷重排；前向时间序更新历史导出精确映射级Volterra方程、非对易核-预解主方程及漂移共轭Caputo型表示（含初值项）；对无能量弛豫情形做Pauli空间分解，并用分支割分析与独立逆拉普拉斯重建验证数值精度。

Result: 无能量弛豫时，Pauli空间动力学分解为两个一维子空间和一个耦合(σ_y,σ_z)子空间，支持相干性与纵向极化间的方向性转移，而σ_x极化对更新不敏感。对0<α<1的重型等待时间，分支割分析给出由初始相干性生成的普适t^{-α}纵向极化存储尾部及O(t^{-1-α})反向转移；恒速泊松更新给出极点生成的指数衰减。独立零温弛豫在有限T_1下，当其速率低于纯退相干速率两倍时指数截断这些尾部。在给定参数下，Prabhakar更新在指定读出时刻的向前转移幅度大于尺度匹配恒速泊松参考与匹配完整期望计数的不均匀泊松参考（含有限T_1测试）；两种泊松参考给出更大的有限窗口峰值。

Conclusion: 由无序启发的更新记忆按状态与方向选择性重塑transmon退相干，Prabhakar型重型等待时间律产生区别于泊松更新的幂律存储尾部与更强向前转移，有限T_1弛豫可指数截断该尾部。

Abstract: We develop a completely positive and trace-preserving non-Markovian transmon model with longitudinal Markovian dephasing and transverse renewal events motivated by a glassy two-level-system environment. A microscopic Hamiltonian identifies coupling subspaces; a normalized Prabhakar waiting-time law phenomenologically coarse grains slow defect reconfigurations. Forward chronological renewal histories yield an exact map-level Volterra equation, a noncommuting kernel--resolvent master equation, and an equivalent drift-conjugated Caputo-type representation with its required initial-value term. Without energy relaxation, Pauli-space dynamics splits into two one-dimensional subspaces and a coupled $(σ_y,σ_z)$ subspace supporting directional transfer between coherence and longitudinal polarization; $σ_x$ polarization is renewal-insensitive. For heavy-tailed waiting times with $0<α<1$, branch-cut analysis gives a generic $t^{-α}$ storage tail in longitudinal polarization generated from initial coherence and $O(t^{-1-α})$ reverse transfer; constant-rate Poisson renewal yields pole-generated exponential decay. Independent zero-temperature relaxation at finite $T_1$ exponentially cuts off these tails when its rate is below twice the pure-dephasing rate. We compare independent inverse-Laplace reconstructions of the waiting-time density, memory kernel, and selected qubit-response components to assess numerical accuracy. For the studied parameters, Prabhakar renewal gives larger forward-transfer magnitudes at the prescribed readout than both a scale-matched constant-rate Poisson reference and an inhomogeneous Poisson reference matching the full expected-count profile, including finite-$T_1$ tests. Both Poisson references yield larger evaluated finite-window peaks. Disorder-motivated renewal memory reshapes transmon decoherence selectively by state and direction.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum Leakage Resilience of Shamir Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37276)
*Rishabh Batra,Fuyuki Kitagawa,Ryo Nishimaki,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究未修改Shamir秘密共享在量子泄漏下的鲁棒性，给出正反两方面结果：阈值率足够高时可抵抗局部量子泄漏，但设备间任意纠缠（如GHZ态）会破坏安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Shamir秘密共享已有单比特局部泄漏研究，但量子泄漏模型（尤其是设备间纠缠）尚未被系统分析，需要明确其安全边界。

Method: 在素域上定义量子局部泄漏信道（每方输出一个泄漏量子比特），分别考虑无预共享纠缠、不相交纠缠块、指定设备与敌手共享纠缠等场景；并构造GHZ态攻击作为反例。

Result: 无纠缠时区分优势为2^{-Ω(n)}，当t/n超过τ_*≈0.73339并保持固定正余量时安全；允许固定大小纠缠块或线性数量设备与敌手纠缠（阈值率足够接近1）仍安全；但t个泄漏设备共享GHZ态时，即使经典单比特泄漏也被攻破。

Conclusion: 固定阈值率高于τ_*时，可与任意纠缠的最大设备数为Θ(n)；最优支持分数仍未解决。

Abstract: We initiate the study of quantum leakage resilience of unmodified Shamir secret sharing over prime fields. A well-studied leakage model for Shamir's secret sharing classically is single-bit local leakage from each share. We consider its quantum analogue where, for each party, a local leakage channel takes as input the party's share and outputs a leaked qubit. Without preshared entanglement, we show that the distinguishing advantage is $2^{-Ω(n)}$ when the threshold rate $t/n=τ$ exceeds $τ_\star\approx0.73339$ by a fixed positive margin.
  More generally, we allow disjoint entangled blocks of any fixed maximum size where there is no entanglement between different blocks or with the adversary, and each block emits at most a fixed number of qubits. Security holds when the threshold rate is high enough (sufficiently close to one). We then allow a specified set of devices to share entanglement with the adversary. We show that security holds even when a linear number of devices ($αn$ for small $α>0$) share entanglement with each other and with the adversary for a large enough threshold rate.
  As a complementary negative result, we also show that even classical single-bit leakage makes Shamir scheme insecure if we allow arbitrarily large entanglement between the leakage devices. A GHZ state shared by exactly $t$ leakage devices makes even classical one-bit leakage insecure, without any entanglement with the adversary. In this attack, each participating device emits only one classical bit, and their joint parity distinguishes any chosen pair of secrets with a constant advantage. Thus, for fixed threshold rates above $τ_\star$, the maximum number of devices that may share arbitrary entanglement with one another and with the adversary while preserving security is linear in $n$ up to constant factors, although the optimal support fraction remains open.

</details>


### [125] [Parametric DRAG for leakage-suppressed exchange gates in superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37296)
*Yiwen Li,Xinsheng Tan,Yang Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出PDRAG方案，通过单实频指令施加一阶与二阶导数修正，在保持目标交换作用的同时抑制超导transmon中快速激发转移带来的两条泄漏通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程交换相互作用是超导电路中量子门与多体模拟的基础，但快速激发转移在弱非谐transmon中引入两条泄漏路径，导致门保真度下降并使模拟动力学偏离编码态空间，因此需要在不破坏目标交换的前提下抑制泄漏。

Method: 提出参数化绝热门导数消除（PDRAG）。利用两条泄漏路径的共轭结构确定一阶与二阶导数修正项，并通过一个实数频率指令实现。在Duffing模型下以g/2π=100 MHz进行校准，扫描12.5–35 ns脉冲时长；并将导数系数迁移至电荷哈密顿量，经幅度与载频重校准后评估转移效果。

Result: 校准后的PDRAG在12.5–35 ns范围内相对基础脉冲实现几何平均5.27×10^5的泄漏抑制；13 ns时校准全交换脉冲端点泄漏为1.98×10^-7。导数系数迁移到电荷哈密顿量后，在12.5–22 ns内实现平均893倍泄漏降低。Floquet回返干涉与电荷诱导的能隙移动解释了校准与迁移增益。

Conclusion: PDRAG以紧凑脉冲设计将导数修正与泄漏抑制的可编程相互作用相衔接，为超导电路中的快速交换门与多体模拟提供了有效的泄漏抑制手段，并揭示了Floquet干涉与能隙移动的物理机制。

Abstract: Programmable exchange interactions support quantum gates and many-body simulation in superconducting circuits. Fast excitation transfer, however, opens two leakage pathways in weakly anharmonic transmons, degrading gates and driving simulated dynamics outside the encoded state space. We introduce parametric derivative removal by adiabatic gate (PDRAG) to suppress both pathways while preserving the target exchange. Their conjugate structure determines first- and second-derivative corrections implemented through one real frequency command. At $g/2π=100$ MHz, calibrated PDRAG achieves a geometric-mean leakage reduction of $5.27\times10^5$ relative to the base pulse across 12.5--35 ns in Duffing simulations. At 13 ns, endpoint leakage reaches $1.98\times10^{-7}$ for the calibrated full-exchange pulse. Transferring the derivative coefficients to a charge Hamiltonian gives a mean 893-fold reduction over 12.5--22 ns after amplitude and carrier recalibration. Floquet return interference and charge-induced gap shifts explain the calibration and transfer gains, connecting compact pulse design to leakage-suppressed programmable interactions.

</details>


### [126] [A magnetic-field insensitive gate set for trapped-ion nuclear spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37318)
*Jonathan Paul Home,Jeremy Flannery,Matteo Mazzanti,Jan Apolin,Shreyans Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于操控高磁场（如Penning阱）中囚禁离子核自旋量子比特的完整操作方案，并设计了一种借助电子自旋实现快速两比特相位门的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高磁场下核自旋与电子自旋因外场哈密顿量主导而基本解耦。核自旋磁矩小，对外磁场不敏感、相干性好，但难以在快速时间尺度上操控。需要在保持核自旋编码抗磁干扰优势的同时实现快速门操作。

Method: 提出两比特相位门技术：利用电子自旋作为中介来实现核自旋量子比特间的快速耦合，同时量子比特仍以核自旋编码以屏蔽磁场影响。该方法适用于低场下对磁场不敏感的量子比特，并兼容微波场梯度与激光场梯度两种驱动方式。

Result: 给出了一套完整的高磁场囚禁离子核自旋量子比特操控操作集合，并验证所提相位门方案在微波和激光梯度下均可工作，且适用于低场磁场不敏感量子比特。

Conclusion: 通过电子自旋中介与核自旋编码相结合，可在保留抗磁场噪声优势的同时克服核自旋操控速度慢的问题，为高磁场囚禁离子量子计算提供可行方案。

Abstract: We outline a complete set of operations for manipulating nuclear spin qubits of trapped-ions stored in high magnetic fields such as required for Penning trapping, where the nuclear and electron spins are largely decoupled due to the dominance of the external field Hamiltonian. The reduced nuclear magnetic moment results in insensitivity to external magnetic fields compared to the use of an electron spin, but also makes the nuclear spin hard to manipulate on fast timescales. To maintain speed, we propose a two-qubit phase gate technique which utilizes the electron spin, but for which the qubit remains protected from magnetic fields through retaining the nuclear encoding. This method works for magnetic-field insensitive qubits at lower fields as well as for both microwave and laser field gradients.

</details>


### [127] [A Memory-Magic Exchange Law in Streaming Clifford+T Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37368)
*Jinze Yang,Yangyang Li,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了坐标式Clifford+T编译中"存储相位片段"与"提前消耗magic态(T门)"之间的交换率α，即每放弃1比特内存所需提前提交的T门数。利用Ramanujan界、行列式计数方法及高度二分法，给出α≥2、α≥11/5、α≥17/7等渐近且无条件下界，并在典型成本假设与均匀分布猜想下论证α=3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到达容错处理器的相位以加性片段形式出现，可选择"等待最后一片到达再记忆"（消耗跨轮经典内存）或"到达即执行"（消耗在相位未知前提交的magic态）。两种策略存在资源权衡，但缺乏对这种内存—T门交换率的定量刻画，本文旨在确定该交换率α。

Method: 针对无辅助比特的坐标式Clifford+T编译：用Clifford+T格的Ramanujan界得到α≥2及显式常数；用初等行列式方法，将四元数分子视为Q(√2)两个实嵌入下球面上的格点，计数任意旋转陪集附近的字，得渐近无条件α≥11/5；借助球面截面的高度二分法提升至α≥17/7。对Clifford框定陪集分析体积律与典型T-count，考察每旋转流水线等提交片段接近Clifford框定z旋转的过程，并研究分数直通族、条件熵、概率混合及相位梯度催化剂下的批量查表。

Result: Ramanujan界给出α≥2（谱方法的平方根障碍）；行列式方法给出渐近无条件α≥11/5，高度二分法提升至α≥17/7。Clifford框定陪集上体积律成立到次指数因子：几乎所有z旋转需T-count (3-o(1))log₂(1/ε)，此类过程有α≥3-o(1)，分数直通族在Ross-Selinger典型成本假设下达此值。在支持到T-count 22穷举验证的均匀分布猜想下，一般情形α=3，且应整旋转地释放内存。界在相位抵消为单位时仍成立，侧信息通过条件熵进入，概率混合减半成本但不改变速率。有干净辅助比特与相位梯度催化剂时，跨坐标批量查表使速率降至O(1/loglog(1/ε))，故常数速率律仅适用于坐标式合成。

Conclusion: 内存与提前提交T门之间存在确定性交换率α：坐标式Clifford+T编译下α从≥2逐步提升至≥11/5、17/7，典型/猜想条件下取α=3（整旋转释放内存最优）。常数速率律是坐标式合成特有的；引入干净辅助比特与相位梯度催化剂后速率降至O(1/loglog(1/ε))。

Abstract: A phase that reaches a fault-tolerant processor in additive pieces can be remembered until the last piece arrives, or executed on arrival: the first option costs classical memory carried across rounds, the second costs magic states committed before the phase is known. We determine the exchange rate $α$, committed $T$ gates per bit of memory forgone, for ancilla-free coordinatewise Clifford+$T$ compilation. The Ramanujan bound for the Clifford+$T$ lattice gives $α\ge2$ with explicit constants, the square-root barrier of the spectral method. An elementary determinant method, using that quaternion numerators are lattice points on spheres in both real embeddings of $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$, counts words near an arbitrary rotation coset below that barrier and gives $α\ge11/5$ asymptotically and unconditionally, and a height dichotomy for the resulting sphere sections raises this to $α\ge17/7$. At Clifford-framed cosets the volume law holds up to subexponential factors: all but a vanishing fraction of $z$-rotations need $T$-count $(3-o(1))\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$, and processes whose committed pieces are close to Clifford-framed $z$-rotations, including per-rotation pipelines, have $α\ge3-o(1)$, which a fractional-passthrough family attains under the Ross-Selinger typical-cost hypothesis. Under an equidistribution conjecture supported by exhaustive enumeration to $T$-count 22, $α=3$ in general and memory should be shed in whole rotations. The bounds hold even when the phases cancel to the identity; side information enters through a conditional entropy; probabilistic mixing halves the costs but not the rate. With clean ancillas and a phase-gradient catalyst, table lookups batched across coordinates drive the rate to $O(1/\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$, so the constant-rate law is specific to coordinatewise synthesis.

</details>


### [128] [Consistent histories and the ultrametric on infinite tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37375)
*Andrew Lesniewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为累积量子记录的历史空间构造了一种渐近几何。当Dowker-Halliwell干涉项按记录的累积保真度因子化时，其衰减由累积平方Bures距离控制；在无限记录极限下，Kakutani-von Neumann二分法给出弱等价与正交超选择扇区两种情形。Bures缺陷的收敛指数定义了历史空间上的伪超度量，商去零距离退化后成为完备超度量，并与规范不变扇区度量$\tilde d$一致，其值称为一致性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致历史表述只有在干涉项严格为零时才能赋予粗粒化事件序列经典概率，但有限模型中干涉项一般不严格消失，其"小"本身并不在历史空间上提供任何渐近几何。因此需要为累积量子记录的历史构造一种渐近几何，以刻画干涉衰减与扇区分离。

Method: 假设两个历史间的归一化Dowker-Halliwell干涉可因子化为其记录的累积保真度，则其衰减由累积平方Bures距离控制。在无限记录极限下运用Kakutani-von Neumann二分法；以Bures缺陷的收敛指数定义伪超度量，商去零距离退化后得到完备超度量，并与规范不变扇区度量$\tilde d$对照。

Result: 得到尖锐的扇区二分：可和Bures缺陷对应弱等价的记录序列，不可和缺陷产生正交超选择扇区并使归一化干涉消失。Bures缺陷的收敛指数给出历史空间上的伪超度量，商去零距离退化后为完备超度量，无需结果空间紧致性或记录族的连续性，且与$\tilde d$一致。一致性率有三重等价解释：扇区分离度量、归一化干涉负对数的多项式增长指数、以及由Helstrom公式给出的累积记录最小错误概率负对数的相应指数。经典分离剖面的长期均值缺陷为正时一致性率取最大值$\delta=1$，边界剖面$d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$则在零多项式率下产生不同超选择扇区，即$\delta=0$。

Conclusion: 干涉衰减的累积Bures距离刻画了历史空间的渐近几何，一致性率作为完备超度量统一了扇区分离、干涉衰减与记录判别误差三个方面，且在极弱正则性假设下成立。

Abstract: The consistent histories formulation of quantum mechanics assigns classical probabilities to sequences of coarse-grained events when the corresponding interference terms vanish. In finite models these terms generically need not vanish exactly, and their smallness alone supplies no asymptotic geometry on the space of histories. We construct such a geometry for histories that accumulate quantum records. When the normalized Dowker - Halliwell interference between two histories factorizes as the cumulative fidelity of their records, its decay is controlled by the accumulated squared Bures distance. In the infinite registration limit, the Kakutani - von Neumann dichotomy gives a sharp sector alternative: summable Bures defect yields weakly equivalent record sequences, whereas nonsummable defect produces orthogonal superselection sectors and vanishing normalized interference. The convergence exponent of the Bures defect defines a pseudo-ultrametric on histories. After quotienting its zero distance degeneracy, it becomes a complete ultrametric, requiring neither compactness of the outcome space nor continuity of the record family, and coincides with the gauge invariant sector metric $\tilde d$ introduced in \cite{LesTP}. Its value, the \emph{consistency rate}, has three equivalent interpretations: it is the sector separation metric, the polynomial growth exponent of the negative logarithm of normalized interference, and, by the Helstrom formula, the corresponding exponent of the negative logarithm of the minimum error probability for discriminating the cumulative records. Classical separation profiles determine the rate: positive long time mean defect gives the maximal value $δ=1$, while the borderline profile $d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$ produces distinct superselection sectors at zero polynomial rate, $δ=0$.

</details>


### [129] [Succinct Arguments for QMA in the Quantum Random Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37429)
*Alessandro Chiesa,Zihan Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子随机预言机模型（QROM）下，利用无结构困难性（理想哈希函数），通过将量子交互式预言机证明（QIOP）经量子commit-and-open范式编译，首次构造了不依赖额外密码假设或未证明猜想的QMA简洁论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典设定下，NP的简洁论证可仅由无结构困难性（如哈希函数）经PCP/IOP的commit-and-open编译得到；而已知的QMA简洁论证却依赖"结构化"密码原语或量子PCP猜想。作者希望消除这些额外假设，证明理想哈希函数同样足以支撑QMA的简洁论证。

Method: 核心是一个保效率的变换：将具有"公开查询可靠性"（public-query soundness，本文形式化的一种性质）的QIOP，通过自然的量子commit-and-open范式编译为同一语言的量子论证。该性质被已知的QMA QIOP所满足。关键组件是在QROM中形式化并构造了带局部打开的、可提取的量子态向量承诺。

Result: 得到首个在QROM下、无需额外密码假设或未证明猜想的QMA简洁论证；实现了仅从无结构困难性出发的QMA简洁论证；并给出了QROM中可提取量子态向量承诺的构造，具有独立价值。

Conclusion: 理想哈希函数不仅足以支持NP的简洁论证，也足以支持QMA的简洁论证，从而将经典commit-and-open编译范式推广到量子设定。

Abstract: Succinct arguments are a fundamental cryptographic primitive for verifying computational claims with small communication. In the classical setting, succinct arguments for NP can be constructed from unstructured hardness alone (e.g., hash functions) by compiling probabilistically checkable proofs (PCPs) or interactive oracle proofs (IOPs) for NP via the commit-and-open paradigm. In contrast, known succinct arguments for QMA rely on ``structured'' cryptographic primitives, or on the quantum PCP conjecture.
  We construct the first succinct argument for QMA in the quantum random oracle model (QROM) without relying on additional cryptographic assumptions or unproven conjectures. This yields succinct arguments for QMA from unstructured hardness alone, showing that ideal hash functions not only suffice for succinct arguments for NP but also for QMA.
  Underlying our result is an efficiency-preserving transformation that compiles quantum interactive oracle proofs (QIOPs), a recently introduced interactive generalization of quantum PCPs, into quantum arguments for the same language, via a natural quantum commit-and-open paradigm. Our transformation applies to every QIOP with public-query soundness, a notion that we formalize to capture a natural requirement of the commit-and-open paradigm and is satisfied by a known QIOP for QMA. As a key ingredient in our transformation, we formalize and construct extractable vector commitments for quantum states with local openings in the QROM, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [130] [Experimental certification of multipartite Bell correlations using only few-body symmetric correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37442)
*Jiacheng Sun,Mengyao Hu,Paweł Cieśliński,Jinfu Chen,Fei Shi,Bowen Wang,Hongyu Yang,Jizhou Wu,Jordi Tura,Valerio Scarani,Dian Wu,Jian Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并实验验证了利用固定最大关联阶数的少体置换不变（PI）关联来认证多体贝尔关联的方法，在六光子与八光子后选择Dicke态上实现贝尔关联违背，显著提升了噪声容忍度，且无需测量全体系关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多体量子系统规模增大，传统贝尔不等式依赖多体关联，估计这些关联在实验上愈发困难。因此需要一种仅用少体关联即可认证多体贝尔关联、且能扩展到更大系统的可实验实现方案。

Method: 构造针对六光子和八光子后选择Dicke态的少体置换不变（PI）贝尔关联见证量，采用固定的最大关联阶数。见证量使用对称的两体与四体关联项，六光子和八光子情形使用相同的关联算符，仅系数不同。其中在原有两体PI见证基础上额外加入少量四体关联项，并比较其噪声容忍度。

Result: 加入少量四体关联项显著提升了噪声容忍度：六量子比特情形下，所选见证量可容忍白噪声比例$p_c\approx0.301$，而相同每方设置数下最佳两体PI不等式仅为$p_c\approx0.048$。六光子和八光子实验分别以$32$和$8.9$个标准差违背见证量界，对应的后选择偏振态与半激发Dicke态的保真度分别为$0.933(4)$和$0.916(11)$。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅使用少体置换不变关联即可认证大规模多体系统的贝尔关联，无需测量全体系关联，为在更大系统中以可实验实现的方式认证多体贝尔关联提供了途径。

Abstract: Certifying Bell correlations in increasingly larger multipartite quantum systems is challenging because conventional Bell inequalities rely on many-body correlations, which become experimentally demanding to estimate as system size grows. Here we demonstrate that multipartite Bell correlations can be certified using few-body permutation-invariant (PI) correlations with a fixed maximal correlation order. We experimentally test few-body PI Bell correlation witnesses tailored for six- and eight-photon postselected Dicke states. The addition of a few four-body correlators is found to significantly enhance the noise tolerance compared with previously studied two-body witnesses: for instance, for six qubits, the selected witness tolerates a white-noise fraction of $p_c\approx0.301$, compared with $p_c\approx0.048$ for the best two-body PI inequality with the same two settings per party. For six and eight photons respectively, we observe violations of the witness bounds by $32$ and $8.9$ standard deviations, arising from postselected polarisation states with fidelities of $0.933(4)$ and $0.916(11)$ to the corresponding half-excitation Dicke states. The witnesses for the six- and eight-photon cases use the same symmetric two- and four-body correlators, just with different coefficients. These results demonstrate an experimentally accessible approach for certifying multipartite Bell correlations in larger systems without requiring full-body correlation measurements.

</details>


### [131] [An Operator-Norm Approach to Security with Quantum Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37467)
*Minki Hhan,Sunghyuk Jo,Qipeng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入算子范数方法处理量子随机预言机与随机置换模型下的非均匀安全性，给出同时适用于搜索成功概率与区分优势的统一归约，改进Yao盒、PRG与加盐(决策/求逆)游戏的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非均匀安全刻画了攻击者可在挑战前获得关于预言机的有界建议，是最现实的攻击模型。既有研究在决策游戏中其归约仅能处理成功概率，导致界较松，缺乏统一且紧致的处理框架。

Method: 提出算子范数视角下的非均匀安全分析方法，在量子随机预言机与随机置换模型中建立统一归约；并定义游戏的一种性质，用以分离离线阶段已有查询与后续在线查询的贡献。

Result: 得到Yao盒（含加盐与否）的紧界、伪随机生成器的改进界、决策游戏的最优通用加盐定理；对特定加盐构造得到更强界，包括加盐置换求逆（仅差对数因子）与加盐随机函数求逆（差对数因子与经典函数求逆已有差距）。

Conclusion: 算子范数方法统一了搜索与决策情形的非均匀安全归约，显著提升了紧致性，并为加盐构造提供最优或接近最优的界。

Abstract: Non-uniform security allows an adversary to receive bounded advice about an oracle before attempting a fresh challenge. This captures the most realistic attacks and has already been studied extensively in prior work. In this work, we introduce an operator-norm approach for non-uniform security in the quantum random oracle and random permutation models. This new approach yields a unified reduction for both search success probability and distinguishing advantage. Previously, the reduction only worked with success probability even in the decision games, yielding a worse bound.
  Our framework enables tight bounds for Yao's box, both with and without salting, and improved bounds for pseudorandom generators. We also prove an optimal generic salting theorem for decision games. By defining a property of a game, which separates the contributions of the existing queries in the offline stage and subsequent online queries, we obtain stronger bounds for specific salted constructions. These include salted permutation inversion, tight up to logarithmic factors, and salted random-function inversion, tight up to logarithmic factors and the gap already present in classical function inversion.

</details>


### [132] [Asymptotic Pseudospectra in Dissipative Floquet Quantum Systems: Geometric Structures and Observable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37523)
*Yuncheng Xie,Haozhe Shi,Zhuocheng Ma,Weibin Chu,Xin-Gao Gong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过按伪谱残差的渐近标度律将单位圆盘划分为指数、代数和有界精度区域，揭示了周期性驱动开放量子系统中伪谱的集体几何结构，并在两个可解模型中展示了其可观测的物理后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动开放量子系统中，非正规性使Floquet谱在热力学极限下不足以刻画动力学，需借助伪谱。然而既有研究多关注孤立伪谱的局域动力学，其全局联系及对可观测量的物理影响尚不清楚。

Method: 作者根据伪谱残差的渐近尺寸标度律，将单位圆盘分类为指数、代数与有界精度三类不同域，从而揭示其底层几何结构，并通过两个精确可解模型（耗散位移链和手性XY模型）验证物理含义。

Result: 在耗散位移链中，参数调节驱动可通过自旋波观测量探测的几何相变；在手性XY模型中，该几何结构解释了连续参数调节下两个关联信号交换保留顺序这一可测现象的起源。

Conclusion: 研究为理解耗散Floquet量子系统建立了新的理论范式，并预测了面向量子计算实验的几何驱动观测量。

Abstract: In periodically driven open quantum systems, nonnormality renders the Floquet spectrum insufficient as the system approaches the thermodynamic limit, so that pseudospectra are needed to characterize the dynamics accurately. While conventional approaches mainly focus on the local dynamics of isolated pseudospectra, their global connections and the resulting physical consequences for observables have remained largely unexplored. Here, we uncover this collective behavior by classifying the unit disk into distinct domains of exponential, algebraic, and bounded accuracy according to the asymptotic size-scaling laws of pseudospectral residuals, yielding an underlying geometric structure. We demonstrate the physical implications of this structure through two exactly solvable models. First, in a dissipative shift chain, parameter tuning drives geometric transitions that are detectable via spin-wave observables. Second, in a chiral XY model, we use this geometric structure to explain the origin of a measurable phenomenon: two correlation signals exchange their retention order under continuous parameter tuning. Our findings not only establish a new theoretical paradigm for understanding dissipative Floquet quantum systems, but also predict geometry-driven observables for quantum computing experiments.

</details>


### [133] [Digital quantum state preparation by nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37527)
*Jean Paul Sadia,Morten Hjorth-Jensen,Dean Lee,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受“成核”启发的格点哈密顿量量子态制备方法：先精确制备小格点的基态，再通过Trotter化绝热演化逐步扩展格点尺寸，并利用绝热定理与Trotter误差界给出演化时间与步数的充分条件；进一步引入变分优化的“优化成核”，在二维Ising模型上优于现有主流态制备方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备对量子算法至关重要，改进态制备方法可将资源需求降低若干数量级，因此需要更高效的格点哈密顿量基态制备方案。

Method: 受成核启发：先在量子电路中精确制备小格点哈密顿量的基态，再通过一系列Trotter化绝热演化“生长”阶段扩大格点尺寸至目标模型；基于绝热定理与Trotter误差界推导总演化时间和Trotter步数的充分条件；并提出在成核结构上叠加变分优化的“优化成核”。

Result: 给出了使成核制备的初始态达到目标保真度所需的总演化时间与Trotter步数的充分条件；在二维Ising模型上，优化成核的性能优于当前数字量子计算机上的一种领先态制备方法。

Conclusion: 成核及其优化版本为数字量子计算机上格点哈密顿量的量子态制备提供了可行且具竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is crucial for quantum algorithms, and improved state preparation methods can reduce resource requirements by orders of magnitude. In this work, we introduce a state preparation method for lattice Hamiltonians on digital quantum computers inspired by nucleation. In our method, the exact ground state of a small lattice Hamiltonian is prepared in a quantum circuit, then a series of growth stages are performed via Trotterized adiabatic evolution to increase the size of the lattice to the desired model. Using the adiabatic theorem and Trotter error bounds, we provide sufficient conditions on the total evolution time and number of Trotter steps required for nucleation to prepare an initial state with desired fidelity. In addition, we introduce optimized nucleation which considers variational optimization on top of the nucleation structure. For a two-dimensional Ising model, we show that optimized nucleation can outperform a current leading method for state preparation on digital quantum computers.

</details>


### [134] [Semiclassical spin-bath calculation of the nitrogen-isotope effect on NV$^-$ ensemble coherence in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37596)
*Chikara Shinei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作用半经典自旋浴模型证明：氮核同位素本身就能降低NV色心系综的T2，而T2*不变；由此解释了15N掺杂磁强计T2/T2*=8.05的部分来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心系综中T2/T2*比值在自然丰度（14N）金刚石中约16且与氮浓度[N]无关，但15N掺杂系综磁强计给出仅8.05±0.18（单量子约定）。作者要检验氮核同位素本身能否导致如此显著的降低，还是需要其他机制。

Method: 构造半经典自旋浴模型，采用Jahn-Teller分辨的P1色心超精细张量。计算P1对中处于超精细简并、因而可自由flip-flop的比例：14N (I=1)为1/4，15N (I=1/2)为5/16。对[N]=0.1–100 ppm进行数值模拟。

Result: 模拟确认该机制只缩短T2、对T2*完全无影响；T2(14N)/T2(15N)=1.118±0.011，与[N]无关，且高出1达11σ。实测对比度为2.07±0.25，比该预测大3.8σ。

Conclusion: 氮核同位素通过改变共振flip-flop通道数目确实会部分降低T2/T2*比值，但不足以解释全部观测效应，说明还存在其他贡献。该结果将“共振通道计数”确立为真实但部分的机制，并为多体计算设定了定量基准。

Abstract: The ratio $T_2$/$T_2^*$ of the Hahn-echo and Ramsey times of nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) ensembles is independent of the nitrogen concentration [N] and is $\approx$ 16 in diamond of natural isotopic abundance ($^{14}$N), yet a recent $^{15}$N-doped ensemble magnetometer gives only 8.05 $\pm$ 0.18 in the single-quantum convention. We ask whether the nitrogen nuclear isotope alone can cause such a reduction. Using a semiclassical spin-bath model with the Jahn-Teller-resolved P1 hyperfine tensor, we show that the fraction of P1 pairs that are hyperfine-degenerate, and hence free to flip-flop, is 1/4 for $^{14}$N (I = 1) but 5/16 for $^{15}$N (I = 1/2). Simulations over [N] = 0.1--100 ppm confirm that this shortens $T_2$ while leaving $T_2^*$ exactly unchanged: $T_2$($^{14}$N)/$T_2$($^{15}$N) = 1.118 $\pm$ 0.011, independent of [N] and 11$σ$ above unity. The measured contrast, 2.07 $\pm$ 0.25, is 3.8$σ$ larger, identifying resonant-channel counting as a real but partial contribution and setting a quantitative benchmark for many-body calculations.

</details>


### [135] [Optimal interferometric certification of multi-photon indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37620)
*Marco Robbio,Michał Oszmaniec,Nicolas J. Cerf,Ernesto Galvão,Leonardo Novo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种高效且实验友好的协议，用于估计或界定N光子态与最接近的完全不可区分态之间的保真度F_ind，分别适用于可分离态源和任意N光子态，且均能以最优的O(1/ε)采样量认证F_ind = 1 - O(ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子不可区分性是光子量子技术的关键资源，但其表征通常依赖资源密集型方法，且此前方法需要对部分不可区分模型作出先验假设，缺乏严格且具有操作意义的认证手段。

Method: 提出两种协议：其一针对制备可分离态的光源，使用单一傅里叶干涉仪结合光子数分辨探测，给出F_ind的紧致双侧界；其二将线性光学干涉仪的随机化实现与光子计数相结合，可直接估计任意N光子态的F_ind。两者均基于Hong-Ou-Mandel测试的多光子推广。

Result: 两种协议均能以可证最优的O(1/ε)采样量认证F_ind = 1 - O(ε)，第一协议提供紧致双侧界，第二协议实现任意N光子态的直接估计，且无需对部分不可区分模型作先验假设。

Conclusion: 该工作使多光子不可区分性的严格且具有操作意义的认证在当前光子技术条件下变得可及。

Abstract: Multiphoton indistinguishability is a key resource for photonic quantum technologies, yet its characterization typically relies on resource-intensive methods. In this work, we develop two efficient and experimentally friendly protocols to estimate or bound the fidelity $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$ of an $N$-photon state to the closest perfectly indistinguishable state. The first protocol applies to sources preparing separable states, uses a single Fourier interferometer together with photon-number-resolving detection, and yields tight two-sided bounds on $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$. The second protocol combines randomized implementations of linear-optical interferometers with photon counting, enabling direct estimation of $F_{\mathrm{ind}}$ for arbitrary $N$-photon states. Both protocols can certify $F_{\mathrm{ind}} = 1 - \mathcal{O}(ε)$ using provably optimal $\mathcal{O}(1/ε)$ samples, in contrast to previous approaches which required prior assumptions on the model of partial distinguishability. Our methods, based on a multiphoton generalization of the Hong-Ou-Mandel test, bring the rigorous and operationally meaningful certification of multiphoton indistinguishability within reach of current photonic technologies.

</details>


### [136] [Operational theory for photonic circuits: generalizing linear optics beyond quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37628)
*Ismaël Septembre,Matthias Kleinmann,Martin Plávala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子光学中正交算符与机械谐振子相空间坐标的类比推广至量子理论之外，建立了基于正交算符的广义概率论光子线路分析框架，并表明Hong-Ou-Mandel效应、Mach-Zehnder干涉等现象并非量子理论所特有。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探讨线性光学中的典型现象（如Hong-Ou-Mandel效应和Mach-Zehnder干涉）究竟是量子理论独有的特征，还是在更广泛的广义概率论框架下同样成立，从而厘清量子光学现象的"量子性"边界。

Method: 利用量子光学正交算符与机械谐振子相空间坐标之间的类比，将线性光学理论推广到量子理论之外；引入一套基于正交算符、面向广义概率论的光子线路分析框架，并在该框架下分别考察分束器输出端的符合计数以及Mach-Zehnder干涉行为。

Result: 分束器输出端光子符合计数的消失不仅出现在量子理论中，也出现在经典光以及一种修正的量子化光理论中；Mach-Zehnder干涉在量子理论与这些替代理论之间不存在定性差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架可作为研究复杂光子装置与光子量子计算机的广义理论的基础，并表明部分被视为量子特征的光学现象实际上具有更广泛的适用性。
