Abstract: We use the analogy between the quadrature operators in quantum optics and the phase-space coordinates in a mechanical harmonic oscillator to generalise the theory of linear optics beyond quantum theory. For this, we introduce a framework for analysing photonic circuits within generalised probabilistic theories based on quadrature operators. Using this framework, we ask whether phenomena like the Hong-Ou-Mandel effect, Mach-Zehnder interferometry are specific to quantum theory or also occur across a broader class of theories. We find the latter: the vanishing photon coincidences at the output of a beam splitter also occur for classical light and for a modified version of quantised light. Similarly, for Mach-Zehnder interferometry we find that there is no qualitative difference between quantum theory and these alternative theories. Our framework can serve as a foundation to study generalised theories of complex photonic set-ups and photonic quantum computers.

</details>


### [137] [Adaptive decoding of quantum LDPC codes through decoder disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37629)
*Maida Wang,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于BP与OSD之间不一致性的自适应qLDPC解码器，将深度搜索资源集中在高风险实例上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码的精确解码通常依赖昂贵的后处理搜索，但不同综合征的解码难度差异很大。作者希望找到一种解码器内部信号，用于识别哪些实例真正需要更深的搜索，从而避免对全部实例统一施加高成本后处理。

Method: 引入基于置信传播（BP）和有序统计解码（OSD）的自适应解码器，以BP硬判决与满足综合征的零阶OSD解之间的不一致性作为内部风险信号，据此对高风险实例升级搜索深度。在[[144,12,12]]二元自行车码上进行电路级去极化噪声仿真，并将结果扩展到提升积构造的径向qLDPC码，同时在Quantinuum H2离子阱处理器上开展Z存储实验验证信号预测性。

Result: 在[[144,12,12]]二元自行车码上，仅对风险最高的20%实例升级搜索，可恢复全单自由变量扫描所带来逻辑错误率改进的85%–92%，同时将平均串行解码成本相对对所有实例施加相同截断搜索降低3.6倍。在相同预算下，基于不一致性的路由优于基于BP综合征残差、综合征权重和随机选择的路由。在结构不同的径向qLDPC码上，升级20%实例可恢复87%–94%的可用增益。H2处理器Z存储实验表明该信号在器件噪声下仍具预测性。

Conclusion: 解码器内部的不一致性信号能够揭示哪些解码实例值得投入更多计算，从而把经典计算资源集中到最可能受益的实例上，为qLDPC码的高效自适应解码提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurate decoding of quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes often relies on expensive post-processing search, although decoding difficulty varies strongly between syndromes. We find that the benefit of deeper post-processing search is highly concentrated in a small subset of decoding instances, and that these instances can be identified directly from the decoder itself. To this end, we introduce an adaptive decoder based on belief propagation (BP) and ordered-statistics decoding (OSD), using the disagreement between the BP hard decision and the syndrome-consistent order-zero OSD solution as an internal risk signal to determine where deeper search is required. On a $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate bicycle code under circuit-level depolarising noise, encoding $12$ logical qubits in $144$ data qubits with distance $12$, escalating only the highest-risk $20\%$ of instances recovers $85\%$ to $92\%$ of the improvement in logical error rate available from a full one-free-variable sweep, while reducing the mean serial decoding cost by a factor of $3.6$ relative to applying the same truncated search to every instance. At matched budget, disagreement-guided routing also outperforms routing based on the BP syndrome residual, syndrome weight and random selection. The same behaviour reappears on a structurally distinct radial qLDPC code obtained from a lifted-product construction, where escalating $20\%$ of instances recovers $87\%$ to $94\%$ of the available gain. A $Z$-memory experiment on the Quantinuum H2 trapped-ion processor, implementing both $X$-type and $Z$-type checks, further shows that the signal remains predictive under device noise. These results show that decoder-internal disagreement can expose where additional decoding effort is valuable, allowing classical computation to be concentrated on the instances most likely to benefit from it.

</details>


### [138] [Resonance Breaking in Noisy Shor's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37636)
*Zhengwei Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析含噪量子电路中的共振破坏现象，论证在一层任意小错误率的去极化噪声下，Shor算法的指数级量子优势被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor算法被认为能提供指数级量子优势，但实际量子设备存在噪声。作者希望探究在极弱噪声（一层任意小错误率的去极化噪声）下，这种指数级量子优势是否仍然成立。

Method: 将n比特串的测量分布表示为Pauli路径积分中4^n个波函数的叠加；分析电路中间层引入独立去极化噪声（速率λ）时的测量概率；针对Hamming权重d，分别给出高频项与低频项的测量率上界。

Result: 高频项的测量率被限定为2(1-λ)^d；低频项的测量率被限定为O(n^d)/2^{n/2}。当n和d趋于无穷时，测量率趋于零，即发生共振破坏。此外，作者设计了一个多项式时间O(n^d)的经典因式分解算法来替代低频贡献。

Conclusion: 在一层任意小错误率的去极化噪声下，共振破坏使Shor算法的指数级量子优势消失，并且低频贡献可由多项式时间的经典算法替代。

Abstract: We argue that the exponential quantum advantage in Shor's algorithm is broken under one-layer depolarizing noise of arbitrary small error rate, by analyzing a resonance breaking phenomenon in noisy quantum circuits. First, we express the distribution of measurements on $n$-qubit strings as the superposition of the $4^n$ wave functions in the Pauli path integral. In the noiseless case, the bit-strings achieving resonant peaks guaranteed a constant rate of successful measurements to factor a large number. Secondly, when the middle layer of the quantum circuit has independent depolarizing noise of rate $λ$, for any Hamming weight $d$, we obtain corresponding measurement rate bounded by $2(1-λ)^d$ for higher frequency terms and by $O(n^d)/2^{n/2}$ for low frequency terms. The rate approaches to zero as $n$ and $d$ approaches to infinity. Resonance breaking destroys the exponential quantum advantage. Furthermore, we design a classical factoring algorithm in polynomial time $O(n^d)$ to substitute the low frequency contribution.

</details>


### [139] [Integrated balanced homodyne detector using CMOS capacitive-feedback TIA for quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37671)
*Sarah Bastiaens,Cedric Bruynsteen,Simone Cammarata,Axl Bomhals,Leandro da Silva,Michiel Van Osta,Johan Bauwelinck,Xin Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一款低噪声高速平衡零差探测器，将28 nm CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器与imec iSiPP200硅光子平台的定制光子集成电路相结合。接收机实现7.6 GHz的3 dB带宽、27 dB的最大散粒噪声裕度、13.7 GHz的散粒噪声受限带宽以及最高54.8 dB的共模抑制比，验证了CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器在量子与相干传感光应用中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向连续变量量子密钥分发、量子随机数生成和光学相干层析等对噪声与速度要求严苛的量子与相干传感光应用，需要兼具低噪声和高速性能的平衡零差探测器，而现有实现方案在带宽、散粒噪声裕度和共模抑制等指标上难以同时满足需求。

Method: 采用28 nm CMOS工艺实现电容反馈跨阻放大器，并与基于imec iSiPP200硅光子平台制造的定制光子集成电路进行异构集成，构成平衡零差探测接收机，通过电容反馈拓扑在保证带宽的同时抑制噪声并提升共模抑制能力。

Result: 接收机实现7.6 GHz的3 dB带宽，最大散粒噪声裕度达27 dB，散粒噪声受限带宽为13.7 GHz，共模抑制比最高达54.8 dB。

Conclusion: 结果表明CMOS电容反馈跨阻放大器适用于高要求的量子与相干传感光应用，包括连续变量量子密钥分发、量子随机数生成和光学相干层析。

Abstract: A low-noise, high-speed balanced homodyne detector is demonstrated, combining a capacitive-feedback transimpedance amplifier implemented in 28 nm CMOS with a custom photonic integrated circuit fabricated in imec's iSiPP200 silicon photonics platform. The receiver achieves a 3-dB bandwidth of 7.6 GHz, a maximum shot noise clearance of 27 dB, a shot noise-limited bandwidth of 13.7 GHz and a common-mode rejection ratio up to 54.8 dB. These results demonstrate the suitability of CMOS capacitive-feedback transimpedance amplifiers for demanding quantum and coherent sensing optical applications, including continuous-variable quantum key distribution, quantum random number generation, and optical coherence tomography.

</details>


### [140] [Constant-rate quantum codes with low-weight stabilizers and full logical Clifford actions via transversal and fold-transversal gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37699)
*Takaya Matsuura,Yohji Chin,Shohei Kiryu,Kosuke Fukui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类量子码，具有渐近恒定的编码速率和次对数增长的稳定子权重，且仅用横向与折叠横向门即可实现完整逻辑Clifford群，为低空间开销的容错量子计算提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空间开销的容错量子计算不仅需要高编码速率的量子纠错码，还需要空间高效的逻辑操作实现。横向与折叠横向门因限制错误传播且不需额外量子比特而极具吸引力，但其启用的逻辑操作通常受限。核心挑战在于构造同时具备完整逻辑Clifford门集合与优良码参数的量子码。

Method: 基于码空间为其Tanner图自同构群的绝对不可约Steinberg模的经典码；对这些经典码取超图积（hypergraph product），得到量子码，使其所有逻辑Clifford操作均可由一组固定的横向与折叠横向门合成。

Result: 所构造的量子码族具有渐近恒定的编码速率与次对数增长的稳定子权重，且支持整个逻辑Clifford群；稳定子权重的缓慢增长使小规模实例具备高纠错性能。

Conclusion: 该结果为使用横向与折叠横向门、实现低空间开销的容错量子计算提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Low-space-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computation requires not only high-rate quantum error-correcting codes but also space-efficient implementations of logical operations. Transversal and fold-transversal gates are promising since they limit error propagation and require no additional qubits. However, the logical operations they enable are typically restricted, and a central challenge is to construct codes that combine a complete set of logical Clifford gates with favorable code parameters. In this work, we introduce a family of quantum codes with an asymptotically constant encoding rate and sublogarithmically growing stabilizer weight, while supporting the entire logical Clifford group using only transversal and fold-transversal gates. Our construction is based on classical codes whose code spaces are absolutely irreducible Steinberg modules of their Tanner-graph automorphism groups. Taking hypergraph products of these classical codes yields quantum codes for which all logical Clifford operations can be synthesized from a fixed set of transversal and fold-transversal gates. Moreover, the slow growth of the stabilizer weight enables a high error-correcting performance in small instances. These results provide a path toward fault-tolerant quantum computation with low space overhead using transversal and fold-transversal gates.

</details>


### [141] [Optimal single-copy estimation of quantum state moments: why $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^3)$ and $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^4)$ are equally hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37781)
*Zhenhuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文确定了估计未知量子态矩 $\operatorname{Tr}(\rho^t)$ 的最优样本复杂度：对每个固定整数 $t\ge2$ 和小常数加性误差为 $\Theta(d^{\lceil\log_2 t\rceil/(1+\lceil\log_2 t\rceil)})$，呈现二分层次结构；并证明非自适应协议下界 $\Omega(d^{1-1/t})$，说明自适应在 $t\ge4$ 时有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计未知量子态的非线性性质在实验访问受限下是量子学习的基础问题。纯度（$t=2$）的最优复杂度已知为 $\Theta(\sqrt d)$，但更高阶矩的最优维度依赖此前不明。本文旨在对每个固定整数 $t\ge2$ 解决该样本复杂度问题，并厘清自适应与非自适应测量的差异。

Method: 上界：采用自适应态滤波（adaptive state filtering），将高阶矩估计归约到更小状态空间中的低阶矩估计。下界：通过光滑插值直接比较矩匹配（moment-matched）系综，绕开最大混合态作为中间假设。

Result: 对任意足够小的常数加性误差，最优样本复杂度为 $\Theta(d^{\lceil\log_2 t\rceil/(1+\lceil\log_2 t\rceil)})$，成功概率为常数。复杂度呈现二分层次：每个整数 $h\ge1$，所有矩阶 $2^{h-1}<t\le2^h$ 共享相同指数；特别地，三阶和四阶矩均需 $\Theta(d^{2/3})$ 个副本。对任意非自适应单副本协议，建立了 $\Omega(d^{1-1/t})$ 下界，与已知上界匹配，证明 $t\ge4$ 时自适应具有优势。

Conclusion: 本文完全刻画了任意固定阶量子态矩估计的最优样本复杂度，揭示其依赖于 $t$ 的二进制对数的二分层次结构；同时明确了自适应测量相对于非自适应测量在 $t\ge4$ 时的严格优势。上界归约与下界插值技术为超越 $\sqrt d$ 的矩估计问题提供了匹配的分析框架。

Abstract: Estimating nonlinear properties of an unknown quantum state with restrictive experimental accessibility is a fundamental problem in quantum learning. We study the sample complexity of estimating the state moments $\operatorname{Tr}(ρ^t)$, allowing arbitrary adaptive single-copy measurements. While purity estimation has optimal sample complexity $Θ(\sqrt d)$ at constant additive error, the optimal dimension dependence for higher moments has remained unclear. We resolve this question for every fixed integer $t\ge 2$: for any sufficiently small constant additive error, the optimal sample complexity is \[ Θ\!\left(d^{\frac{\lceil\log_2 t\rceil}{1+\lceil\log_2 t\rceil}}\right) \] with constant success probability. The complexity thus follows a dyadic hierarchy: for each integer $h\geq1$, all moment orders $2^{h-1}<t\leq2^h$ share the same exponent. In particular, the third and fourth moments both require $Θ(d^{2/3})$ copies. We also establish an $Ω(d^{1-1/t})$ lower bound for arbitrary nonadaptive single-copy protocols, matching the known upper bound and demonstrating an advantage from adaptivity for every $t\geq4$. Our upper bound uses adaptive state filtering to reduce higher-order moment estimation to lower-order moment estimation in a smaller state space. For the lower bound, we directly compare moment-matched ensembles through a smooth interpolation, bypassing the maximally mixed state as an intermediate hypothesis and yielding matching bounds beyond $\sqrt d$.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Complexity of Ancilla-Free Unitary Embeddings for Nonlinear Dynamics via Generalized State-Dependent Double-Bracket Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37802)
*Yuki Ito,Hideaki Hakoshima,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无辅助比特的双括号量子算法推广到状态依赖的厄米算符，实现对非线性动力学的全相干无辅助比特幺正嵌入，并将查询复杂度与动力学对初值的敏感性（收缩/非膨胀/膨胀）联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子演化本质是线性的，直接模拟非线性动力学通常需要额外量子资源。核心问题是：非线性动力学能否嵌入全相干、无辅助比特的幺正线路，以及其复杂度如何决定所需量子资源。

Method: 将无辅助比特双括号虚时间演化算法中的状态无关哈密顿量替换为状态依赖厄米算符，递归调用初始态制备 oracle 及其逆，构造全相干、无辅助比特的幺正嵌入，并对查询成本与动力学复杂度进行关联分析。

Result: 给出查询上界：收缩情形为 exp(O(T))，非膨胀情形为 exp(O(T²))，膨胀情形为 exp(exp(O(T)))。对离散 Gross–Pitaevskii 方程，在特定单量子比特初态族上达到最优最坏情形查询复杂度 Θ(e^{gT/2})，其中 g 为非线性强度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非线性动力学复杂度与相干量子模拟查询成本之间的联系，为设计具有最优查询复杂度的无辅助比特幺正嵌入提供了基础。

Abstract: Simulating nonlinear dynamics with quantum computers has gained increasing attention. In general, such simulations require additional quantum resources because unitary quantum evolution is linear. A fundamental question is how nonlinear dynamics can be embedded into fully coherent, ancilla-free unitary circuits and how the complexity of the dynamics governs the required quantum resources. In this work, we generalize the ancilla-free double-bracket quantum algorithm for imaginary-time evolution by replacing its state-independent Hamiltonian with a state-dependent Hermitian operator. Our framework recursively calls an initial state preparation oracle and its inverse, and prepares the target solution to any prescribed accuracy using a fully coherent, ancilla-free unitary embedding. We relate the query cost to the complexity of the nonlinear dynamics, specifically their sensitivity to initial conditions. We obtain query upper bounds of $\exp(O(T))$, $\exp(O(T^2))$, and $\exp(\exp(O(T)))$ when the distance between solutions contracts at least exponentially (contractive), does not increase (nonexpansive), or grows at most exponentially (expansive), respectively, where $T$ is the target evolution time. For the discrete Gross--Pitaevskii equation, our ancilla-free double-bracket circuit achieves optimal worst-case query complexity $Θ(e^{gT/2})$ over a specified family of single-qubit initial states, where $g>0$ is the nonlinearity strength. These results connect the complexity of nonlinear dynamics to the query cost of coherent quantum simulation and provide a foundation for designing ancilla-free unitary embeddings with optimal query complexity.

</details>


### [143] [Measuring the spin of a spin-1/2 and getting a result of 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37822)
*Joseph McGowan,Nicholas Mantella,Noah Baker,Aephraim M. Steinberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次按Albert-Aharonov-Vaidman最初方案，在BEC中实现弱Stern-Gerlach实验，测得反常弱值并对弱磁动量踢实现14倍放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱值的分母含初末态重叠，重叠很小时弱值可任意大，从而允许出现位于被测量算符本征谱之外的反常弱值。虽然此类反常弱值已在多种光学系统中被观测，但1988年Albert、Aharonov与Vaidman提出的原始弱Stern-Gerlach实验方案此前尚未被真正实现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将87Rb的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体置于磁场梯度中，通过弱Stern-Gerlach相互作用产生微小的动量踢，并进行低概率的事后选择（postselection），从而测量对应算符的反常弱值。

Result: 对约8 μm/s的弱磁动量踢实现了14倍的等效放大；同时发现增大动量踢会削弱测量的"弱性"，导致放大倍数下降。

Conclusion: 该工作首次以最初提出的形式实现了弱Stern-Gerlach实验方案，在原子体系中验证了反常弱值与弱值放大效应，并实验展示了放大倍数与测量弱性之间的制约关系。

Abstract: The weak value of an operator is the average result of measuring that operator on a quantum system given specified initial and final states. The overlap between these states appears in the denominator of the weak value, and thus if that overlap is small, the weak value can be arbitrarily large. In 1988, Albert, Aharonov, and Vaidman proposed a weak Stern-Gerlach experiment which would be able to measure a so-called anomalous weak value, a measurement result which lies out- side of the eigenvalue spectrum of the operator being measured. Though anomalous weak values have been measured in numerous optical systems, here we present the first such realization of this experiment as originally proposed. By placing a Bose-Einstein condensate of 87Rb in a magnetic gradient and performing an unlikely postselection, we measure an effective magnification of a weak magnetic momentum kick (~ 8 $μ$m/s) by a factor of 14. We also demonstrate that increasing the momentum kick decreases the "weakness" of the measurement and reduces the amplification.

</details>


### [144] [Towards verifiable quantum advantage with random circuits: Observables that survive concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37890)
*Antonio A. Mele,Francesco A. Mele,Jarrod R. McClean,Thomas E. O'Brien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在任意固定空间维度的局域随机电路中，固定阶OTOC在系统尺度深度下具有逆多项式级别的涨落，从而排除强集中现象对OTOC类可验证量子优势方案的阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路的量子优势演示是量子计算的核心目标，但采样类方案难以验证，可观测量类方案又面临集中效应抹平电路实例间差异的问题。近期实验提出以OTOC估计作为可验证量子优势任务，但其涨落能否随系统规模增长而存续尚未明确。

Method: 对任意固定空间维度的广泛局域随机电路，理论证明固定阶OTOC在系统尺度深度下的逆多项式涨落；针对一维Haar随机brickwork电路，进一步刻画贡献涨落的门数量与空间局域性；并构造一种经典算法用于OTOC模拟。

Result: 证明固定阶OTOC的涨落随系统规模仍保持逆多项式量级；在一维Haar随机brickwork电路中，宏观多数量子门对涨落有贡献，但每层贡献被限制在亚线性宽度区域；所提经典算法在一维OTOC模拟上首次实现对暴力经典模拟的严格改进。

Conclusion: OTOC涨落在系统规模增大时依然存在，排除了强集中作为OTOC类可验证量子优势方案的障碍；经典不可解性仍未解决，但该工作为OTOC量子优势提议提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Demonstrating quantum advantage on current quantum hardware is a central goal of quantum computing, and random quantum circuits underpin many leading proposals. Yet sampling-based demonstrations are often difficult to verify, while observable-based approaches face a different challenge: concentration can suppress differences between circuit instances. Recent experiments have put forward the estimation of out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) in random circuits as a promising task for verifiable quantum advantage, yet whether their circuit-to-circuit fluctuations survive concentration as system size grows has remained open. Here we show that they do. For broad classes of local random circuits in any fixed spatial dimension, we prove inverse-polynomial fluctuations of fixed-order OTOCs at system-scale depths. In one-dimensional Haar-random brickwork circuits, we further show that macroscopically many gates contribute to these fluctuations, yet in each layer they remain confined to a sublinear-width region. As a byproduct, we develop a classical algorithm exhibiting the first rigorous improvement over brute-force classical simulation of OTOCs in 1D. Although classical hardness remains open, our results rule out strong concentration as an obstruction to OTOC-based proposals for verifiable quantum advantage.

</details>


### [145] [A Neutral Atom-Based Hybrid Classical-Quantum Approach for the Entanglement Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37900)
*M. Yassine Naghmouchi,Quentin Ma,Agathe Blaise,René Veyland,Wesley Coelho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将保真度约束的纠缠路由建模为不可拆分多商品流问题，并以中性原子量子优化作为列生成中的定价预言机，在小规模基准上实现低于1%的最优性间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息网络中的端到端纠缠分发需要在有限资源与保真度约束下进行路由；如何高效求解带保真度约束的路由并最大化被接纳请求数，是一个具有实际需求但计算困难的问题。

Method: 将纠缠路由刻画为保真度约束的不可拆分多商品流问题；采用经典—量子混合列生成框架：经典受限主问题选择路由，定价问题生成满足保真度的路径；将NP难的约束最短路定价问题表述为QUBO，并用硬件感知的寄存器嵌入与实例驱动脉冲整形在中性原子处理器上求解。

Result: 在中性原子处理器模拟器上的小规模基准中，结合warm-start与后处理的方法在所有测试规模上最优性间隙低于1%；所选模拟退火路由生成基线的间隙最高达6%。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子优化可作为列生成中的定价预言机，生成的比特串能为经典精化提供有用候选路由；但该概念验证未证明量子优势或可扩展性，仍需在更大规模实例、更强经典基线与真实量子硬件上进一步评估。

Abstract: Efficient end-to-end entanglement distribution in quantum information networks requires routing under limited resources and fidelity constraints. We study entanglement routing as a fidelity-constrained unsplittable multicommodity flow problem that maximizes the number of admitted requests. As a proof of concept, we integrate neutral-atom quantum optimization into a hybrid classical--quantum column-generation framework. A classical restricted master problem selects routes, while a pricing problem generates fidelity-feasible paths. We formulate this NP-hard constrained shortest-path pricing problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and address it with hardware-aware register embedding and instance-driven pulse shaping. To our knowledge, this is the first study of neutral-atom quantum optimization as a pricing oracle for fidelity-constrained entanglement routing. On small benchmark instances evaluated with neutral-atom processor emulators, the method, combined with warm-start and post-processing, achieves an optimality gap below 1% across all tested sizes; the selected simulated-annealing route-generation baseline exhibits gaps of up to 6%. These results indicate that the generated bitstrings can provide useful candidate routes for classical refinement within column generation. This proof of concept does not establish quantum utility or scalability; further evaluation on larger instances, against stronger classical baselines, and on quantum hardware is needed.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal Quantum Junta Testing without Inverse Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37926)
*Jinge Bao,Minbo Gao,Penghui Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子 juntas 测试的查询复杂度，给出酉算子与信道的紧致界：在 $n\ge 2k$、$k$ 充分大及较宽的 $\varepsilon$ 范围内，查询复杂度为 $\Theta(k/(\varepsilon^2\log k))$；通道情形在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下确立最优 $\Theta(k/\log k)$，闭合了此前 $\tilde O(k)$ 上界与 $\tilde\Omega(\sqrt k)$ 下界之间的间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 junta 测试是量子性质测试的核心问题，但酉算子与信道的查询复杂度界并不紧致：通道情形在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下存在 $\tilde O(k)$ 与 $\tilde\Omega(\sqrt k)$ 之间的显著间隙，且前向查询模型与带逆访问模型的差异尚不清晰。作者旨在确定最优复杂度并揭示其经典本质。

Method: 针对酉算子采用仅前向模型（可访问 $U$，但不能访问 $U^\dagger$ 或受控-$U$）。上界：将量子 junta 测试归约为随机子集分布的广义支撑集大小问题，并设计新的样本复杂度更优的测试器，使用积态输入与单量子比特测量的非自适应测试器。下界：反向将经典支撑集大小测试归约到量子 junta 测试，通过相位掩码难集合并证明前向量子查询在平均意义上可精确经典化。

Result: 对酉算子与信道 junta 测试均给出紧致界：在 $k$ 充分大、$n\ge 2k$ 及较宽 $\varepsilon$ 范围内查询复杂度为 $\Theta(k/(\varepsilon^2\log k))$。通道情形在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下达到最优 $\Theta(k/\log k)$；在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下还给出 $\Theta(k/\log k)$ 前向查询与 $\tilde O(\sqrt k)$ 带逆访问查询之间的分离。

Conclusion: 支撑集大小测试是量子 junta 测试的经典内核：两个方向的归约同时给出匹配的上界与下界。该结果闭合了 Bao 与 Yao 的间隙，并量化了前向查询模型相对带逆访问模型的优势。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of testing quantum juntas. Given an unknown $n$-qubit unitary or quantum channel, the goal is to decide whether it acts nontrivially on at most $k$ qubits or is $\varepsilon$-far from every such operation. For unitaries, we consider the forward-only model, where the tester has access to $U$, but not to $U^\dagger$ or controlled-$U$. We give tight bounds for both unitary and channel junta testing. For sufficiently large $k$, $n \ge 2k$, and a broad range of $\varepsilon$, both problems have query complexity $Θ\left(k/(\varepsilon^2\log k)\right)$. Our upper bounds are achieved by simple nonadaptive testers using product inputs and single-qubit measurements. For junta channels, this closes the gap by Bao and Yao (COLT 23, TPAMI 2025) between an $\widetilde{O}(k)$ upper bound and a $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{k})$ lower bound at constant $\varepsilon$, establishing the optimal $Θ(k/\log k)$ query complexity. At the heart of both bounds is a strong connection between quantum junta testing and classical support-size testing. For the upper bounds, we reduce quantum junta testing to a generalized support-size problem for distributions over random subsets, and develop a new tester with improved sample complexity. For the lower bounds, we proceed in the reverse direction, reducing classical support-size testing to quantum junta testing through a phase-masked hard ensemble and showing that forward quantum queries can be exactly classicalized on average. Together, these reductions identify support-size testing as the classical core of quantum junta testing and yield matching lower bounds. At constant $\varepsilon$, they also give a separation between $Θ(k/\log k)$ forward queries and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$ queries with inverse access.

</details>


### [147] [Rapid Mixing of Parallel Kac's Walk: From Spheres to Stiefel Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37999)
*Qian Chen,Minglong Qin,Fang Song,Penghui Yao,Mingnan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究并行Kac随机游走在同时作用于k个正交归一本征态时的混合性质，证明其在复杂度O((k+log d)log(d/ε))步后，k个输出态的联合分布与共同Haar随机酉作用结果在Wasserstein和全变差距离下ε-接近，从而将单态的散布性质推广至多态，并给出了复Stiefel流形V_{d,k}上的快速混合时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行Kac随机游走此前已知能在O(log d)轮内混合单个量子态，并可构造伪随机态扰乱器和伪随机酉。然而其作用于多个正交归一本征态时的行为尚不清楚。本文旨在将单态的散布（dispersing）性质推广到k个正交归一本征态，并刻画其在复Stiefel流形上的混合时间，扩展已有关于实Stiefel流形上标准Kac游走的Wasserstein混合结果。

Method: 研究并行Kac随机游走同时作用于k个正交归一本征态；通过离散化随机性并采用合适的伪随机原语，分析其联合分布。将k个有序正交归一态视为复Stiefel流形V_{d,k}上的点，分别用Wasserstein距离和全变差距离度量其与共同Haar随机酉作用结果的接近程度，并推导混合时间上界。

Result: 对任意1≤k<d，经过O((k+log d)log(d/ε))步后，k个输出态的联合分布在Wasserstein和全变差距离下均与共同Haar随机酉作用结果ε-接近；等价地，并行Kac游走在复Stiefel流形V_{d,k}上快速混合，两种距离下的混合时间均由O((k+log d)log(d/ε))界定。

Conclusion: 本文把并行Kac随机游走的散布性质从单量子态推广到多个正交归一本征态，证明了其在复Stiefel流形V_{d,k}上的快速混合，并扩展了Pillai、Smith和Vaikuntanathan关于实Stiefel流形上标准Kac游走的Wasserstein混合结果。

Abstract: Kac's walk is a classical local random walk whose action on a single real unit vector in dimension $d$ mixes in total variation in $Θ(d\log d)$ sequential steps~\cite{PS17}. Lu, Qin, Song, Yao, and Zhao introduced a parallel version of Kac's walk that mixes a single quantum state in $O(\log d)$ rounds~\cite{LQSY+26}. After discretizing the randomness and replacing it by suitable pseudorandom primitives, this parallel walk gives rise to pseudorandom state scramblers, and was subsequently shown to yield pseudorandom unitaries~\cite{LQSY+25}.
  We study what happens when the parallel Kac's walk acts simultaneously on $k$ orthonormal quantum states. We prove that, for any $1\leq k < d$, after $O\!\left((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon)\right)$ steps, the joint distribution of the $k$ output states is $\varepsilon$-close, in both Wasserstein and total variation distance, to that obtained by applying a common Haar-random unitary to the same inputs. This generalizes the dispersing property of the parallel Kac's walk from a single quantum state to multiple orthonormal quantum states. Equivalently, viewing an ordered collection of $k$ orthonormal states as a point on the complex Stiefel manifold $V_{d,k}=\{X\in\mathbb C^{d\times k}:X^\dagger X=I_k\}$, we show that the parallel Kac's walk mixes rapidly on $V_{d,k}$, with both Wasserstein and total variation mixing times bounded by $O\!\left((k+\log d)\log(d/\varepsilon)\right)$. This extends the Wasserstein mixing result of Pillai, Smith, and Vaikuntanathan for the standard Kac's walk on real Stiefel manifolds~\cite{PSV26}.

</details>


### [148] [Improved estimate of local Markovianity for quantum Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38007)
*Tai Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文改进了正温度下量子Gibbs态的局部Markov性质：对有限程、有界相互作用度，CMI随距离指数衰减且前因子至多随边界指数增长（改进了Chen-Rouzé的体积指数依赖）；对指数与拉伸指数衰减相互作用得到拉伸指数CMI衰减；对幂律相互作用得到逆对数或代数衰减，区域尺寸依赖为多项式。证明为静态方法，通过将Gibbs态与去掉边界相互作用所得的乘积态比较，把CMI界归结为可用局域性估计控制的相对熵损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Gibbs态的局部Markov性质（条件互信息CMI随距离衰减）是理解热态纠缠结构、关联衰减与局域性的核心问题。已有工作（Chen与Rouzé \cite{Chen2025}）建立了局部Markov界，但其前因子对边界区域呈指数体积依赖，在区域较大时界会变得松弛。本文旨在加强这一结果：在保持正温度的前提下，将前因子对区域的依赖从体积指数降低为边界指数，并推广到更一般的长程相互作用（指数、拉伸指数、幂律衰减）的CMI衰减形式。

Method: 证明为静态方法，区别于Chen与Rouzé的动力学方法。核心思路是将Gibbs态与通过“去掉边界相互作用”得到的乘积态进行比较，从而把CMI界化简为一个相对熵损失项，再利用局域性估计（locality estimates）来控制该项。针对不同相互作用衰减形式：有限程有界相互作用度给出指数CMI衰减与边界指数前因子；指数及拉伸指数衰减相互作用给出拉伸指数CMI衰减、前因子依赖边界；幂律相互作用给出逆对数或代数衰减，区域尺寸依赖为多项式。

Result: 对有限程、有界相互作用度的相互作用：CMI随分离距离指数衰减，前因子对边界尺寸呈指数增长，相比Chen与Rouzé \cite{Chen2025}的指数体积依赖有所改进。对指数衰减及拉伸指数衰减相互作用：得到拉伸指数CMI衰减，前因子依赖边界。对幂律相互作用：证明逆对数或代数衰减，且对区域尺寸的依赖为多项式。

Conclusion: 本文在正温度下强化了量子Gibbs态的局部Markov界，将区域依赖从体积指数降到边界指数，并系统推广到有限程、指数、拉伸指数及幂律相互作用，给出相应的CMI衰减形式。所采用的静态方法通过将Gibbs态与去边界相互作用后的乘积态对比，把问题转化为相对熵损失并借局域性估计加以控制，为后续热态局部Markov性与关联衰减研究提供了更紧的工具。

Abstract: We establish improved locally Markovian properties of quantum Gibbs states at every fixed positive temperature. For finite-range interactions of bounded interaction degree, the conditional mutual information (CMI) decays exponentially with separation, with a prefactor exponential in the boundary size. This improves the exponential volume dependence of the local Markov bounds established by Chen and Rouzé \cite{Chen2025}. For exponentially and stretched-exponentially decaying interactions, we obtain stretched-exponential CMI decay with a boundary-dependent prefactor. For power-law interactions, we prove inverse-logarithmic or algebraic decay with polynomial region-size dependence. Our proof is static, in contrast to the dynamical approach of Chen and Rouzé. We compare the Gibbs state with a product state obtained by removing boundary interactions, reducing the CMI bound to a relative-entropy loss that we control using locality estimates.

</details>


### [149] [Density Matrices of Pulsed Quantum Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38009)
*Joscelyn van der Veen,Daniel F. V. James*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用密度矩阵统一描述脉冲光，证明在有限探测时间下不同平稳性与相干性的关联函数给出等效测量结果，从而揭示量子化腔长度的物理含义和脉冲光物理谱的起源，并推广到脉冲高斯态以统一描述不同统计性质的脉冲光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲光的密度矩阵及关联函数在平稳性与相干性上性质各异，其物理意义的来源（如量子化腔长度、脉冲光物理谱）尚不清晰，且不同统计性质的脉冲光缺乏统一描述框架。

Method: 以密度矩阵表征脉冲光，考察其关联函数的平稳性与相干性；在引入有限探测时间（finite detection times）后比较不同密度矩阵的测量结果；并将密度矩阵推广至脉冲高斯态。

Result: 在计入有限探测时间后，不同平稳性与相干性的密度矩阵给出等效的测量结果；这一等效性赋予量子化腔长度以物理含义，并解释了脉冲光物理谱的起源；脉冲高斯态的推广使不同统计性质的脉冲光可在同一基下描述，并将光计数率与脉冲谱联系起来。

Conclusion: 有限探测时间下的等效性是理解脉冲光关联函数差异的关键，它为量化腔长度和脉冲光物理谱提供了物理诠释，并建立了统一的脉冲高斯态描述框架及其与光计数率的联系。

Abstract: Pulsed light is represented by density matrices whose correlation functions have different properties of stationarity and coherence. We show that these density matrices yield equivalent measurement results after accounting for finite detection times. This in turn reveals a physical meaning to the length of a quantization cavity and explains the origin of the physical spectrum for pulsed light. By generalizing the density matrices for pulsed Gaussian states of light, we can describe pulsed light of varying statistics in the same basis and relate the photocount rate to the pulse spectrum.

</details>


### [150] [Universal quantum coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38038)
*Jacopo Rizzo,Ludovico Lami,Jens Eisert,Lorenzo Leone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Schur-Weyl对偶建立了无需先验知识的普适量子纠错原理,将纠缠蒸馏与量子通信统一在表示论框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠蒸馏与噪声信道下的可靠量子通信是量子信息论的基础任务,但最优编码方案通常依赖对底层量子态或信道的先验知识,这在实际中往往无法获得,因此需要一种无需层析的普适编码机制。

Method: 通过Schur-Weyl对偶分析未知双边混合态多份拷贝的局部Schur-Weyl分解,证明其不可约置换空间构成最大纠缠量子码空间,纠错结构完全由表示论决定;纠正这些对称性分辨的误差,构造普适的纠缠蒸馏与量子通信协议。有限分组长度下的可达速率由Schur-Weyl信息谱刻画,并与Petz-Rényi条件熵建立联系。

Result: 所得协议达到相干信息速率,误差指数衰减并具最优二阶修正;在局部酉变换下不变,可精确恢复输入边缘分布。样本复杂度仅取决于相关谱秩而非希尔伯特空间维度;对全局秩为O(poly n)的n比特态,Petz-Rényi条件熵的假设即足以实现最优且样本高效的纠缠蒸馏。

Conclusion: Schur-Weyl对偶是保证最优、免层析普适量子编码的基本且普适的机制,将纠缠蒸馏、量子纠错与量子通信纳入统一的表示论框架。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation from noisy bipartite states and reliable quantum communication over noisy channels are fundamental tasks in quantum information theory, yet optimal coding schemes typically rely on prior knowledge of the underlying state or channel. Here, through Schur-Weyl duality, we establish a universal quantum error-correction principle that removes this dependence entirely. We show that the irreducible permutation spaces arising from the local Schur-Weyl decomposition of many identical copies of an unknown bipartite mixed state form maximally entangled quantum code spaces, with an error structure determined entirely by representation theory. Correcting these symmetry-resolved errors yields universal protocols for entanglement distillation and quantum communication that achieve coherent-information rates with exponentially vanishing error and optimal second-order correction. The protocols are invariant under local unitaries and allow both input marginals to be recovered exactly. At finite block length, their achievable rates are governed by an associated Schur-Weyl information spectrum, which we relate to Petz-Rényi conditional entropies of the underlying state. The resulting sample complexity depends only on the relevant spectral ranks, rather than on the respective Hilbert space dimensions. In particular, for $n$-qubit states of $O(\operatorname{poly} n)$ global rank, we show that a Petz-Rényi conditional-entropy promise suffices for optimal sample-efficient entanglement distillation. Altogether, these results identify Schur-Weyl duality as a fundamental and general mechanism for optimal, tomography-free universal quantum coding, placing entanglement distillation, quantum error correction and quantum communication within a unified representation-theoretic framework.

</details>


### [151] [A Tale of Two Walks: Kipnis, Marchioro and Presutti Meet Kac in a Quantum World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38044)
*Qian Chen,Jingcheng Liu,Minglong Qin,Leonard Schulman,Fang Song,Penghui Yao,Mingnan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了并行Kac游走与KMP过程之间的意外联系，证明了并行Kac游走在对称子空间上的twirling信道由分拆上的经典马尔可夫链精确编码，并可提升为完全图上的并行KMP过程；由此得到均匀于副本数t的Haar twirling近似界，并证明了一般图上KMP过程混合时间的Aldous猜想类比，改进了单纯形上coordinate hit-and-run的混合时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行Kac游走是量子信息中生成Haar随机性的重要工具，其twirling信道的混合分析此前缺乏均匀于副本数的界；同时，一般图上多粒子KMP过程的混合时间与单粒子混合时间的关系（Aldous猜想类比）尚不清楚，且coordinate hit-and-run的混合时间界存在改进空间。作者旨在建立并行Kac游走与KMP过程之间的新联系，以此统一并强化这些问题的分析。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 建立并行Kac游走在对称子空间上的twirling信道与分拆上经典马尔可夫链的精确对应，并将其提升为完全图上的并行KMP过程，从而将twirling信道分析归约为并行KMP过程的混合问题；(2) 提出并行与标准KMP过程的条件乘积结构：在合适的辅助随机性条件下，带标签粒子独立演化；(3) 结合该结构与精确耦合，得到均匀于粒子数的混合界。

Result: (1) 证明O(log d + log(1/ε))次重复即可在(ℂ^d)^{⊗t}的对称子空间上以误差ε逼近Haar twirling，且均匀于副本数t；(2) 对一般图上的标准KMP过程，证明固定精度下t粒子过程的混合时间至多为单粒子混合时间乘以顶点数对数的常数倍，均匀于t，即Aldous猜想的混合时间类比；(3) 应用该结果将n维标准单纯形上coordinate hit-and-run的全变差混合时间界从Õ(n^3)改进至Õ(n)，同时去除对初始分布的依赖。所得界在对数因子意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示并行Kac游走与KMP过程的深刻联系，并借助条件乘积结构与精确耦合技术，为两类KMP模型的非标签化版本提供了均匀于粒子数的混合界。这些界不仅在对数因子内是紧的，还蕴含并行Kac twirling信道在对称子空间上的快速收敛，并显著改进了coordinate hit-and-run的混合时间分析。

Abstract: We reveal an unexpected connection between the parallel Kac's walk and the Kipnis-Marchioro-Presutti (KMP) process. The twirling channel induced by the parallel Kac's walk on the symmetric subspace is exactly encoded by a classical Markov chain on partitions, which lifts to a parallel KMP process on complete graphs. This correspondence reduces the analysis of the twirling channel to the mixing of the parallel KMP process. We prove that $O(\log d+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ repetitions suffice to approximate Haar twirling on the symmetric subspace of $(\mathbb C^d)^{\otimes t}$ to error $\varepsilon$, uniformly in the number of copies $t$.
  For the standard KMP process on general graphs, we prove a mixing-time analogue of Aldous's conjecture: at fixed accuracy, the mixing time of the $t$-particle process is at most a constant times the single-particle mixing time multiplied by the logarithm of the number of vertices, uniformly in $t$. As an application, we improve the total variation mixing-time bound for coordinate hit-and-run on the $n$-dimensional standard simplex from $\widetilde O(n^3)$ (Kook and Vempala, 2026) to $\widetilde O(n)$, while removing the dependence on the initial distribution.
  Our main technical contribution is conditional product structure for both parallel and standard KMP processes. Conditioned on suitable auxiliary randomness, the labeled particles evolve independently. Combining this structure with an exact coupling yields mixing bounds uniform in the number of particles for both unlabeled KMP models. These bounds are sharp up to logarithmic factors and imply rapid convergence of the parallel Kac twirling channel on the symmetric subspace.

</details>


### [152] [Classical Verification of Quantum Computation with Quasilinear Resources, from Compiled Nonlocal Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38060)
*Finn Holler,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在LWE假设下，构建了首个总资源为准线性的BQP委托论证系统，通过单证明者计算自检验实现张量积态的可验证远程制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算自检验可让经典验证者控制单个有界证明者的量子寄存器。此前Coladangelo等在多方证明者设置下实现了相关自检验，但单证明者设置下尚无总资源准线性的BQP论证系统。

Method: 基于LWE假设，构造新的计算自检验，用于证明量子态并验证性地随机远程制备单量子比特Clifford可观测量σ_X、σ_Y、σ_Z及其线性组合的张量积态，具有常数鲁棒性；随后应用于去量子化Broadbent的高效验证协议。通过Kalai等的编译器将非局域博弈转化为单证明者论证系统，对Coladangelo等自检验的修改版进行编译。

Result: 对g个门的电路委托，总资源为O(poly(λ, log g)·g)，其中λ为LWE安全参数，验证误差与制备量子比特数无关。

Conclusion: 在单证明者设置下复现了Coladangelo等在多方证明者下的结果，实现了准线性总资源的BQP委托论证系统。

Abstract: Computational self-testing gives a classical verifier command over the quantum register of a single computationally bounded prover. We use this framework to construct the first argument system for BQP with quasilinear total resource requirements in the circuit model. Our argument system is based on the learning with errors (LWE) assumption and requires total resources of $O(\mathrm{poly}(λ, \log g)\cdot g)$ for delegating a circuit with $g$ gates, where $λ$ is the LWE security parameter. This is achieved by constructing a new computational self-test for certifying the prover's quantum state and using it to dequantize the efficient verification protocol of Broadbent (ToC 2018). Specifically, this self-test enables the verifiable, random remote state preparation of tensor product states of the single-qubit Clifford observables $σ_X, σ_Y, σ_Z, (σ_Y-σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$ and $(σ_Y+σ_X)/\sqrt{2}$, with constant robustness: the verification error is independent of the number of prepared qubits. This approach was first proposed by Coladangelo et al. (ToC 2024) in the multi-prover setting. We replicate their result in the single-prover setting by applying the compiler proposed by Kalai et al. (STOC 2023)---which turns any nonlocal game into a single-prover argument system---to a modified version of their self-test.

</details>


### [153] [Optimal Quantum-Classical Separations for Exact Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38073)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Amin Shiraz Gilani,Nikhil S. Mande*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念类的精确学习在有成员查询情形下确定性、随机化和量子查询复杂度之间的关系，反驳了长期存在的猜想 R(C)=O(Q(C)^2+Q(C)log N)，并构造概念类给出更强的三次方下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在成员查询的精确学习模型中，量子加速的两个经典来源是Grover搜索和Bernstein-Vazirani算法，由此长期猜想随机化查询复杂度与量子查询复杂度之间满足 R(C)=O(Q(C)^2+Q(C)log N)。作者希望厘清经典与量子查询复杂度之间的最优关系，并验证该猜想是否成立。

Method: 通过构造两类概念类 C 和 C'，分别给出随机化查询复杂度与确定性查询复杂度相对于量子查询复杂度的下界，并与已有上界（Arunachalam 等[Quantum'21]和Servedio-Gortler[SICOMP'04]）进行匹配分析。

Result: 构造的概念类满足 R(C)=Ω(Q(C)^3 log N / log Q(C)) 以及 D(C')=Ω(Q(C')^3 log N)。第一个界与Arunachalam等的上界在常数因子意义下匹配，第二个界与Servedio-Gortler的上界匹配，从而反驳了原猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果表明Arunachalam等随机化上界中的节省本质上依赖于随机性，同时首次证明学习中的量子加速可以超越Grover与Bernstein-Vazirani范式，刻画了经典与量子查询复杂度之间的最优关系。

Abstract: We study exact learning with membership queries for concept classes $\mathcal C\subseteq\{0,1\}^N$, focusing on the relationships among their deterministic, randomized, and quantum query complexities, denoted $\mathsf{D}(\mathcal C)$, $\mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)$, and $\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)$, respectively. The two canonical quantum speedups in this model are witnessed by Grover search and Bernstein-Vazirani, leading to the longstanding conjecture $$ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=O(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^2+\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)\log N). $$ We first refute this conjecture by constructing concept classes $\mathcal C$ and $\mathcal C'$ satisfying \[ \mathsf{R}(\mathcal C)=Ω\!\left(\frac{\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)^3\log N}{\log \mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C)}\right) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \mathsf{D}(\mathcal C')=Ω(\mathsf{Q}(\mathcal C')^3\log N). \] The first bound matches the upper bound of Arunachalam et al.~[Quantum'21] up to constant factors, while the second matches the upper bound of Servedio and Gortler~[SICOMP'04]. In particular, this shows that the saving in the randomized upper bound of Arunachalam et al. fundamentally relies on randomness. Apart from characterizing the optimal relationship between classical and quantum query complexity, our results are the first to show that quantum speedups for learning can go beyond the Grover and Bernstein-Vazirani paradigms.

</details>


### [154] [Ramanujan quantum expanders from the Weil representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38075)
*Siddhartha Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对每个奇素数幂 $q$ 和 $D=q+1$，构造了一族无限次的度数为 $D$ 的 Ramanujan 量子扩展图；通过将 Morgenstern 的 Ramanujan Cayley 图经 $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 的 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示转移实现。实现一个维度为 $N$ 的量子扩展图仅需 $O(\log^2 N)$ 基本门和 $O(\log N)$ 辅助 qudit，且在精确电路假设下严格达到 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 奇异值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子扩展图在量子纠错、去随机化和量子算法中具有重要应用，但显式构造度数最优（Ramanujan 界）且门复杂度低的量子扩展图仍是核心难题。此前 Iyer、Jain、Jordan 和 Somma (FOCS 2026) 的工作在奇异值界上存在加性误差，无法精确达到 Ramanujan 最优界，本文旨在消除这一误差并给出更优的显式构造。

Method: 对每个奇素数幂 $q$，取 $D=q+1$，利用 Morgenstern 在 $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 上构造的 Ramanujan Cayley 图（其中 $p$ 为 $q$ 的偶次幂），将其转移为 $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ 的 Weil 表示的奇不可约子表示，从而得到无限族度数为 $D$ 的量子扩展图。实现层面使用依赖 $q$ 的固定有限门集，对维度 $N$ 的扩展图使用 $O(\log^2 N)$ 基本门与 $O(\log N)$ 辅助 qudit。

Result: 构造出无限族度数为 $D=q+1$ 的 Ramanujan 量子扩展图；在量子电路被精确实现的假设下，严格满足 $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ 的奇异值界且无任何加性误差，相比之前工作 (FOCS 2026) 是一大改进；同时给出了 $O(\log^2 N)$ 基本门和 $O(\log N)$ 辅助 qudit 的高效实现。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个基于 Weil 表示与 Morgenstern Ramanujan Cayley 图的显式量子扩展图构造，实现了最优 Ramanujan 奇异值界且无加性误差，并保持了对数多项式级的门复杂度和对数级辅助 qudit 数，优于此前存在加性误差的量子扩展图工作。

Abstract: For every odd prime power $q$ and $D=q+1$, we construct an infinite family of Ramanujan quantum expanders of degree $D$. The construction transfers Morgenstern's Ramanujan Cayley graphs on $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$ for $p$ which is an even power of $q$, through the odd irreducible subrepresentation of the Weil representation of $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_{p})$. For a quantum expander of dimension $N$, our implementation uses $O(\log^2 N)$ elementary gates, and $O(\log N)$ ancilla qudits, using a fixed finite gate set depending on $q$. An advantage compared to the previous work of Iyer, Jain, Jordan, and Somma (FOCS 2026) is that assuming the quantum circuit is implemented exactly, we satisfy the $2\sqrt{D-1}/D$ singular value bound exactly without any additive error.

</details>


### [155] [A commuting operator self-test for exact entanglement embezzlement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38083)
*Connor Paddock,Simon Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类基于纠缠挪用（embezzlement）的广义非局域博弈的最优可交换算子关联是一个可交换算子自检验，并给出了可交换算子框架下首个精确纠缠挪用协议的关联自检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自检验结果多集中于量子张量积模型，而对于可交换算子框架下的关联自检验，尤其是涉及精确纠缠挪用协议的情形，尚缺乏实例。作者希望构造一个例子，说明存在最优关联无法用张量积模型实现，从而刻画可交换算子框架与张量积框架的本质差异。

Method: 作者研究Coladangelo双人广义非局域博弈的一个变体，该博弈基于纠缠的挪用。主要证明技术依赖于Harris关于精确纠缠挪用的唯一抽象态刻画，其中涉及Cuntz代数。

Result: 该博弈的最优可交换算子关联是一个可交换算子自检验；由于唯一最优关联要求精确的纠缠挪用，该关联在量子张量积模型下不可实现，即使允许局域空间为无限维亦然。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可交换算子自检验的实例，其中两方共享的Hilbert空间无法分解为与局域测量算子相容的局域空间的张量积，揭示了可交换算子框架相对于张量积模型的独特性质。

Abstract: We show that the optimal commuting operator correlation for a variant of Coladangelo's two-player generalized nonlocal game, based on the embezzlement of entanglement, is a commuting operator self-test. Our result gives the first correlation self-test for an exact entanglement embezzlement protocol in the commuting operator framework. Because the unique optimal correlation necessitates the exact embezzlement of entanglement, the correlation is unrealizable using quantum tensor-product models, even if the local spaces are allowed to be infinite dimensional. As such, our result provides an example of a commuting operator self-test where the Hilbert space shared by the two parties cannot be factored into a tensor-product of local spaces that is compatible with the local measurement operators. Our main proof technique relies on Harris' unique abstract state characterization of exact embezzlement of entanglement involving Cuntz algebras.

</details>


### [156] [Learning quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38085)
*Isaac Holt,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典HSP推广到量子对象的对称性学习：对非阿贝尔StateHSP在隐藏子群正规及广泛非阿贝尔群类下给出高效量子算法；提出物理上更自然的Anyonic态对称性学习问题，通过投影表示线性化与线性纠错码的联系归约到StateHSP；并给出适用于任意局部维度混合qudit的稳定子群学习改进算法，同时将酉算子、哈密顿量、有限态集合等量子对象的对称性学习归约到StateHSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典HSP与Shor算法在寻找经典对象对称性上极为成功，但量子对象（量子态、酉算子、哈密顿量等）的对称性学习尚缺乏一般理论。已有StateHSP仅覆盖阿贝尔情形，且其Bose对称性把相差全局相位的物理等价纯态视为不同，与物理直觉不符。因此需要将StateHSP推广到非阿贝尔群，并建立更符合物理的对称性学习框架。

Method: 1) 研究StateHSP：对隐藏子群为正规子群、且环境群属于广泛非阿贝尔群类的情形，设计高效量子算法，将一般理论从阿贝尔推广到非阿贝尔；2) 定义Anyonic态对称性学习问题：以投影表示刻画仅相差全局相位的纯态等价对称性；3) 通过建立投影表示线性化与线性纠错码之间的新联系，把Anyonic问题归约到StateHSP并给出高效量子算法；4) 将该归约应用于任意局部维度混合qudit的稳定子群学习；5) 对酉算子、哈密顿量及有限态集合定义对称性学习问题，并归约到StateHSP实例。

Result: 获得非阿贝尔StateHSP的高效量子算法（隐藏子群正规，环境群为广泛非阿贝尔群类）；提出并高效求解Anyonic态对称性学习问题，关键工具是投影表示线性化与线性纠错码的新联系；得到适用于任意局部维度混合qudit的稳定子群学习改进算法；对酉算子、哈密顿量和有限态集合等量子对象给出可归约到StateHSP的对称性学习问题及高效算法。

Conclusion: 本文将隐藏子群问题的量子算法理论从经典对象与阿贝尔设定推广到量子对象与非阿贝尔设定，建立了以StateHSP为核心的统一归约框架，并借助投影表示与纠错码的联系处理了物理上更自然的Anyonic对称性，为量子对象对称性学习提供了通用方法论。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are powerful tools for finding symmetries of classical objects, most famously through Shor's algorithm and the framework of the Hidden Subgroup Problem (HSP). In this work, we study quantum algorithms for learning symmetries of quantum objects. Our starting point is the recently introduced State Hidden Subgroup Problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalisation of HSP in which the task is to learn the symmetry subgroup of a quantum state. We obtain positive results for non-abelian StateHSP, giving efficient quantum algorithms whenever the hidden subgroup is normal and for ambient groups belonging to a broad class of non-abelian groups, extending the previous general theory beyond the abelian setting.
  StateHSP learns \emph{Bose} symmetries, associated with linear representations, whereas physically equivalent pure states are defined only up to global phase. Motivated by this, we introduce a new \emph{Anyonic} state symmetry learning problem based on a more physically natural notion of symmetry than that considered by StateHSP. We give an efficient quantum algorithm by reducing the problem to StateHSP, where the reduction relies on a new connection between linearisations of projective representations and linear error-correcting codes. As an application, we obtain an improved algorithm for learning stabiliser groups that applies to mixed qudit states of arbitrary local dimension. Finally, we introduce symmetry learning problems for other quantum objects, including unitaries, Hamiltonians, and finite collections of states, and obtain efficient quantum algorithms by reducing them to instances of StateHSP.

</details>


### [157] [Optimal Ground-State Preparation with a Guiding State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38091)
*Stacey Jeffery,Rolando D. Somma,Freek Witteveen,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文提出利用导引态与相位估计构造基态制备算法，仅需 \(O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)\) 次 \(U=e^{iH}\) 与 \(A\) 及其逆的调用，即可获得基态的 \(\varepsilon\)-近似，并给出两种算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算中，制备哈密顿量的基态是许多算法的核心前置任务。已有工作（作者此前的基态能量估计论文）解决了能量估计问题，但缺少与之配套的最优基态制备方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出达到最优复杂度的基态制备算法，完成能量估计与态制备的统一处理。

Method: 问题设定：\(H\) 有唯一基态 \(|\psi_0\rangle\)、能量 \(E_0\)，已知估计 \(\tilde{E}_0\) 满足 \(|\tilde{E}_0-E_0|\le\delta\)，且 \(E_0\) 与其余本征值间距至少 \(3\delta\)；可用的酉算子 \(A\) 能产生导引态 \(A|0\rangle\)，与 \(|\psi_0\rangle\) 的重叠至少为 \(\gamma\)。算法使用 \(U=e^{iH}\) 实现相位/能量分辨滤波，并反复调用 \(A\) 注入基态分量。论文给出两种实现：(1) 采用 [HMdW03] 风格的幅度放大与误差缩减交替进行（interleaving amplitude amplification and error-reduction）；(2) 基于 transducer 复合（composition of transducers）。

Result: 两种算法均能在 \(O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)\) 次对 \(U=e^{iH}\)、\(A\) 及其逆的调用内输出 \(|\psi_0\rangle\) 的 \(\varepsilon\)-近似。结合作者此前的基态能量估计结果，联合给出基态制备的 \(O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)\) 最优上界，匹配相应下界。

Conclusion: 本文完成了基态能量估计到基态制备的延伸，证明基态制备具有最优复杂度 \(O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/\gamma\delta)\)，并提供两条独立算法路径（幅度放大路线与 transducer 复合路线），验证了该上界的可实现性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Suppose a Hamiltonian $H$ has a unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$ with an eigenvalue $E_0$, and we have an estimate $\tilde{E}_0$ such that $|\tilde{E}_0-E_0|\leqδ$, and there is a gap of at least $3δ$ between $E_0$ and all other eigenvalues. Suppose we have a unitary $A$ available that can produce a "guiding state'" $A|0\rangle$ that has overlap at least $γ$ with $|ψ_0\rangle$. We show how to obtain an $\varepsilon$-approximation of $|ψ_0\rangle$ using $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ applications of $U=e^{iH}$ and $A$, and their inverses. We give two different algorithms, one based on interleaving amplitude amplification and error-reduction in the style of [HMdW03], and one using the composition of transducers. This paper is the state-preparation follow-up to our two recent ground-state-energy estimation papers [JW26, SdW26]. Combined, our results show an optimal $O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/γδ)$ upper bound for ground state preparation.

</details>


### [158] [Resource Compatibility in Optimal Quantum Symmetry Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38134)
*Yimeng Cao,Ranyiliu Chen,Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对称性检验能否在不使用与对称性自然关联的量子资源时达到最优，针对并行子群对Haar检验，建立了定量表示论逆定理，并刻画了若干群（对角环面、正交子群、qutrit Clifford群）的最优协议及其资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究：与某对称性天然关联的量子资源（如相干性、Wigner正性等）是否为最优对称性检验所必需，即是否存在“无资源”的最优协议，并刻画其所需的最小资源量。

Method: 方法基于表示论，针对零第一类错误的任意协议导出逆定理：第二类误差超出部分由两项非负惩罚下界给出——一项来自次优子群类型的权重，一项来自各类型内Schur轮廓相对类型极值轮廓的偏离。最优时两项惩罚消失，得到最优系统边际的支撑锁定与权重锁定。分析对角环面（d≥2）、正交子群（d≥2）及qutrit Clifford群的具体情形。

Result: 对角环面：无相干性最优检验仅当单次查询可行；给出所有非相干系统边际协议的确切最优第二类误差，以及达到无限制最优所需的最小系统边际相干性。正交子群：实协议在任意查询次数下均达到最优。qutrit Clifford群：3、4次查询存在Wigner正最优协议，5至9次不存在，9次以上待定。

Conclusion: 最优对称性检验的资源需求（子群与查询次数相关）与最优系统边际的结构约束密切相关；锁定集与自由边际集的不相交排除了无资源最优性，而仅重叠不足以证明可达性。

Abstract: Can a symmetry be tested optimally without using the quantum resource naturally associated with it? For parallel subgroup-versus-Haar testing, we derive a quantitative representation-theoretic converse for every protocol with zero type-I error: the excess type-II error is bounded below by the sum of two nonnegative penalties, one for weight on suboptimal subgroup types and one for deviations of within-type Schur profiles from their typewise extremal profiles. At optimality, both penalties vanish, yielding support and weight locking for every optimal system marginal. Disjointness of the resulting locked set and the free marginal set rules out resource-free optimality, whereas overlap alone does not establish attainability. For the diagonal torus in dimension $d\geq 2$, optimal testing without coherence is possible only with a single query. We determine both the exact optimal type-II error over all protocols with incoherent system marginals and the minimum system-marginal coherence required to attain the unrestricted optimum. For the orthogonal subgroup in dimension $d\geq 2$, a real protocol attains the optimum at every query number. For the qutrit Clifford group, Wigner-positive optimal protocols exist at three and four queries but not from five through nine; whether they exist beyond nine queries remains open. Together, these results relate the subgroup- and query-dependent resource requirements of optimal symmetry testing to structural constraints on optimal system marginals.

</details>


### [159] [Local autonomous inference machines for quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38139)
*Siddhant Midha,Dmitry A. Abanin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于置信传播（BP）的局域分布式量子LDPC码译码框架，利用严格的局域处理、局域反馈与自主并行操作，在保留阈值的迭代BP码及朴素BP失效的码族上均实现阈值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的传统BP译码存在两个问题：一是全局集中式操作不利于物理实现与并行化；二是朴素BP在若干重要码型（如toric码的点状扇区）缺乏阈值行为。因此需要一种兼具局域性、自主性与图通用性的译码架构。

Method: 构建在BP之上的局域分布式译码框架，包含症状信息的严格局域处理、局域反馈与异步自主动力学。分两个层次：(1) 对迭代BP下存在阈值的码，提升为自主局域测量-反馈动力学并保持阈值；(2) 对朴素BP无阈值的码，利用BP提供的局域边际信念，使活跃症状缺陷通过异步反馈进行移动、配对与湮灭。

Result: 数值验证该构造在二维与三维toric码的点状扇区展现阈值行为，而该处常规BP译码失败；并借助BP的图通用性，进一步研究三维toric码膜状扇区及一族bivariate-bicycle量子LDPC码上的推断动力学。

Conclusion: BP提供的局域边际信息足以驱动症状缺陷的自主移动、配对与湮灭，从而在朴素BP失效的码型上恢复阈值行为，表明该局域分布式框架具有超越常规BP的适用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce a local and distributed framework for decoding quantum LDPC codes. Built atop belief propagation (BP), the architecture combines strictly local processing of syndrome information, local feedback, autonomous operation, and highly parallelized decoding. We establish the framework at two complementary levels. First, we show that any code exhibiting a threshold under iterative BP decoding can be promoted to an autonomous local measurement-and-feedback dynamics while preserving that threshold. We then turn to codes for which naive BP does not itself exhibit threshold behavior. Our key observation is that BP can nevertheless provide sufficiently accurate \emph{local} marginal information: active syndrome defects use these local beliefs to \emph{move}, pair, and annihilate through asynchronous feedback. We demonstrate numerically that this construction exhibits threshold behavior in the point-like sectors of the two- and three-dimensional toric codes, where conventional BP decoding fails. Exploiting the graph-generality of BP, we then study the inference dynamics on the membrane-like sector of the three-dimensional toric code and in a family of bivariate-bicycle quantum LDPC codes.

</details>


### [160] [The exact strong converse exponent for private communication over quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38144)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Christoph Hirche,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文确定了有限维量子窃听信道下密钥传输与生成的精确强逆指数，引入正则化Renyi私密信息刻画该指数，并证明其连续收敛于私密容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子窃听信道的私密容量刻画了密钥可靠且保密传输的渐近最优速率，但对于高于私密容量的速率，密钥保真度的指数衰减规律（强逆行为）此前缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 以对理想密钥的平方保真度联合度量可靠性与保密性；定义一种新的Renyi私密信息，并通过对其次数参数的正则化来构造强逆指数；利用其以普通私密信息表示的精确积分表达，推导一致连续性界。

Result: 给出密钥传输与生成强逆指数的精确表达式；证明正则化Renyi私密信息随Renyi阶数趋于1时收敛到私密容量，并获得一致连续性界。

Conclusion: 对任意量子窃听信道，在一切高于私密容量的速率上，密钥保真度均呈指数衰减。

Abstract: We determine the exact strong converse exponent for secret-key transmission and generation over general finite-dimensional quantum wiretap channels, measuring reliability and secrecy jointly by squared fidelity to an ideal secret key. We introduce a novel Renyi private information whose regularization characterizes this exponent. An exact integral representation in terms of ordinary private information gives a uniform continuity bound, showing that the regularized quantity converges to private capacity as the Renyi order approaches one. This establishes for any wiretap channel an exponential fidelity decay at every rate above private capacity.

</details>


### [161] [Query-optimal unitary channel tomography in diamond distance with parallel access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38145)
*Entong He,Zihao Li,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种并行查询协议，仅用 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ 次并行查询即可在钻石距离意义下学习 $d$ 维酉信道，达到下界并弥合了并行与顺序策略之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子过程层析中，如何在严格并行查询下以与顺序查询同等效率学习酉信道一直是未解决问题。顺序查询可借助自适应性优化估计与学习策略，而并行查询无法调整；从高阶量子操作角度看，并行策略可被顺序策略模拟，反之通常不成立，因此直觉上顺序策略更强。本文旨在检验并突破这一直觉。

Method: 作者设计了一种新的学习协议：对未知 $d$ 维酉信道进行并行查询，在钻石距离意义下将其学习到误差 $\varepsilon$ 以内。该协议在并行查询框架下构造，查询复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$，与已知下界匹配。

Result: 协议达到 $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ 的并行查询复杂度，匹配 [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23] 给出的下界，从而在酉信道层析中闭合了并行与顺序策略之间的差距。作为应用，该协议在边界区域的量子信道层析中达到最优查询复杂度，并提升了已知最优的费米子线性光学层析协议的查询效率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，尽管并行查询缺乏自适应性，但在酉信道层析中其查询效率可与顺序查询相同，推翻了顺序策略必然更强的直觉，并对相关量子信道与费米子线性光学层析任务带来查询复杂度和效率上的改进。

Abstract: In the study of quantum process tomography, it has remained open how to learn a unitary channel in diamond distance with strictly parallel queries as efficiently as with sequential queries. Sequential queries allow one to exploit adaptivity to refine the estimate and adjust the learning strategy accordingly, whereas such adjustments are impossible for parallel queries. From the perspective of higher-order quantum operations, any parallel learning strategy can be simulated by a sequential one, whereas the converse does not hold in general, suggesting that sequential learning strategies are potentially more powerful. Contrary to this intuition, we present a new protocol for learning an unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channel to within $\varepsilon$ in diamond distance using $\mathcal{O}(d^2/\varepsilon)$ parallel queries, matching the lower bound presented in [Haah, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Tang, FOCS '23]. Our protocol thus closes the gap between parallel and sequential strategies for unitary channel tomography. As applications, it achieves optimal query complexity for boundary-regime quantum channel tomography and improves the query efficiency of the best-known protocol for tomography of fermionic linear optics.

</details>


### [162] [Optimal Tolerant Testing of Lindbladian Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38160)
*Jinge Bao,Yiyi Cai,Zhong-Xia Shang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在仅有前向演化访问下，对未知、时不变 Lindblad 生成元（跳跃算符局域但重叠不受限）进行耗散强度阈值判别，给出最优演化时间算法与匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制量子噪声与刻画开放系统动力学需要量化耗散。实验中即使尽力抑制仍存在残余噪声，因此判断耗散强度是否落在给定容差阈值内十分重要。既有耗散测试受限于阈值设定与有界度要求，适用范围较窄。

Method: 针对强度有界的未知时不变 Lindblad 生成元，跳跃算符局域但允许任意重叠，仅使用无记忆前向演化。任务是区分规范化 Frobenius 范数下耗散强度至多 ε1 与至少 ε2（0 ≤ ε1 < ε2）。提出算法在总演化时间 O(ε2/(ε2-ε1)^2) 内以常数成功概率完成判别，并给出匹配下界 Ω(ε2/(ε2-ε1)^2)。

Result: 算法对所有非平凡阈值均达到 O(ε2/(ε2-ε1)^2) 的总演化时间且成功概率为常数；下界与之匹配，表明即使允许自适应协议也达到最优。该结果将耗散测试推广到容差设定，并去除了有界度要求。

Conclusion: 本文实现了耗散强度阈值判别的最优演化复杂度，放宽了局域跳跃算符重叠与有界度限制，使框架适用于更广泛的实验相关场景。

Abstract: Quantifying dissipation is essential for controlling quantum noise and characterizing open-system dynamics. In practical experimental settings, residual noise may still persist despite efforts to suppress it, making it important to determine whether the dissipative strength remains within a prescribed tolerance threshold. We study this question for an unknown, time-independent Lindblad generator with bounded strength, where the jump operators are local but with unrestricted overlap, using only memoryless forward-evolution access. The task is to distinguish dissipative strength of at most $\varepsilon_1$ from strength of at least $\varepsilon_2$, for $0 \leq \varepsilon_1 < \varepsilon_2$, measured in the canonical dissipator's normalized Frobenius norm. We give an algorithm that solves this task in total evolution time $O(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ for all nontrivial thresholds, with constant success probability, and provide a matching lower bound $Ω(\varepsilon_2/(\varepsilon_2-\varepsilon_1)^2)$ that establishes optimality even for adaptive protocols. This extends dissipation testing to the tolerant setting and eliminates the bounded-degree requirement, making the framework applicable to a broader range of experimentally relevant settings.

</details>


### [163] [When Classical Correlations Certify Entanglement Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38168)
*Takeru Utsumi,Yota Tachibana,Yoshifumi Nakata,Takaya Matsuura,Ryuji Takagi,Francesco Buscemi,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明纠缠与可区分性之间的联系可推广到无需互补性假设的一般测量，其关键条件是测量诱导的不可约性，并据此建立了纠缠转化误差与态分辨失败概率之间的定量关系，应用于纠缠蒸馏与量子纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与态可区分性虽各自是量子基础中的丰富主题，但二者通常通过互补基测量相关联；这种联系是否能在不假设互补性的情况下推广到一般测量，此前基本未解决。

Method: 对满足不可约性条件（比互补性更宽松）的POVM，建立单侧局域变换为最大纠缠态的infidelity与态分辨失败概率之间的定量关系，并刻画两种误差同时消失的条件；进一步推导连接信息增益-不可逆性与可观测噪声-扰动两类测量权衡的新关系。

Result: 证明了纠缠与可区分性的联系在不可约性条件下成立，给出了定量误差关系与误差同时消失的判据，并将结果推广到量子测量理论，导出新的测量权衡关系。

Conclusion: 基于不可约性的纠缠-可区分性联系为纠缠蒸馏、量子纠错与测量权衡提供了统一框架；其误差可通过实验可及的局域测量估计经典输入-输出关联来认证，无需互补性。

Abstract: Entanglement and state distinguishability have long been central topics in quantum foundations. While each has developed into a rich subject in its own right, they can be connected through measurements in complementary bases. This connection provides insights into quantum and classical correlations and underlies many information-processing tasks, most notably quantum error correction (QEC). However, it has remained largely open whether the connection between entanglement and distinguishability extends to general measurements without assuming complementarity. We answer this question in the affirmative: the key is irreducibility induced by measurements, a much more relaxed condition than complementarity. Specifically, for POVMs satisfying an irreducibility condition, we establish a quantitative relation between the infidelity of one-sided local transformation into a maximally entangled state and the failure probability in state discrimination. We then characterize when both errors can vanish simultaneously. Our results have direct applications to entanglement distillation and QEC. Their errors can be certified by estimating classical input-output correlations from experimentally accessible local measurements, without requiring complementarity. The results also extend to quantum measurement theory. We derive novel trade-off relations that bridge two historically distinct approaches: information gain--irreversibility and observable noise--disturbance. Our connection between entanglement and distinguishability via irreducibility thus offers a common framework for entanglement distillation, QEC, and measurement trade-offs.

</details>
